### Per creature kill count

- **Scope:** QoL · **Feasibility:** trivial · **Who needs it:** Client · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** [Creature Kill and Tame Counts 0.1.0](../src/Exploration/Stats.PerCreature) (in development)
- **Approach:** Read m_playerStats[0].m_enemyStats[0][Character.m_name] (and modifier buckets) and show it on the enemy nameplate, in the trophy panel (map trophy prefab -> Character.m_name by scanning ZNetScene prefabs with CharacterDrop) and in the stats/compendium page. Data already exists and is retroactive.
- **Hooks:** `PlayerProfile.m_playerStats`, `PlayerProfile.IncrementStatEnemy`, `Game.RPC_RegisterKill`, `EnemyHud.UpdateHuds`, `EnemyHud.ShowHud`, `InventoryGui.UpdateTrophyList`, `TextsDialog.AddStats`
- **Risks:** Counts are per character across worlds; key is the localization token shared by variants; PvP victims mixed in (filter non-'$' keys); read index 0 not achievement buckets; minor conflicts with nameplate UI mods.
- **Related:** Compendium
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/exploration-player.md](game/exploration-player.md)
- **Existing mods:** partial

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [TrophyHuntMod (oathorse)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/oathorse/TrophyHuntMod/) | 0.12.1, 2026-09-28, updated post-1.0; MIT-0 | Trophy tray HUD; tooltips show player kill counts, trophy pickups and drop rates. Built for speedrun modes. |
  | [Almanac (RustyMods)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/RustyMods/Almanac/) | 3.8.0, 2026-09-25, 1.0 tagged | Creature, item and piece index on the trophy panel; player metrics including kills; YAML achievements with kill thresholds; leaderboard. Large all-in-one. |
  | [SlayerSkills (Neobotics)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Neobotics/SlayerSkills/) | 1.2.0, 2026-09-11, 1.0 tagged | Per-creature slayer points from trophy drops give bonus damage against that creature. |
  | [KillCountEvolution (basicMods)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/basicMods/KillCountEvolution/) | 1.1.0, 2026-05-13, pre-1.0, AI-generated | World-wide kill total drives elite spawns and loot. No per-creature UI. |

- **Inspiration:** Key finding: vanilla already stores per-creature kills in PlayerProfile.m_playerStats[i].m_enemyStats[KillModifiers] (keyed by creature name, split total/unarmed/magic/ranged/melee, incremented by PlayerProfile.IncrementStatEnemy, saved in the .fch profile). Achievements use it, but the Compendium stats page (TextsDialog.AddStats) only prints category headers. Build a tiny mod that reads these vanilla stats (no own tracking, no save risk) and shows them in trophy tooltips, the creature hover/nameplate and the Compendium. Kill milestones can then unlock bestiary knowledge tiers (Compendium idea).

### Sleep through the day

- **Scope:** QoL · **Feasibility:** easy · **Who needs it:** Both · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** [Sleep Through the Day 0.1.0](../src/Exploration/Sleep.ThroughDay) (in development)
- **Approach:** Client: postfix EnvMan.CalculateCanSleep to allow mornings (keep cooldown) and write a sleep-mode ZDO key on the player before AttachStart. Server: Game.UpdateSleeping prefix that, when everyone is in bed in the morning, sets EnvMan private m_skipTime/m_skipToTime (day*m_dayLengthSec + configurable raw fraction ~0.80)/m_timeSkipSpeed (delta/12), sets Game.m_sleeping and sends SleepStart; vanilla stop branch finishes; patch wake message.
- **Hooks:** `EnvMan.CalculateCanSleep`, `Bed.Interact`, `Game.UpdateSleeping`, `Game.EverybodyIsTryingToSleep`, `EnvMan.SkipToMorning`, `Player.SetSleeping`
- **Risks:** Extra time skips accelerate crops/smelters/fermenters (balance); Rested granted each wake; must use live m_dayLengthSec (day-length mods like ValheimPlus); unmodded clients cannot lie down in the morning so no forced skips.
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/exploration-player.md](game/exploration-player.md)
- **Existing mods:** partial

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [BedRules (TastyChickenLegs)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/TastyChickenLegs/BedRules/) | 2.0.6, 2026-09-11, 1.0 tagged | Sleep anytime; ignore fire, enemies and wet; no spawn or claim needed. Time target not documented; presumably still skips to the next morning. |
  | [Sleepover (Azumatt)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Azumatt/Sleepover/) | 1.1.4, 2024-11-07, deprecated | 'Sleep at any time of day (may be buggy)', multiple sleepers per bed, every check toggleable. Needs the mod on all clients and the server. |
  | [Sleep Options (Nexus 2716)](https://www.nexusmods.com/valheim/mods/2716) | Nexus-only, older | Toggles the night, enemy, exposure, fire and wet checks. |
  | [SleepSkip (Azumatt) / SkipSleep / NowYouSleep](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Azumatt/SleepSkip/) | various | Partial-player or vote-based night skipping. None handles day sleep. |

- **Inspiration:** Vanilla Game.UpdateSleeping only allows sleep when EnvMan.IsAfternoon or IsNight, and EnvMan.SkipToMorning always targets the next morning (12 s skip in EnvMan.UpdateTimeSkip). No mod offers 'rest until dusk'. Add a second bed option, 'Rest until evening', that reuses the vanilla skip but targets the same day's evening. Keep the everyone-agrees rule (optional vote ratio), Rested, and the enemy/wet safety checks. Server-side patch plus an RPC; clients only need the mod for hover text. Avoid Sleepover's check-bypassing approach.

### Wish bone deep north fix

> Wish bone should detect treasures in meadow-like poi in deepnorth

- **Scope:** QoL · **Feasibility:** easy · **Who needs it:** Client · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** idea
- **Approach:** Vanilla cause, checked in the 1.0.16 asset bundles: the Wishbone (SE_Finder asset 'Wishbone') pings only loaded objects that carry a Beacon. The Deep North Viking Graveyard (the Deep North version of the Meadows stone ship ShipSetting01) buries gem pickables Pickable_MorkHallaTreasure: ShipSetting02 is one stone ship (90%) with five gems (50% each) and a chest (50%), ShipSetting03 is three stone ships (50% each) with one gem and one chest each. The Deep North shipwrecks (ShipWreck01_DN/02_DN) bury a shipwreck_vikingship_chest (75%). The location prefabs give the gems and the wreck chest a 20 m Beacon only on the nested copies; the standalone ZNetScene prefabs have none, and after generation every load re-creates the object from the standalone prefab (ZNetScene.CreateObject), so the Beacon is practically never there. Fix: a PickableItem.Awake postfix adds a Beacon (m_range 20) to Pickable_MorkHallaTreasure when it has none (children included, like Smart Wishbone's guard) and is not in an interior (Morkhalla uses the same prefab in its rooms, 5000 m up), and a Container.Awake postfix does the same for shipwreck_vikingship_chest; the 'has no Beacon' guard makes the mod a no-op after a game fix or on the rare copy made at generation. Server-synced settings: graveyard gems (on), Deep North wreck chests (on), older wreck chests shipwreck_karve_chest (same gap, added beyond the request), ignore emptied chests (these chests have m_autoDestroyEmpty off, so a looted chest keeps pinging and, being the closest Beacon, can hide a gem beside it). Implement that option by removing the Beacon component of an emptied chest in a Container.CheckForChanges postfix (Beacon.OnDestroy unregisters it; put it back if items return), not by filtering Beacon.FindClosestBeaconInRange: Exploration transpiles that method to scale ranges and Smart Wishbone never calls it, while a removed component works with every finder. Live toggle: add beacons to already loaded objects on activation; on deactivation destroy the ones the mod added and restore the ones it removed (SE_Finder null-checks its cached beacon). Technically client-only (Beacon and SE_Finder are local, no ZDO data); ships as Both under the house rule for gameplay mods, because it changes what a player's Wishbone finds: the server refuses players without the mod and sends its settings to everyone (open choice: as a pure vanilla bug fix it could ship client-only). Test trap: the 'location' console command spawns in Full mode (and turns world saving off until restart unless SAVE is added), so its fresh copy pings even in vanilla; test after leaving the zone and returning, after a relog, or at a natural site (findtp ShipSetting02); 'spawn Pickable_MorkHallaTreasure' gives a quick vanilla-negative check.
- **Hooks:** `PickableItem.Awake (postfix: add a 20 m Beacon to Pickable_MorkHallaTreasure outside interiors)`, `Container.Awake (postfix: add a 20 m Beacon to shipwreck_vikingship_chest, optionally shipwreck_karve_chest; remember which containers carry a Beacon)`, `Container.CheckForChanges (optional postfix, runs once per second per loaded container: remove the Beacon of an emptied chest, put it back if items return)`, `Container.GetInventory + Inventory.NrOfItems (emptied-chest check)`, `Character.InInterior(Vector3)`, `ZNetView.GetZDO + ZDO.GetPrefab (match precomputed prefab hashes)`, `Beacon.m_range / Beacon.m_instances`, `ZNetScene.m_instances (live toggle: loaded objects)`, `SE_Finder.UpdateStatusEffect and Beacon.FindClosestBeaconInRange (read only: re-pick the closest Beacon once per second; Exploration (Smoothbrain) transpiles both)`, `ZoneSystem.SpawnLocation / ZoneSystem.SpawnProxyLocation / ZNetScene.CreateObject (why the location-level Beacon is lost; read only)`, `Humanoid.UpdateEquipmentStatusEffects (Wishbone equip effect; read only)`
- **Risks:** Targets and ranges are game data (1.0.16 bundles), not code: an update may fix the prefabs (the no-Beacon guard makes the mod a no-op) or rename them (log once when a target prefab is missing from ZNetScene). The interior filter assumes every outdoor Pickable_MorkHallaTreasure is a graveyard gem (true for the vanilla prefabs: outdoors it only appears in ShipSetting02/03, otherwise in the Morkhalla rooms; location packs may place it elsewhere). Smart Wishbone replaces the Wishbone's SE_Finder with a finder that only tracks prefab names from its own YAML, so it ignores these beacons unless they are listed. Exploration (Smoothbrain) transpiles SE_Finder.UpdateStatusEffect and Beacon.FindClosestBeaconInRange to scale every Beacon range with its skill: that also scales the restored beacons, and it is why the emptied-chest option must not re-implement that search. Whether HUD mods such as Wishbone Direction read beacons is not documented (unverified). The emptied-chest check runs once per second on every loaded container, base chests included: track only containers that carry a Beacon (found once in the Container.Awake postfix) so the check stays one lookup. Balance: restoring the designers' own Beacon makes the Deep North wreck chests and the graveyard gems easier to find; that is the point of the fix, but players get more loot per trip than today. No ZDO data, so removing the mod leaves nothing behind.
- **Related:** Deep North revamp; Gemstone revamp
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/exploration-world.md](game/exploration-world.md)
- **Existing mods:** none

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [Smart Wishbone (Goldenrevolver)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Goldenrevolver/Smart_Wishbone/) | v1.0.2, updated 2023-10-17, 1.0: unknown | Replaces the Wishbone's SE_Finder with its own finder (SE_CustomFinder, set up in an ObjectDB.Awake postfix): a YAML list of tracked prefabs with ranges and boss-key conditions, hotkeys to cycle targets, server sync. Its search reads Beacon.m_instances directly and skips any Beacon whose prefab name is not in its YAML. It adds beacons only to Destructible objects (Destructible.Awake postfix, only when no Beacon exists in children or parents), so it cannot reach the graveyard gems (PickableItem) or the wreck chests (Container). MIT, source on GitHub (Goldenrevolver/SmartWishbone). |
  | [Wishbone Direction (MbIwA, Nexus 3305)](https://www.nexusmods.com/valheim/mods/3305) | version unknown, updated 2026-04-10, 1.0: unknown (Nexus, from a search snippet) | HUD arrow, world marker or compass toward a Wishbone target within 30 m (silver veins, muddy scrap piles, buried treasure and chests); configurable search behaviour; needs Jotunn. Whether it reads Beacon components, and would then show the restored ones, is not documented (unverified). |
  | [Extended Wishbone Tweaks (MofoMojo, Nexus 906)](https://www.nexusmods.com/valheim/mods/906) | v1.2, updated 2021-04-10, 1.0: dead (Nexus, from a search snippet) | Adds copper, tin and Deathsquitos as Wishbone targets, configurable ranges for silver, buried chests and mud piles. It added targets beyond the vanilla beacons, but not pickables or location chests. |
  | [Exploration (Smoothbrain)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Smoothbrain/Exploration/) | v1.0.4, updated 2026-02-05, 1.0: unknown | Exploration skill that also grows the Wishbone radius: it transpiles SE_Finder.UpdateStatusEffect, Beacon.FindClosestBeaconInRange and Beacon.FindBeaconsInRange to scale every Beacon's m_range (source: blaxxun-boop/Exploration). A larger range does not help objects that have no Beacon, but it will scale the restored ones. |
  | [Valheim Community Patch (MidnightMods)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/MidnightMods/ValheimCommunityPatch/) | v0.30.1, updated 2026-09-29, 1.0: yes | Vanilla bug and performance fixes with no gameplay change. Its readme and changelog list no Wishbone or Beacon fix; a natural upstream home for this fix once ours is tested. |

- **Inspiration:** Nobody fixes this: every Wishbone mod works with the beacons the game already has or adds its own targets (Smart Wishbone only to destructibles, the dead Extended Wishbone Tweaks to copper, tin and Deathsquitos); none covers pickables or location chests. Restore the designers' own beacons instead of inventing targets: the same prefabs and the same 20 m range the Deep North location prefabs set on their nested copies, nothing else. Borrow Smart Wishbone's guard (add a Beacon only when the object and its children and parents have none). Small extra: stop pinging emptied chests by removing their Beacon, which also stops a looted graveyard chest from hiding the gems beside it and works with Exploration's scaled search and Smart Wishbone's own finder. Once tested, offer the fix to Valheim Community Patch or report it to Iron Gate.

### Cartography table revamp

> When next to the cartography table, enable additional pins icons in the map The map when not near the cartography table does not change The map when near the cartography table has additional pins for various types of POI: Dungeon, cave, tower, stone, plant, creature (paw), etc. Removing a pin generated from another player by interacting with the table should prevent it from being placed again when interacting with the table again (click on the table, get pin A, remove pin A from my map, click on table again, pin A should not be added again)

- **Scope:** QoL · **Feasibility:** medium · **Who needs it:** Client · **Custom assets:** yes · **Status:** idea
- **Approach:** Three parts. (1) Extra icons: a fixed block of new PinType ints for Dungeon, Cave, Tower, Stone, Plant and Creature (paw), plus proposed extras for the 'etc.' (for example Ore, Camp, Chest, Danger), placed well clear of other icon mods (Cartur's Map Pins uses 100 upward) and never changed after release. A Minimap.Start postfix grows the private m_visibleIconTypes to cover the block (grow only, new entries visible), appends one SpriteData per type to m_icons (white PNG icons from the mod folder), clones the Icon4 button (parent of m_selectedIcon4) into a second icon row, rewires each clone (it keeps vanilla's Icon4 wiring, right click included) to IconPressed(type) and the filter toggle, and adds its highlight image to m_selectedIcons so vanilla SelectIcon, ToggleIconFilter and the gamepad D-pad handle it. Minimap.Start runs once per world load, so when the feature is switched on mid-session OnActivated runs the same setup on Minimap.instance. Double click then places, saves and shares the pin through vanilla ShowPinNameInput/AddPin. (2) Only next to a table: a MapTable.Start postfix keeps a static list of tables (destroyed entries dropped). When the large map opens (SetMapMode) and every half second while it is open (Minimap.Update postfix), the row is shown only if the local player is within TableRange (default about 5 m, the code default of Player.m_maxInteractDistance) of a listed table. Away from a table a SelectIcon prefix refuses the new types (the D-pad stops at the vanilla icons) and a selected new type falls back to Icon0; pins already placed with a new icon stay visible and removable everywhere (if the sheet means they should only be drawn at a table, the same check can switch their m_visibleIconTypes entries off away from it). (3) Removed shared pins stay removed: a Minimap.RemovePin(PinData) prefix records type and XZ position of every saved, non-Death pin the player removes outside a table read (right click, gamepad, pin-manager mods, also pins first claimed with a left click; own pins too, harmless because a table never returns a player's own pins). The list lives in Player.m_customData under a per-world key (ZNet.GetWorldUID), capped. During AddSharedMapData (flag set in a prefix, cleared in a finalizer) a Minimap.AddPin prefix skips incoming pins of the same type within 1 m of a recorded entry (name ignored so pin editors cannot defeat it); a postfix recomputes the 'map synced' result from real changes (pins added or removed, ExploreOthers returning true) because vanilla counts every AddPin call. The dismissal stays personal: during MapTable.OnWrite the skipped pins are kept aside, added back to m_pins with their author id for the duration of GetSharedMapData and removed again, so a write never deletes another player's pin from the table (vanilla rule). A setting or console command forgets the list for the current world. Optional small extra (off by default): open the large map after reading a table. Ships client-side: a map UI helper that changes no odds, costs or world rules, so the house rule that experience-changing mods ship as Both does not apply.
- **Hooks:** `Minimap.Start`, `Minimap.SetMapMode`, `Minimap.Update`, `Minimap.IconPressed`, `Minimap.SelectIcon`, `Minimap.ToggleIconFilter`, `Minimap.AddPin`, `Minimap.RemovePin`, `Minimap.AddSharedMapData`, `Minimap.ExploreOthers`, `Minimap.GetSharedMapData`, `MapTable.Start`, `MapTable.OnWrite`
- **Risks:** Pin type ints can collide with other icon mods (Cartur's Map Pins uses 100 upward; BetterMap, the MoreMapPins family and Multiplayer Tweaks assign their own); another mod may replace m_visibleIconTypes with a smaller array or iterate the whole array: grow only, pick the block once. A client without the mod, or this mod removed or disabled when a world loads (the framework removes every patch of a turned-off feature), turns the new pins into Icon3 at load and saves them that way: the pin survives, its icon is lost for good; in mixed groups a table written by an unmodded player carries Icon3. Cartur's Map Pins avoids the disabled case by growing the array even while its custom icons are switched off, which the MC framework cannot do without a patch outside the feature toggle; a sturdier but larger design saves a vanilla type and keeps the real icon in a side record. The icon panel hierarchy is prefab data (unverified names); UI overhauls and other icon grids (Cartur's grid, BetterMap's legend rows, RavenwoodMapPins/MoreMapPins menus) may overlap the new row. Better Cartography Table, Asocial Cartography (its GetSharedMapData patch swaps m_pins for a filtered list during the write), Pintervention, PlayerMapLayers, The Greatest Map and ServersideQoL AutoMapTables hook the same table read/write path or shared pins. Dismissal matching (type + 1 m) also hides a later pin of the same type on the same spot. A new type hidden with the filter toggle can only be shown again at a table (or when a pin of that type is added). The grown array and sprites must stay when the feature is toggled off mid-session (UpdatePins indexes the array by pin type, and a valid type without a sprite draws as a white square).
- **Related:** Spyglass; Ping system revamp; Compendium
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/exploration-player.md](game/exploration-player.md)
- **Existing mods:** partial

Saves its icons as PinType ints from 100 upward by growing m_visibleIconTypes in a Minimap.Start postfix (grow only), even while its custom icons are switched off, so saved pins never lose their type. 1.6.0 added PinSharing (what goes to the table) and fixed cloned pin buttons keeping vanilla's right-click wiring. Always on, not tied to a table. AI Generated tag; GitHub jekkle/cartur-map-pins. |

A deleted auto pin stays deleted. Removing the mod turns its pins into a plain icon. Client and server (ServerSync). |
  | [Pinnacle (ComfyMods)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/ComfyMods/Pinnacle/) | v1.17.0, updated 2026-09-16, 1.0: yes | Pin manager (search, edit, filter). Name tags [:sprite_name], [#color], [150%] change icon, colour and size for Pinnacle users; vanilla players see the vanilla icon. |
  | [RavenwoodMapPins (JamesJonesTV)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/JamesJonesTV/RavenwoodMapPins/) | v3.0.1, updated 2026-09-11, 1.0: yes | Pin menu with 250 bundled 32x32 icons ordered by file number; the cartography table option draws over its bottom row. Ships the DLL of KG's MoreMapPins. Family: arielle's MoreMapPins rework (v1.3.0, updated 2025-09-26, 1.0: unknown), BlackViking's MorePins remake (v420.0.12, updated 2026-06-06, 1.0: unknown); KG's original is deprecated, yet icon packs such as PixelMapIcons and Sakey's Cozy Pixel Map Icons (v1.1.2, updated 2026-09-17) still depend on it. |
  | [Asocial Cartography (VentureValheim)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/VentureValheim/Asocial_Cartography/) | v1.0.0, updated 2026-09-10, 1.0: yes | Client-side. Stops sharing your hand-placed pins to the table and/or receiving others'; ReceivePinRadius drops incoming pins near yours. Applies to the 5 player icons, optionally boss/Hildir pins and custom types listed by id. All or nothing per category, no memory of single removed pins. Swaps m_pins for a filtered list during GetSharedMapData and replaces the AddPin call in AddSharedMapData (transpiler). |
  | [Pintervention (ComfyMods)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/ComfyMods/Pintervention/) | v1.3.0, updated 2025-03-19, 1.0: unknown | Hide table pins by the player who placed them (Ctrl+F panel); switch off reading pins/map data from tables; stores player names on the table ZDO. |
  | [Better Cartography Table (nbusseneau)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/nbusseneau/Better_Cartography_Table/) | v0.8.1, updated 2025-08-12, 1.0: unknown | Private-by-default pins shared one by one as public/guild pins, stored apart from vanilla shared pins and mirrored into them. Using the table opens the map, where public pins can be removed for everyone or unshared. Real-time table sessions. Jotunn, MIT; strongly recommended on the server too. |
  | [The Greatest Map (DeathMonger)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/DeathMonger/TheGreatestMap/) | v1.7.0, updated 2026-09-29, 1.0: likely | Server and every client. Markers sync through its own server store and merge at tables or between nearby players; the table carries only exploration. An erasure is a dated tombstone that wins over older copies, so erased markers stay erased for everyone (erasing needs server permission). Recorded markers use item and trophy icons. AI Generated tag. |
  | [ServersideQoL AutoMapTables (ArgusMagnus)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/ArgusMagnus/ServersideQoL_AutoMapTables/) | v2.1.1, updated 2026-09-27, 1.0: likely | Server-only: adds pins for portals, ships, ore deposits and dungeons players found to map tables, with vanilla pin types and short labels; option to discard players' pins from tables. |
  | [ZenMap (ZenDragon)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/ZenDragon/ZenMap/) | v1.11.0, updated 2026-09-13, 1.0: yes | Related: the map is only usable at a table (NoMap style); tracks ships, carts, mounts and tombstones. |
  | [PlayerMapLayers (Zephyrfit)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Zephyrfit/PlayerMapLayers/) | v0.2.13, updated 2026-09-27, 1.0: likely | Related: per-player exploration layers and explicit per-pin sharing through the server; reading a table opens the map. AI Generated tag. |

- **Inspiration:** Borrow Cartur's Map Pins' registration: a fixed block of type ints, m_visibleIconTypes grown (never shrunk) in a Minimap.Start postfix for every new Minimap, sprites appended to m_icons; its 1.6.0 changelog warns that a cloned vanilla pin button keeps vanilla's right-click wiring. Borrow BetterMap's placement of the new icons in the vanilla pin legend, where a right click hides a type as it does for vanilla icons. Borrow Better Cartography Table's idea that the table is where shared pins are managed, but stay small: no separate pin layer, no server part. Differ from Asocial Cartography and Pintervention (all-or-nothing filters by category, distance or player) with per-pin dismissal, and from The Greatest Map and Better Cartography Table (deletion for everyone) by keeping it personal: a dismissed pin stays on the table for everyone else, as vanilla never lets a player delete another player's table pin. BetterMap and AutoPOI already keep deleted auto pins deleted; this does the same for table pins. Showing the extra icons only next to a table gives the table a purpose in normal (non-NoMap) worlds. Integrate with the Spyglass (pin what I look at with a POI icon), the Ping system revamp (typed pings) and the Compendium, implemented as MC's Encyclopedia mod: it lists no places yet (locations are outside its 0.1.0 scope), so a later locations page could list the POI pins, and its paw print, drawn in code without an asset file, can serve as the Creature pin icon.

### Better shops

- **Scope:** New · **Feasibility:** easy · **Who needs it:** Client · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** idea
- **Approach:** Config-driven stock per trader with progression unlocks, buy-quantity selector, sell-selected/sell-all, buyback and optional daily rotation seeded by world day + trader position (deterministic across clients with no networking).
- **Hooks:** `Trader.GetAvailableItems`, `Trader.Start`, `StoreGui.FillList`, `StoreGui.BuySelectedItem`, `StoreGui.SellItem`, `StoreGui.GetSellableItem`, `StoreGui.UpdateBuyButton`, `ItemDrop.SharedData.m_value`, `Trader.TradeItem.m_buyKey / m_incrementKey`, `Trader.UseItem`, `Trader.CheckConditionals`
- **Risks:** EpicLoot heavily extends Haldor's StoreGui (bounties/gamble) and other trader overhauls patch the same methods; coin exploits from price asymmetry; world-level/quality/variant of added items; per-client configs diverge unless synced.
- **Related:** Late Game quest
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/exploration-world.md](game/exploration-world.md)
- **Existing mods:** partial

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [TradersExtended (shudnal)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/shudnal/TradersExtended/) | 2.0.4, 2026-09-24, 1.0 tagged | Per-trader buy/sell lists, two-column UI, custom currencies, repairs, finite balances, markups, buyback, discovery and key gating. Excellent. |
  | [EpicLoot Adventure Mode (RandyKnapp)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/RandyKnapp/EpicLoot/) | 0.14.13, 2026-09-24, 1.0 tagged | Haldor secret stash, gambling, treasure maps, bounties, token economy. |
  | [Haldor Overhaul / Trader Overhaul (ProfMags)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/ProfMags/HaldorOverhaul/) | 2026-02/03, pre-1.0, AI-generated | 580+ items priced from recipes, search and tabs, one UI for all three traders. |
  | [Better Trader (Menthus)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Menthus/Better_Trader/) | 2.0.4, 2021-05-18, deprecated | The original overhaul: 310+ items, price fluctuation, discovery gating. |

- **Inspiration:** Vanilla Trader.TradeItem supports m_requiredGlobalKey and m_buyKey (one-time purchases such as the 1.0 inventory upgrades). Keep vanilla scarcity rather than 'buy everything'. Ideas: daily rotating stock, a specialty per trader, reputation from purchases and quests that unlocks tiers, low sell-back prices limited by the trader's coin, and a new traveling sea trader found by ship (Exploration). Study TradersExtended rather than compete on generic configuration.

### Hip lantern as utility item

- **Scope:** New · **Feasibility:** easy · **Who needs it:** Both · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** idea
- **Approach:** Clone the vanilla Lantern item, set m_itemType = Utility, and add an attach_Hips (or thigh bone) child holding the lantern visual + Light under an offset holder, since AttachArmor zeroes the attach child's local transform; copy durability settings and reduce light range, add a recipe.
- **Hooks:** `ObjectDB.Awake`, `ObjectDB.CopyOtherDB`, `Humanoid.EquipItem`, `Humanoid.UpdateEquipment`, `VisEquipment.SetUtilityEquipped`, `VisEquipment.AttachArmor`
- **Risks:** Uses the single utility slot (competes with Megingjord/Wishbone/Wisplight); an extra slot would conflict with ExtraSlots/AzuEPI-style mods; clients without the prefab see nothing; items deleted if mod removed; many lights cost performance (no shadows).
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/exploration-player.md](game/exploration-player.md)
- **Existing mods:** full

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [HipLantern (shudnal)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/shudnal/HipLantern/) | 1.1.12, 2026-09-21, 1.0 tagged (~173K downloads) | Craftable hip lantern that burns fuel (0 = infinite), refuel recipe, own custom slot or the utility slot, configurable light, item-stand placement, EpicLoot integration, server-synced. |
  | [ExtraSlots (shudnal)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/shudnal/ExtraSlots/) | 1.0-era, reported working | Extra equipment slots; the usual companion to HipLantern. |

- **Inspiration:** Already solved and well maintained, so do not duplicate it. Vanilla has a single utility slot (Humanoid.m_utilityItem) plus a trinket slot. If we touch lighting, make the vanilla lanterns (Dvergr and the new 1.0 lanterns) hip-mountable, or fold it into a broader 'utility belt' UX idea. Recommendation: deprioritize.

### Boss summon revamp

> Bring new challenges and rewards for resummoning bosses

- **Scope:** New · **Feasibility:** medium · **Who needs it:** Both · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** idea
- **Approach:** Track per-world kills per boss in a valued global key; on summon the altar owner (prefix on DelayedSpawnBoss sets a static spawn context) stamps a tier into the boss ZDO right after Instantiate. Every peer applies the tier from the ZDO in Character.Awake (HP/damage multipliers, resistances, a tier-specific m_bossEvent RandomEvent that spawns adds/changes weather) and the boss owner appends tier rewards in GenerateDropList.
- **Hooks:** `OfferingBowl.UseItem`, `OfferingBowl.Interact`, `OfferingBowl.GetHoverText`, `OfferingBowl.RPC_SpawnBoss`, `OfferingBowl.DelayedSpawnBoss`, `Character.Awake`, `Character.SetLevel`, `Character.GetDamageModifiers`, `Attack.ModifyDamage`, `Character.OnDeath`, `CharacterDrop.GenerateDropList`, `RandEventSystem.m_events / Character.m_bossEvent`, `ZoneSystem.SetGlobalKey`, `EnemyHud`
- **Risks:** SetLevel also multiplies level-multiplied drops by 2^(level-1); conflicts with CLLC boss affixes, Drop That/Spawn That, EpicLoot (level-keyed loot tables, death/drop patches); keep activeBosses/NoBossPortals bookkeeping; world-level summon items; ownership migration mid-fight (store state in ZDO, apply idempotently).
- **Related:** Mob variant; Big unique dungeon; Late Game quest
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/exploration-world.md](game/exploration-world.md)
- **Existing mods:** partial

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [StarLevelSystem (MidnightMods)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/MidnightMods/StarLevelSystem/) | 1.19.1, 2026-09-26, 1.0 tagged | Boss modifiers (BossSummoner, SoulEater, LifeLink, ResistPierce, Brutal), stars above 2, YAML loot scaling, public API. |
  | [CreatureLevelAndLootControl (Smoothbrain)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Smoothbrain/CreatureLevelAndLootControl/) | 4.6.4, 2025-05-26, deprecated | The classic: boss affixes (Reflective, Shielded, Mending, Summoner, Elementalist, Enraged, Twin), boss level range, loot multipliers. |
  | [Enhanced Bosses Redone (MaxFoxGaming)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/MaxFoxGaming/Enhanced_Bosses_Redone/) | 1.10.3, 2023-06, deprecated | New boss attacks, with each attack's frequency and HP-threshold phases set in JSON. |
  | [ValheimFortress (MidnightMods)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/MidnightMods/ValheimFortress/) | 0.37.2, 2026-09-18, 1.0 tagged | Buildable shrine or arena: summon enemy waves for rewards. Closest to a challenge-and-reward loop. Also CustomizeAltars (deprecated 2024): JSON altar config. |

- **Inspiration:** Nothing escalates the fight on each re-summon or rewards repeat kills. Idea: 'Boss Trials'. Track wins per boss per world (OfferingBowl ZDO or a world counter). Each tier raises the offering cost, adds stars and affixes (reuse our mob variants), adds arena modifiers (forced weather, adds), and gives tier-specific rewards (trinket or Forsaken-power upgrades, crafting materials, cosmetics). Borrow Enhanced Bosses' JSON phases and ValheimFortress's reward flow. Integrate with the StarLevelSystem API instead of competing. Avoid global difficulty scalers.

Build a discovery-driven, spoiler-safe bestiary and atlas (like Hollow Knight's Hunter's Journal). Entries unlock on first encounter; knowledge tiers driven by vanilla kill counts reveal HP, resistances, drops, variants and boss trial records. Atlas pages hold discovered POIs, dungeons and lore. Extend the native Compendium tabs instead of adding a new window. Almanac is bloated and admin-oriented; VNEI is recipe-first and full of spoilers.

### Deep North revamp

> Rework Deep north to be dormant then VERY active after the first stone is broken

- **Scope:** New · **Feasibility:** medium · **Who needs it:** Both · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** [Deep North Awakening 0.1.0](../src/Exploration/DeepNorth.Awakening) (in development)
- **Approach:** The server counts destroyed BlackIce_Start ZDOs (the Morkhalla Malicious Ice) into a valued global key; stage = min(count, 3); every stone shows the vanilla banner. The Deep North is cut into seeded jittered-grid cells; a stage wakes a growing share of them (20/40/70 percent). Own SpawnData entries (Krigen, Hexen, Elaking) run from an UpdateSpawning postfix and only spawn inside awake cells (IsSpawnPointGood prefix), with stage star odds through m_overrideLevelupChance; nature bands (10-20 frost Greydwarfs with 2-4 shamans, 1-3 Gammeltroll or Barka) now and then, also after Kall until the area is cleared. Each cell storms on a seeded schedule (blizzard env override, meteor entry). The vanilla stone trigger is skipped on the owner; the server starts 3 vanilla invasions at the third stone and one per later stone. Nature vs Jotun through an IsEnemy postfix. After Kall an area spawns its Jotun once each time a player walks into it (then engaged on the server while anyone is near) and is cleared for good once 8-13 Jotun are killed inside it (the server counts destroyed Jotun ZDOs by position; counts and cleared cells kept on zone-control ZDOs; half-way and cleared news). The areas show as one purple tint on the explored map (vanilla map colour texture). Built as Deep North Awakening.
- **Hooks:** `ZDOMan.m_onZDODestroyed (server: BlackIce_Start destroyed)`, `TriggerPersistentEventOnDestroy.OnDestroyed`, `PersistentEventSystem.RPC_RequestStartEvent`, `ZoneSystem.SetGlobalKey / GetGlobalKey(string, out string)`, `SpawnSystem.UpdateSpawning / UpdateSpawnList`, `SpawnSystem.IsSpawnPointGood`, `SpawnData.m_overrideLevelupChance`, `Character.Awake`, `BaseAI.IsEnemy(Character, Character)`, `EnvMan.GetEnvironmentOverride`, `ZDOMan.GetAllZDOsWithPrefabIterative`, `SpawnSystem.GetNrOfZDOInstances (burst caps per area)`, `Minimap.Update / GenerateWorldMap / TryLoadMinimapTextureData (map tint)`
- **Risks:** Persistent events are saved by list index and drawn on every map, so the areas must not be persistent events; spawning runs on zone-owner clients, so every player needs the stage and rules; spawn-list editors (EWD, Spawn That, DropNSpawn) replace SpawnSystem lists (own entries stay outside them); level mods (StarLevelSystem, CLLC) override star odds; World Advancement Progression blocks unknown global keys; worlds already progressed need a one-time count of broken Morkhalla.
- **Related:** Weather staff; Mob variant; Big unique dungeon; Wish bone deep north fix
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/exploration-world.md](game/exploration-world.md)
- **Existing mods:** none

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [MonstrumDeepNorth (Therzie)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Therzie/MonstrumDeepNorth/) | 2.0.6, 2025-03-10, deprecated | Pre-1.0 content for the old placeholder Deep North (creatures, locations, Forsaken bosses). Obsolete. |
  | [Expand World Data (JereKuusela)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/JereKuusela/Expand_World_Data/) | 1.74.0, 2026-10-01 (Expand World Events merged in) | Key-conditioned spawns, events and environments per biome in YAML; can prototype dormant/active phases. 1.74 added requiredPersistentEvent to its spawn data; no persistent-event code. |
  | [Spawn That / Custom Raids (ASharpPen)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/ASharpPen/Spawn_That/) | 2026-09-09 / 2026-09-10, 1.0 tagged | Config-driven spawners and raids with global-key conditions. |
  | [World Advancement Progression (VentureValheim)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/VentureValheim/World_Advancement_Progression/) | 1.0.0, 2026-09-10, 1.0 tagged | Key-based progression gating. |

- **Inspiration:** Nothing like this exists (no public mod hooks PersistentEventSystem or the Malicious Ice, checked 2026-10-01). In 1.0, breaking the Malicious Ice (BlackIce_Start) in Mörkhalla triggers a Jotun Invasion (event jotun_invasion; its centre location is FimbulLocation01). The user confirmed the Malicious Ice is the first stone. Vanilla spawns and events already gate on global keys (SpawnSystem.SpawnData.m_requiredGlobalKey, CreatureSpawner.m_requiredGlobalKey, RandomEvent.m_requiredGlobalKeys), and AltBiome can swap environments and spawn lists. Design: dormant Deep North (calm weather, sparse life) until a world key is set on the first break, then an active phase (blizzards, Jotun patrols, raids on bases in the biome, new music, more loot). Mostly data plus a small plugin; prototype with EWD or Spawn That.

### Distant horizon

> See farther

- **Scope:** New · **Feasibility:** medium · **Who needs it:** Client · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** [Distant Horizons 0.1.0](../src/Exploration/View.DistantHorizons) (in development)
- **Approach:** Built (Distant Horizons, ported 2026-10-02 from the author's standalone mod): a level-of-detail quadtree of the game's own distant heightmaps over the whole world replaces TerrainLod's 3x3 grid (exact zone heights on the finest levels, computed on the game's builder thread); far tiles are painted by a camera CommandBuffer in a scaled-down space so the terrain shader's 200-400 m fade never starts; far trees as runtime-baked impostor cards plus big rocks and buildings from the client's ZDO store, handed over to the real objects once spawned; a far sea sheet with the game's water material; clear-weather fog thinning that multiplies the rendered density. Client-only, nothing sent.
- **Hooks:** `TerrainLod.OnEnable`, `TerrainLod.OnDisable`, `HeightmapBuilder.Build`, `HeightmapBuilder.RequestTerrain`, `Heightmap.ApplySettingsOnAll`, `Water.ApplySettings`, `EnvMan.SetEnv`, `Camera.onPreRender (CommandBuffer)`, `ZDOMan.m_objectsBySector`, `ZDOMan.m_onZDODestroyed`, `ZNetScene.HaveInstance`
- **Risks:** GPU/CPU cost and memory; the builder thread is shared with zone loading; shader constants (fades, water bands) are not in the code and can change with a game update; fog mods that write the fog density (NoFogBruh, Seasons, GammaOfNightLights, Skarif) stack with its thinning; New Horizons: Treelines does the same job; world-size mods need WorldRadius set by hand; on a host far objects show every generated zone, other players' bases included.
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/exploration-world.md](game/exploration-world.md)
- **Existing mods:** partial

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [Render Limits (JereKuusela)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/JereKuusela/Render_Limits/) | 1.15.0, 2026-08-30, deprecated (probably superseded by the vanilla 1.0 setting) | Number of active, loaded and generated zones; real-terrain distance; LOD bias; clutter distance; shadows; server caps. |
  | [RenderSettings (romenh)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/romenh/RenderSettings/) | 2021, dead | Extra camera and render settings. |
  | [Render Limits Fix (MagiCorp)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/MagiCorp/Render_Limits_Fix/) | 1.16.0, 2026-09-17, 1.0 yes, AI | 1.0 fork of Render Limits (same plugin ID); maps its zone limits to the 1.0 SimulationDistance. |
  | [New Horizons: Treelines (EchoesOfBunglas)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/EchoesOfBunglas/New_Horizons_Treelines/) | 4.6.35, 2026-07-17, 1.0 unknown, AI | Unlit far terrain underlay (700 m tiles, about 1.3-2 km to 8.5 km from the player, heights from GetBaseHeight only) and painted billboard tree cards placed by seeded noise (Meadows, Black Forest, Mountains), raised far plane, clear-sky fog and cloud dimming. Patches nothing. Closed source. Same job as Distant Horizons. |
  | [Valheim Performance Overhaul (Skarif)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Skarif/ValheimPerformanceOverhaul/) | 8.2.4, 2026-09-29, 1.0 yes, AI | Distant Terrain LOD Improvements: far plane 3000 m, LOD hide distance x1.25, fog density / 2.25. |
  | [NoFogBruh (Vapok)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Vapok/NoFogBruh/) | 2.1.2, 2026-10-01, 1.0 yes | Removes distance fog, mist and fog particles. |

- **Inspiration:** Built as Distant Horizons (src/Exploration/View.DistantHorizons, 2026-10-02). Before: vanilla 1.0 added a SimulationDistance setting (more near zones; far ring fixed at 2; server-synced). Distant terrain already draws as a low-detail heightmap (Heightmap.IsDistantLod), but objects beyond loaded zones do not. Nothing like Minecraft's 'Distant Horizons' exists for Valheim. Idea: a client-only impostor layer for trees, rocks and landmarks beyond loaded zones (from WorldGenerator and vegetation data, or cached from visited zones), plus better distant terrain and fog. Unlike Render Limits it loads nothing from the server. High technical risk: investigate Unity 6 rendering and instancing first.

### Mob variant

> from valheim magic (elemental, blood, etc)

- **Scope:** New · **Feasibility:** medium · **Who needs it:** Both · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** idea
- **Approach:** The spawning client rolls a variant (fire/frost/lightning/poison/spirit/blood) from a biome/star weighted table and stores it in the creature ZDO; every peer applies tint/VFX/name from the ZDO in Character.Awake, the owner applies resistances (GetDamageModifiers), extra elemental damage/status effects (Attack.ModifyDamage), death effects and variant essence drops for the magic/crafting mods.
- **Hooks:** `SpawnSystem.Spawn`, `CreatureSpawner.Spawn`, `SpawnArea.SpawnOne`, `TriggerSpawner`, `Character.Awake`, `Character.GetDamageModifiers`, `Attack.ModifyDamage`, `Character.SetMaxHealth`, `Character.OnDeath`, `CharacterDrop.GenerateDropList`, `LevelEffects.SetupLevelVisualization (pattern)`
- **Risks:** Direct overlap with CLLC infusions/effects and EpicLoot damage modifiers; inheritance on taming/procreation; exclude bosses; particle cost per creature; spawns happen on clients so the mod must be everywhere.
- **Related:** Boss summon revamp; Deep North revamp
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/exploration-world.md](game/exploration-world.md)
- **Existing mods:** partial

  | Mod | Status | Notes |

Incompatible with CLLC. |
  | [EliteCreaturesReborn (MilkyTeam)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/MilkyTeam/EliteCreaturesReborn/) | 3.11.0, 2026-09-28, AI-generated | 13 mutations (Leeching, Warding, Bloated, Splintering, Blinking, Gilded...), colored nameplates, per-biome YAML, 4 loot modes. |
  | [Monster Modifiers (warpalicious)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/warpalicious/Monster_Modifiers/) | 1.2.7, 2025-08-20, pre-1.0 | 31 modifiers, one per star; open source (jneb802/MonsterModifiers). |
  | [CreatureLevelAndLootControl (Smoothbrain)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Smoothbrain/CreatureLevelAndLootControl/) | 4.6.4, 2025-05-26, deprecated | Original Fire/Frost/Lightning/Spirit/Poison/Chaos infusions. |

- **Inspiration:** Generic affixes are well covered. Variants themed on Valheim's magic schools are not. Elemental-touched variants: fire, frost or lightning auras mirroring the vanilla staffs. Blood-cursed variants: lifesteal, raising skeletons, sacrifice-HP bursts. Give each distinct shaders and drop essences or reagents for the Magic/Crafting revamps. Regional variants via AltBiome sectors (e.g. a Blood-soaked Swamp). Be additive to, or plug into, the StarLevelSystem API rather than compete with its install base.

### New ability depending on skill level

> at 25 - 50 - 75 - 100

- **Scope:** New · **Feasibility:** medium · **Who needs it:** Depends · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** idea
- **Approach:** Each skill gets four named abilities, at 25, 50, 75 and 100. A registry declares them as (SkillType, tier, id, name, description, passive or active); injected custom skills such as Sailing register the same way. The local player's tiers are re-evaluated on Player.OnSpawned, in a Skills.RaiseSkill postfix and on a 1 s timer (Player.Update postfix, filtered to Player.m_localPlayer). The timer also catches meads and set bonuses (SEMan.ModifySkillLevel), the console command Skills.CheatRaiseSkill and deaths, none of which go through the level-up event. Each check takes the highest tier at or below Skills.GetSkillLevel (or, as an option, the peak level kept in Player.m_customData) and compares it with a per-character cache, so a respawn does not re-announce every tier. Gained and lost abilities are announced with Player.Message and the skill icon. Passive tiers are hidden SE_Stats created at runtime (m_hidden, m_ttl 0, a unique object name because StatusEffect.NameHash hashes it). SEMan.AddStatusEffect(StatusEffect) clones them without an ObjectDB entry, and the HUD skips them. Fall damage, the fall-speed cap, jump force, swim speed, stealth, noise and stamina costs then need no Harmony patch. Passive tiers are re-added after respawn, because Player.OnDeath calls SEMan.RemoveAllStatusEffects. Active tiers reuse vanilla animation triggers that already replicate through ZSyncAnimation.SetTrigger: 'jump' (Character.ForceJump) and 'dodge' (Player.UpdateDodge). Example Jump set: 25 soft landing; 50 landing roll (vanilla already queues a Dodge pressed in the air for 0.5 s and fires it after touchdown, so an UpdateGroundContact prefix only checks that queue and cuts the fall damage); 75 one jump in the air (a Player.SetControls prefix calls ForceJump); 100 slow fall while Jump is held (fall-speed cap plus a fall damage cut, because fall damage depends on the height fallen, not on speed). A SkillsDialog.Setup postfix adds each skill's four tiers to its tooltip. First pack: Run, Jump, Swim, Sneak, Dodge and Ride (24 abilities). Sailing gets its tiers from the Sailing revamp. Feasibility is medium: the framework is small and many tiers need no patch. The cost is content, and abilities that need new animation or physics (diving, ledge mantle) stay in their own ideas. Who needs it depends on the ability. Abilities on the local player are client-only: the owner simulates the player, and stealth and noise reach enemies through the player ZDO (s_stealth, s_noise). The rider owns the Sadle ZDO after Sadle.RPC_RequestControl and sends its Ride factor in the Controls RPC. Abilities on ships or on other characters need everyone.
- **Hooks:** `Skills.GetSkillLevel`, `Skills.GetSkillList`, `Skills.RaiseSkill`, `Player.OnSkillLevelup`, `Skills.CheatRaiseSkill`, `Skills.OnDeath`, `Skills.LowerAllSkills`, `Skills.Clear`, `SEMan.ModifySkillLevel`, `Player.OnDeath`, `Player.OnSpawned`, `Player.Update`, `Player.Message`, `SkillsDialog.Setup`, `UITooltip.Set`, `SEMan.AddStatusEffect`, `SEMan.RemoveStatusEffect`, `SE_Stats (m_fallDamageModifier, m_maxMaxFallSpeed, m_jumpModifier, m_swimSpeedModifier, m_stealthModifier, m_noiseModifier, stamina-use modifiers)`, `Player.m_customData`, `Player.SetControls`, `Character.Jump`, `Character.ForceJump`, `Character.UpdateGroundContact`, `Player.Dodge`, `Player.UpdateDodge`, `Player.CheckRun`, `Player.OnSwimming`, `Player.UpdateStealth`, `Sadle.UpdateRiding`
- **Risks:** Death: on the Normal preset a hard death costs 5 % of every skill. The presets cost 1 % (Casual, Very easy), 2.5 % (Easy), 5 % (Normal) and 7.5 % (Hard), and Hardcore sets the DeathSkillsReset global key, which clears all skills through Skills.Clear (wiki). Game.m_skillReductionRate defaults to 1, so the Player prefab must override the C# initialiser Skills.m_DeathLowerFactor = 0.25; log it at runtime. A Normal death drops a tier only when the base level is less than about 5 % above it (25 to 26.3, 50 to 52.6, 75 to 78.9), but always drops the level-100 ability (100 becomes 95). Choose between current-level gating (default, vanilla-consistent) and peak-level gating. Meads and set bonuses unlock and relock tiers: avoid message spam. Skills.GetSkillLevel creates a level-0 entry for a skill the player never used, and that entry then shows in the skills panel and is saved. Only read skills listed by Skills.GetSkillList. For an injected skill without a SkillDef, that entry has m_info = null and breaks the panel and the save. Content volume: covering all 24 vanilla skills means 96 abilities. Tiers stack on vanilla's linear scaling, which needs balancing. The air jump must not fire while a grappling hook is attached, because Character.Jump uses that case to pull the hook. Conflicts: ImpactfulSkills patches Character.Jump, Character.UpdateGroundContact, Character.UpdateSwimming, Character.UpdateWalking, Player.OnSwimming and Character.AddNoise. PIXPIX Movement adds its own double jump and landing roll. GrindstoneSkills and SkillPeak also change the skills panel. Skill overhauls, XP multipliers and death-penalty settings change how fast tiers are reached or lost. Mods that clear status effects remove passive tiers until the next check. Skills are client-authoritative (saved in the character file by Skills.Save), so a server cannot enforce tiers. Ship and other-character abilities need everyone and must be evaluated on the owner. Stealth stacking: the Sneak tiers' stealth SE_Stats add up with the Sneak revamp's standing-still SE (SE_Stats.ModifyStealth adds base x modifier for each, and Player.UpdateStealth only clamps the sum to 0..1, so the factor could reach 0); the Sneak revamp floors the combined factor while its SE is active, so tune the tiers with it loaded.
- **Related:** Swim dive; Sailing revamp; Sneak revamp; Increase base HP/Stamina with stats
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/exploration-player.md](game/exploration-player.md)

Its movement modules only change numbers. Jump: height from level 10, fall damage reduction from 15 and a higher safe-fall height from 25 (patches Character.Jump and Character.UpdateGroundContact). Swim: speed from 25 and stamina from 50 (Character.UpdateSwimming, Player.OnSwimming). Sneak: backstab bonus from 25 and noise reduction from 50 (Character.AddNoise). Perk effects run through one aggregate status effect. The page says only 69 of 384 nodes do something so far. |

They unlock by defeating bosses (configurable), not by skill level. Slide and Slam use custom animation work, plus sound effects and a 3D asset. Mostly client-driven, but also install it on a dedicated server if you use one. About 7K downloads. |

It records peaks in a Skills.Skill.Raise postfix, stores them in Player.m_customData (Player.Save prefix, read back in a Player.OnSpawned postfix) and draws them in a SkillsDialog.Setup postfix. A ready pattern for a 'keep abilities at peak level' option. |

Nothing released; listed only because it is the closest concept. |

- **Inspiration:** What to borrow: GrindstoneSkills' named milestones listed with their level in the skills panel, and its configurable milestone levels (a level above 100 turns one off). ImpactfulSkills' per-effect required levels. SkillPeak's Player.m_customData record for an optional peak-level mode. NorntasticSkillGrowth's single aggregate status effect for perk effects, the same approach as our hidden SE_Stats. PIXPIX Movement's movement actions (landing roll, double jump, dash) as candidate tiers. What existing mods get wrong: ImpactfulSkills' movement effects are hidden number increases with no unlock feedback (announcements are only on its roadmap). GrindstoneSkills skips the movement skills and must be installed on the server and every client. PIXPIX gates movement abilities on bosses, not on the skill the ability uses. NorntasticSkillGrowth adds a point-buy perk tree whose nodes are mostly unimplemented. Valheim Mastery replaces vanilla progression with its own 21 skills. What ours does differently: exactly four named abilities per vanilla skill, at 25, 50, 75 and 100. Each unlock gets one clear message, the tiers are shown in the vanilla skills tooltip, and every ability can be switched off. The first pack is client-only and covers the exploration skills (Run, Jump, Swim, Sneak, Dodge, Ride). Most passive tiers are hidden status effects rather than patches. Active tiers reuse vanilla animation triggers, and the landing roll is vanilla's own queued dodge. The Sailing revamp adds the Sailing tiers.

### Sailing revamp

> Skill (easier to catch the wind, turn, stop, accelerate, wider map reveal radius)

- **Scope:** New · **Feasibility:** medium · **Who needs it:** Both · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** [Sailing Skill 0.1.0](../src/Exploration/Sailing.Skill) (in development)
- **Approach:** Register a Sailing skill (Jotunn SkillManager or own injector) and grant XP to the helmsman while sailing with sail up. On taking the helm write the helmsman skill factor to the ship ZDO (and optionally make the helmsman owner); the owner's Ship.CustomFixedUpdate prefix scales m_sailForceFactor/m_stearForce/m_stearVelForceFactor/m_backwardForce and restores them in the postfix, GetWindAngleFactor/GetSailForce patches widen usable wind angles and speed up acceleration, an instant-stop key calls the unused Ship.Stop, and Minimap m_exploreRadius grows with skill while aboard.
- **Hooks:** `Skills.IsSkillValid`, `Skills.GetSkillDef`, `Skills.Load`, `ShipControlls.RPC_RequestControl`, `Ship.UpdateOwner`, `Ship.CustomFixedUpdate`, `Ship.GetWindAngleFactor`, `Ship.GetSailForce`, `Ship.ApplyControlls`, `Ship.Stop`, `Minimap.UpdateExplore`, `Player.Update`
- **Risks:** Physics runs on the ship ZDO owner (possibly a passenger) so mixed installs desync; must restore scaled fields (finalizer) to avoid compounding; conflicts with ValheimRAFT, sail/wind mods and the linked Nexus mod 922; custom skill data dropped from saves if mod removed; do not alter EnvMan wind globally.
- **Related:** New ability depending on skill level; Cartography table revamp
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/exploration-player.md](game/exploration-player.md)
- **Existing mods:** partial (sheet link: https://www.nexusmods.com/valheim/mods/922)

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [Sailing Skill (Gaijinx, Nexus 922, the user's link)](https://www.nexusmods.com/valheim/mods/922) | 2021-era; needs pipakin Skill Injector and Mod Config Enforcer 1.x; dead | Tailwind boost, forewind dampening, ship damage reduction, rudder speed, each up to 50%. |
  | [Sailing (Smoothbrain)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Smoothbrain/Sailing/) | 1.1.8, 2026-02-05, deprecated | Skill: health of ships you built, speed of ships you command, explore radius; per-ship speed; nudge hotkey. |
  | [ImpactfulSkills - Voyager skill (MidnightMods)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/MidnightMods/ImpactfulSkills/) | 0.20.2, 2026-09-28, 1.0 tagged | Level 25 paddle speed, 35 less boat damage, 50 smaller wind penalty, 75 impact immunity. |
  | [GrindstoneSkills - Sailing (MilkyTeam)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/MilkyTeam/GrindstoneSkills/) | 0.9.1, 2026-09-27, updated post-1.0 | +50% ship HP, +20% speed, 2x explore radius, level 50 Lookout pulse. Also: Smooth Sailing and HelmWind (1.0, AI-generated, always-favorable wind), ShipExploration (1.0, explore radius), LongshipUpgrades (1.0). |

- **Inspiration:** Vanilla 1.0 has no Sailing skill (Skills.SkillType added Crafting, Dodge and Ride only), and map reveal is a fixed 100 m (Minimap.m_exploreRadius) with no ship bonus. Handling is tuned by Ship.m_sailForceFactor, m_rudderSpeed, m_stearForce and m_backwardForce, used in Ship.GetSailForce and UpdateSail. Build a real Sailing skill, gained at the helm, covering the user's list: a wider angle where the sail still pulls (not wind cheating, so tacking keeps skill), turn rate, braking/reverse, acceleration, a larger explore radius, and milestone abilities (lookout, wind forecast). Apply the controlling player's skill (Player.GetControlledShip). Avoid 'always tailwind' cheats and bonuses only for ships you built. Check for conflicts with ImpactfulSkills and GrindstoneSkills.

### Spyglass

> See things far away

- **Scope:** New · **Feasibility:** medium · **Who needs it:** both · **Custom assets:** yes · **Status:** [Spyglass 0.1.0](../src/Exploration/View.Spyglass) (in development)
- **Approach:** Built (Spyglass, 2026-10-02): a craftable tool like the hammer (clone of KnifeFlint made a Tool with no build pieces, both hands taken, model and icon made in code), raised with Attack: a two-bone arm pose made in code (synced by a player ZDO bool) brings it to the eye while a GameCamera.UpdateCamera postfix slides the camera into the eye and narrows the field of view of both cameras (no SetTempFOV, m_fov, m_distance or m_mouseSens); a round sharp view with a blurred, darker edge drawn without a shader of its own (OnRenderImage downsample + UI ring meshes); aim scaled in Player.SetMouseLook, feet locked in Player.SetControls. Far land and objects come from the MC Distant Horizons mod, which draws finer land and farther objects in the looked-at direction while a spyglass is up (AppDomain slot, no reference). Both side with server rules (recipe, max zoom, fog clearing). Map pins, map reveal and the target readout are left for later.
- **Hooks:** `GameCamera.UpdateCamera`, `Player.SetControls`, `Player.SetMouseLook`, `Player.AlwaysRotateCamera`, `Player.UpdateHover`, `Player.OnDamaged`, `Player.Update`, `Player.LateUpdate`, `Hud.UpdateCrosshair`, `ObjectDB.Awake`, `ObjectDB.CopyOtherDB`, `ObjectDB.GetAllCraftableWeapons`, `ZNetScene.Awake`, `Minimap.AddPin`, `Minimap.Explore`
- **Risks:** Far creatures, ships and carts never exist outside the active area, so the spyglass cannot show them; without Distant Horizons the view ends at the vanilla range (about 300-500 m of objects, land in the fog); camera and first-person mods also place the camera in UpdateCamera (the spyglass runs last); the item is deleted from inventories without the mod; the arm pose fights the animator (AnimatePhysics) and needs remote players to have the mod.
- **Related:** Cartography table revamp
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/exploration-player.md](game/exploration-player.md)

- **Inspiration:** The user thought this did not exist, but basic zoom (a GameCamera.m_fov change) is solved and maintained. Only worth building as an exploration tool: reveal the map in the view cone at long range (ties to cartography), one-key pin on the target, show the target's name, stars, variant and kill count (kill count / mob variant / Compendium), sky-pointed weather forecast, and ship crow's-nest use. Smooth zoom, vignette, sway. Advize is GPL, so reimplement rather than copy.

### Weather staff

> Change the weather with the staff

- **Scope:** New · **Feasibility:** medium · **Who needs it:** Both · **Custom assets:** yes · **Status:** idea
- **Approach:** Casting spawns a networked weather totem whose ZDO stores env name, radius and expiry; each client's EnvMan.GetEnvironmentOverride postfix returns that env while the local player is inside an active totem (below boss/raid overrides). Variants or a radial menu pick Clear/Rain/Storm/Fog/Snow from EnvMan.m_environments filtered per biome; optional wind control.
- **Hooks:** `Attack (m_attackEitr, m_attackProjectile / m_spawnOnTrigger)`, `EnvMan.GetEnvironmentOverride`, `EnvMan.AppendEnvironment`, `EnvMan.ForceInstantEnvironmentSwitch`, `EnvMan.UpdateWind / SetTargetWind`, `PersistentEventSystem (alternative carrier)`, `ZoneSystem.SetGlobalKey (alternative carrier)`, `Character.InInterior`
- **Risks:** Override priority vs raid/boss/AltBiome/persistent-event envs and forced EnvZones; clients without the mod see vanilla weather (wet/cold and env-gated spawns desync); balance (sailing wind exploits, mist is biome-gated ParticleMist); conflicts with other EnvMan-patching mods.
- **Related:** Deep North revamp
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/exploration-world.md](game/exploration-world.md)
- **Existing mods:** partial
