- **Inspiration:** Vanilla already has EnvMan.SetForceEnvironment, EnvMan.m_debugEnv and EnvZone.m_force. Idea: an Elemental Magic staff that, when channeled, casts Clear Skies, Call Storm or Summon Fog for N minutes. Costs eitr plus a reagent, has a cooldown, and its duration and radius scale with skill. The server owns the forced environment. Synergies: clear weather for sailing, storms that boost lightning magic, fog for stealth. Borrow Rune Magic's lore framing; avoid WeatherStones' type-a-string UI.

### Late Game quest

> Pick up quest from NPC to get rare items (kill specific target, bring specific item, find specific location, etc)

- **Scope:** New · **Feasibility:** hard · **Who needs it:** Depends · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** idea
- **Approach:** Client-only v1 on vanilla traders. Vanilla Trader.Interact ignores its alt argument, so Shift+E on Haldor, Hildir or the Bog Witch opens a contract board once a late-boss global key is set. The board offers 3 contracts per in-game day, seeded by ZNet.GetWorldUID + EnvMan.GetDay so every player of a world sees the same list, and keeps accepted contracts in Player.m_customData under the world UID. There are four contract types. (1) Slay a named elite: when the quester nears the pinned spot and ZoneSystem.IsZoneLoaded is true, the quester's client instantiates the creature, sets stars with Character.SetLevel, writes a name to ZDOVars.s_overrideHoverName and a contract id to a custom ZDO key, and calls BaseAI.SetPatrolPoint. Character.GetHoverName, and therefore EnemyHud, shows that name on every client, including clients without the mod. The contract completes when Game.RPC_RegisterKill arrives for that m_name while the tracked ZDO has 0 health (ZDOVars.s_health) or is destroyed (ZDOMan.m_onZDODestroyed). (2) Hunt N: count matching Game.RPC_RegisterKill calls (postfix) while the player is in the contract's world. Do not use the PlayerProfile per-creature totals, which cover every world. (3) Deliver: Inventory.CountItems/RemoveItem at the trader, through a Trader.UseItem prefix or a button on the board. (4) Survey: Game.DiscoverClosestLocation with discoverAll false and a chosen point (for example a random point a few km away), so the server answers with the one instance closest to that point. A Game.RPC_DiscoverLocationResponse prefix recognises the request's unique pinName, swallows that reply and stores the position. Arrival is then a distance check or Location.GetLocation. Rewards are rare vanilla items, so only the quester needs the mod. A new NPC, custom reward items or shared party contracts would require everyone to install it (side: depends).
- **Hooks:** `Trader.Interact`, `Trader.GetHoverText`, `Trader.UseItem`, `StoreGui.Show`, `Chat.SetNpcText`, `Player.m_customData`, `ZNet.GetWorldUID`, `EnvMan.GetDay`, `ZoneSystem.GetGlobalKey`, `Game.RPC_RegisterKill`, `ZDOMan.m_onZDODestroyed`, `ZNetScene.GetPrefab`, `ZoneSystem.IsZoneLoaded`, `ZoneSystem.GetSolidHeight`, `Character.SetLevel`, `Character.GetHoverName (ZDOVars.s_overrideHoverName)`, `BaseAI.SetPatrolPoint`, `ZNetView.ClaimOwnership`, `Game.DiscoverClosestLocation`, `Game.RPC_DiscoverLocationResponse`, `ZoneSystem.FindClosestLocation`, `ZoneSystem.FindLocations`, `Location.GetLocation`, `Inventory.CountItems`, `Inventory.RemoveItem`, `Minimap.AddPin`, `Minimap.RemovePin`, `MessageHud.ShowMessage`, `Trader.TraderUseItem.m_setsGlobalKey + Trader.TradeItem.m_requiredGlobalKey (Hildir pattern)`
- **Risks:** Vanilla kill credit is keyed by player name in the victim's ZDO (set in Character.RPC_Damage, which runs on the owner). Only the owner sends it: Character.OnDeath returns before the credit loop on other peers. OnDeath is reached from CheckDeath on the owner, or from the CharacterAnimEvent.Die animation event for creatures with a death animation. The RPC carries the creature name, boss order, modifiers, attacker count and a cheat flag, but no ZDO id. So a specific target needs a second signal, players with the same name share credit, and hooks on the owner's code path (as in Haldor Fetch Quests) miss kills of creatures that another peer owns. The PlayerProfile per-creature totals cover every world the character played and also grow on cheated kills, so a 'kills since accepted' delta could be filled in another world. Spawned targets never despawn: only SpawnSystem sets the despawn-in-day and event-creature flags. An expired contract must therefore remove its tagged creature (as owner or after ZNetView.ClaimOwnership), and any player can kill it. Two questers who take the same shared daily contract would spawn two elites unless the spawn first looks for an existing tagged creature. ZDOMan.m_onZDODestroyed fires only on peers that hold the ZDO. With discoverAll, the server sends one reply per instance. Each reply that is not intercepted adds a pin and turns the player's view (Character.SetLookDir, when the map is closed). A prefix must not swallow the reply to a vanilla vegvisir read made at the same time, so match the pin name. The RPC only knows registered locations, and the chosen one may already be looted. The late-boss defeat keys are not in the GlobalKeys enum (it names only the first five bosses): they come from Character.m_defeatSetGlobalKey on the boss prefabs (read the prefab at runtime, or check with listkeys). Inventory counts match world level by default. EpicLoot (its own window at Haldor), HaldorBounties (a board in Haldor's shop) and TradersExtended already change the trader UI, so check that no other trader mod uses alt interact. Other risks: reward inflation, and cross-world farming if contracts are not keyed by the world UID.
- **Related:** Better shops; Mob variant; Boss summon revamp; Big unique dungeon; Deep North revamp; Per creature kill count
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/exploration-world.md](game/exploration-world.md)
- **Existing mods:** partial

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [Almanac (RustyMods)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/RustyMods/Almanac/) | v3.8.0, 2026-09-25, tagged Deep North (1.0: yes) | YAML quests (Collect, Farm, Harvest, Kill, LearnItems, Mine) with StartQuest/CompleteQuest dialogue commands. NPCs whose YAML dialogue syncs from the server and reloads live. Bounties spawn a buffed creature in a biome with a map pin, and the cost is refunded if it despawns or escapes. The defaults are Boar, Neck, Troll, Serpent, Abomination, Wraith, Lox, Seeker Brute and Fallen Valkyrie. Treasure hunts place map pins that spawn loot containers. A token store. The content is YAML that the server admin edits, and there is no 'reach a location' quest type. About 227K downloads and 355 dependants. RustyMods' earlier TraderQuest (a trader panel with bounty, treasure hunt, alt shop and gambling; v1.0.7, 2025-03-12) is deprecated. |
  | [EpicLoot Adventure Mode (RandyKnapp)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/RandyKnapp/EpicLoot/) | v0.14.13, 2026-09-24, tagged Deep North (1.0: yes) | A new window at Haldor. Bounties spawn a mini-boss, sometimes with minions, at a random spot in its biome. They pay coins plus Iron/Gold tokens, can be limited per player, and can be gated by boss kills (Gated Bounty Mode). Treasure maps send the player to a chest in a biome. Also gambling and a secret stash. 0.14.13 repairs bounties and treasure that never spawned because of the 1.0 save migration; 0.14.11 moved their pin filters into the vanilla map. Tied to the whole magic-loot system. About 2.2M downloads. |
  | [HaldorBounties (ProfMags)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/ProfMags/HaldorBounties/) | v0.0.5, 2026-03-09, tagged Ashlands and Bog Witch, AI-generated (1.0: unknown) | A daily board in Haldor's shop: 4 bounties per day (2 kill, 1 named miniboss with a map marker that fights like a player, 1 raid warband at campfires). The daily selection is shared server-wide. The player picks 1 of 4 rewards (coins, ingots, resources, consumables), and a live timer shows the next reset. Tiers follow boss progression only up to Mistlands (after Yagluth), so there are no Ashlands or Deep North contracts. Requires HaldorOverhaul (1.0.18+ per its GitHub README). About 8K downloads. |
  | [BeastMarketOutpost (Yggdrah)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Yggdrah/BeastMarketOutpost/) | v1.0.1, 2026-09-21, tagged Deep North, AI-generated (1.0: likely) | A new outpost NPC mod. Nugget, a small dragon, guards a Quest Board where players browse missions to hunt creatures, gather resources and complete objectives across the biomes for rewards; the page gives no details on objective types, rotation or rewards. Also has companions (pets and mounts unlocked per Forsaken boss). Must be installed on the server and all clients. About 1K downloads. |
  | [Marketplace And Server NPCs Revamped (KGvalheim)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/KGvalheim/Marketplace_And_Server_NPCs_Revamped/) | v9.8.1, 2026-04-25, deprecated (dead) | The classic server NPC framework: a Quests NPC with admin-made quests (custom types, conditions, timeouts, random rewards, hand-in at another NPC), plus trader, banker, teleporter, gambler and buffer NPCs. About 387K downloads. Its add-on Marketplace Quests (JewelHeim, v1.0.1, 2023-06-25) is also deprecated. |
  | [Haldor Fetch Quests + Quest Framework (aedenthorn)](https://github.com/aedenthorn/ValheimMods/tree/master/HaldorFetchQuests) | v0.4.0 source (references Quest Framework 0.3.0), written 2021-10, folder last changed 2025-03-04; published on Nexus as Haldor Quests (1.0: unknown) | Random kill or fetch quests offered in Haldor's store (3 at a time by default). The list refreshes every 86,400 game seconds by default (the config calls it 24 hours). The gold reward is based on the item value or the creature's max health. Kill progress comes from a Character.RPC_Damage postfix that checks the attacker is the local player; that RPC runs only on the creature's owner, so kills of creatures owned by another peer do not count. Quest state is saved in a file named after the character and the world name. Its patch targets still exist by name in 1.0.16. |
  | [HildirsQuest (Smoothbrain)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Smoothbrain/HildirsQuest/) | v1.0.2, 2023-10-06, tagged Ashlands (1.0: unknown) | Makes Hildir's vanilla chest quests per player instead of world-wide, and lets mini-bosses drop one chest per nearby player. It fills a vanilla gap: Trader.UseItem completes a Hildir quest by setting a world global key. About 86K downloads. |

- **Inspiration:** Borrow EpicLoot's world-spawned target with minions, a map pin and boss-kill gating. Borrow HaldorBounties' daily board (pick 1 of N rewards, visible reset timer), Almanac's typed objectives (Kill, Collect, ...) as the data schema, and Hildir's key-based unlocks. What existing mods get wrong: token currencies and gambling that sidestep progression (EpicLoot, Almanac); content the server admin must write in YAML or config (KG Marketplace, most of Almanac apart from its default bounties); no Ashlands or Deep North contracts (HaldorBounties stops at Mistlands, and it needs HaldorOverhaul); new NPCs that every player and the server must install (BeastMarketOutpost); owner-side kill detection that misses kills in multiplayer (Haldor Fetch Quests patches Character.RPC_Damage); and quest completion that is world-wide rather than per player (vanilla Hildir). Ours: a small, curated late-game contract board that unlocks after the Queen or a later boss. It lives on vanilla traders behind Shift+E, so StoreGui stays free for EpicLoot, TradersExtended and HaldorBounties. It has four contract types: named elite, hunt N, deliver a rare item, and survey a late-biome location. Rewards are rare vanilla items with no new currency. The client-only v1 is built only on vanilla RPCs and ZDO keys, so it works in mixed groups and on vanilla servers.

### music instruments

> (prerecorded songs + midi support) for buffs well rested

- **Scope:** New · **Feasibility:** hard · **Who needs it:** both · **Custom assets:** yes · **Status:** [Music Instruments 0.3.0](../src/Exploration/Music.Instruments) (in development)
- **Approach:** Built (Music Instruments, 2026-10-05): three craftable instruments, Wooden Flute (FineWood), Tambourine (FineWood, LeatherScraps) and Silver Lyre (Silver, LinenThread), KnifeFlint clones made Tools with models and icons made in code. A synthesizer of the mod's own plays them in OnAudioFilterRead (flute wavetable with breath, Karplus-Strong lyre, noise and partials tambourine) on an AudioSource that loops a clip of 1.0 samples, so Unity's 3D panning and the SFX mixer group still apply; no audio files. Attack with an instrument opens a song window: eight built-in songs (four public-domain traditional tunes, four written for the mod) or MIDI files from BepInEx/config/MC_Valheim/Songs (own SMF reader; automatic melody part, lyre bass line, tambourine from the drums), played by themselves or in a four-lane rhythm mini-game (keys held back through a Chat.HasFocus postfix). The performer's game streams notes over a plain ZRpc to the server, which relays them to compatible players within hearing range; listeners map the performer's clock to their own audio clock with a small jitter buffer. 20 s of good mini-game play (rolling accuracy, success meter) give the Music status effect (+3 comfort through a Player.GetComfortLevel postfix, which SE_Rested reads for the Rested length, also when resting outdoors) to the performer and, through an Encore flag in the stream, to every listener within BonusRange, each game applying it to its own player. Prerecorded songs (the sheet's wording) are covered by the built-in songs and MIDI files; the buff works through comfort, which lengthens Rested. Version 0.2.0 adds server songs: a server can share the MIDI files of a folder with every player (ShareSongs; a file is sent when a player picks one, in 4 KB pieces paced by the connection send queue so world data keeps priority).
- **Hooks:** `Player.GetComfortLevel`, `SE_Rested.CalculateComfortLevel`, `SE_Rested.UpdateTTL`, `SE_Cozy.UpdateStatusEffect`, `SEMan.AddStatusEffect`, `ObjectDB.Awake`, `ObjectDB.CopyOtherDB`, `ZNetScene.Awake`, `Player.SetControls`, `Player.PlayerAttackInput`, `Player.Update`, `Player.LateUpdate`, `Player.OnDamaged`, `Chat.HasFocus`, `Terminal.TryRunCommand`, `Menu.Update`, `GameCamera.UpdateMouseCapture`, `GameCamera.OnDestroy`, `MusicVolume.UpdateProximityVolumes`, `ZNet.OnNewConnection`, `ZNet.RPC_PeerInfo`
- **Risks:** Comfort only lengthens Rested while resting (after the Resting delay, near a fire, in shelter or sitting), so the bonus helps players who rest during or after a performance; Rested never shortens. Comfort displays that call CalculateComfortLevel themselves do not show the bonus (the GetComfortLevel hook keeps the MaxComfort stat and its achievement vanilla). Game music mods (Viking Shanties, Ominous) also write the music volume. Crossplay resends lost packets after 1-3 s: listeners drop notes that come too late. The synthesized timbres and the poses need tuning by ear and eye.
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/exploration-player.md](game/exploration-player.md)
- **Existing mods:** partial

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [Bardheim (LongHouseListings)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/LongHouseListings/Bardheim/) | 0.7.0, 2026-06-07, 1.0: unknown; all rights reserved | Lyre, drum and flute (workbench, wood and hides); embedded WAV samples per note; own MIDI parser (format 0/1, tempo, running status); manual play on keys 1-8; multiplayer audio off by default (one routed RPC per note to everybody); no buffs, no mini-game. |
  | [Bragi (Jumenteam)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Jumenteam/Bragi/) | 0.2.0, 2026-10-01, 1.0: likely; GPL-3 | Lyre, Bone Flute, Jaw Harp; plays your own OGG/WAV songs with a JSON file, synced by a start time; its Rested buff (Skald's Blessing) cannot work (string.GetHashCode for the effect, AddStatusEffect(StatusEffect) on other players). No MIDI, no mini-game. |
  | [Viking Shanties (Glasir)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Glasir/VikingShanties/) | 1.0.13, 2026-09-30, 1.0: yes | No items: dance and sing nine bundled shanties (MP3) near a fire or on a ship, heard nearby; makes Rested come twice as fast while singing at a sheltered fire; fades the game music. |
  | [Skald of Camelot (Soukteam)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Soukteam/Skald_Of_Camelot/) | 1.0.7, 2026-10-04, 1.0: likely | Add-on to Bardheim and Bragi: instrument buffs (Rested extension with the same hash bug, carry weight, health regen) with a cooldown; mutes any sound whose name contains "cat". |

- **Inspiration:** Instruments with MIDI exist (Bardheim) and prerecorded songs with a Rested-related buff exist (Viking Shanties, Bragi), but no mod synthesizes its music, has a rhythm mini-game, ties a comfort bonus to playing well, shares the bonus reliably with nearby players, or places the instruments on the progression tiers. Research 2026-10-05: EXISTS ALREADY = Partially.

### Swim dive

> Allows to dive vertically

- **Scope:** New · **Feasibility:** hard · **Who needs it:** Client · **Custom assets:** yes · **Status:** [Swim Dive 0.1.0](../src/Exploration/Swimming.Dive) (in development)
- **Approach:** Postfix Character.UpdateSwimming for the local player to override vertical velocity from Crouch (dive) / Jump (ascend) read directly from ZInput, letting buoyancy return the player when released; track head under water via GetHeadPoint vs GetLiquidLevel to drain a breath meter and apply Drowning damage. Remove the camera water clamp while submerged and apply an underwater fog/tint.
- **Hooks:** `Character.UpdateSwimming`, `Character.UpdateWater`, `Character.IsSwimming`, `Player.OnSwimming`, `Player.UpdateCrouch`, `GameCamera.GetCameraPosition`, `Character.UnderWorldCheck`, `WaterVolume.OnTriggerExit`, `Hud`
- **Risks:** Water volume trigger may not contain the player at depth (OnTriggerExit sets level -10000 -> falling); ocean surface may be invisible from below and no vanilla underwater effect; needs dive animation for good visuals; other clients see an upright sinking swimmer unless they run the mod; Ashlands water/tar differ.
- **Related:** New ability depending on skill level
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/exploration-player.md](game/exploration-player.md)
- **Existing mods:** full

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [BetterDiving (MainStreetGaming)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/MainStreetGaming/BetterDiving/) | 1.1.2, 2026-09-10, 1.0 tagged; Apache-2.0 | Crouch toggles diving and you dive where you look; Diving skill; oxygen bar; fast swim. Fork of Easy_Develope's Valheim Diving Mod (Nexus 1271). |
  | [VikingsDoSwim (blacks7ar)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/blacks7ar/VikingsDoSwim/) | 1.4.2, 2026-09-15, 1.0 tagged | Swim speed and stamina scale with the Swim skill, plus diving. Incompatible with BetterDiving. |
  | [Dive In (sighsorry)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/sighsorry/Dive_In/) | 1.2.3, 2026-09-09, 1.0 tagged, AI-generated | Dedicated ascend/descend keys (true vertical diving), depth-scaled stamina drain, underwater visuals, creatures chase underwater, weapons usable in water. |
  | [Underwater (Crystal) / UnderTheSea (Jacobo)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Crystal/Underwater/) | older | Walk on the seafloor; crouch to dive. |

- **Inspiration:** Vanilla keeps the player at the surface (Character.m_swimDepth = 2) and has no breath system. Diving is covered three times over, by mutually incompatible mods that patch the same swimming code. Only build our own as the base for the Underwater biome: vertical dive keys, Breath tied to the vanilla Swim skill, crafted diving gear (helmet, weights, underwater lantern), cold/pressure in Deep North waters. Or soft-depend on BetterDiving (Apache-2.0) and ship only content.

### Big unique dungeon

> One very large unique dungeon per biome with end game level of difficulty

- **Scope:** New · **Feasibility:** very-hard · **Who needs it:** Both · **Custom assets:** yes · **Status:** idea
- **Approach:** One location prefab per biome with a DungeonGenerator using a custom Room.Theme bit (e.g. 0x8000) and larger m_zoneSize, registered unique+prioritized so world gen places exactly one; revealed by a vegvisir/NPC. For existing worlds a server pass spawns it in an ungenerated/empty zone via the private SpawnLocation and registers it as placed.
- **Hooks:** `ZoneSystem.SetupLocations`, `ZoneSystem.ZoneLocation (m_unique, m_prioritized, m_quantity)`, `DungeonDB.SetupRooms`, `DungeonGenerator.Generate`, `DungeonGenerator.m_zoneSize / m_useCustomInteriorTransform`, `Location.Awake`, `CreatureSpawner`, `Teleport.Interact`, `EnvZone`, `Game.DiscoverClosestLocation`, `ZoneSystem.SpawnLocation (private) + RegisterLocation`
- **Risks:** Room geometry is parented to the generator and vanishes when the generator's zone leaves the player's near simulation ring (1 zone at lowest setting) - chain zone-sized generators via Teleport pairs for very large dungeons; interior EnvZone only 64x500x64; one location per zone and only ungenerated zones; orphan ZDOs/'Missing room' if mod removed; clashes with location packs and world-gen mods (Warpalicious, Expand World, Better Continents); render/light cost.
- **Related:** Boss summon revamp; Late Game quest; Deep North revamp
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/exploration-world.md](game/exploration-world.md)
- **Existing mods:** partial

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [More World Locations AIO (warpalicious)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/warpalicious/More_World_Locations_AIO/) | 5.1.4, 2026-09-25, 1.0 tagged | 190 POIs including 2 procedural dungeons. High quality. |
  | [Forbidden Catacombs / Underground Ruins (warpalicious)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/warpalicious/Underground_Ruins/) | 2025-04/06, pre-1.0 | Procedural Swamp (21 rooms) and Black Forest (24 rooms, puzzles) dungeons, vanilla-like progression. |
  | [Expand World Data + Dungeon Splitter (JereKuusela)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/JereKuusela/Expand_World_Data/) | 1.73.0, 2026-09-23, updated post-1.0 | YAML dungeon generators and rooms, locations, biomes and events. Dungeon Splitter (deprecated) splits network sync by height (1500 m); players above one spot still share one dungeon, so it is not instancing. |
  | [Dungeon Creation Kit (MoonTower)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/MoonTower/Dungeon_Creation_Kit/) | 3.1.2, 2026-09-27, AI-generated | Build multi-level dungeons from pieces, in a shared cave placed high in the sky above an entrance (not instanced, no bosses). Also Dungeonheim (Nexus 1997), a handcrafted 7-dungeon world seed in German. |

- **Inspiration:** Nothing adds one handcrafted, unique, endgame-difficulty dungeon per biome. Idea: a named legendary dungeon per biome, placed exactly once per world, with multiple floors and key/lock progression, mini-bosses and a final guardian. Endgame difficulty regardless of biome, unique loot tied to Crafting, optional reset. Borrow warpalicious' asset-bundle and Jotunn room pipeline, EWD's dungeon YAML and Dungeon Splitter's height-layer sync filter (for performance; it does not instance anything). Avoid procedural sameness. The most art-heavy idea: it needs a Unity asset pipeline.

### Underwater biome

- **Scope:** New · **Feasibility:** very-hard · **Who needs it:** Both · **Custom assets:** yes · **Status:** idea
- **Approach:** Phase 1: ocean AltBiome sub-biome (reef/abyss) adding seabed vegetation, sea creatures and ambience with vanilla ocean-depth filters, reachable from boats. Phase 2: diving - patch swimming depth with a breath status effect, lift the camera water clamp while diving and add an underwater fog/tint post-process when the camera is below the liquid level.
- **Hooks:** `AltBiomeList.m_altBiomes (AltBiome with m_biome=Ocean)`, `ZoneSystem.PlaceVegetation (m_minOceanDepth/m_maxOceanDepth)`, `SpawnSystem.IsSpawnPointGood`, `Character.UpdateSwimming`, `GameCamera.GetCameraPosition`, `Floating.IsUnderWater`, `WaterVolume.GetWaterSurface`, `StatusEffect (breath)`, `BaseAI`, `Fish`
- **Risks:** Adding a Heightmap.Biome value breaks BiomeHelpers switches, terrain shader, minimap and AltBiomeWorldData - use AltBiome instead; one-sided water surface, no underwater lighting; no navmesh on seabed; conflicts with diving mods patching UpdateSwimming/camera; AltBiome lists must match on all peers.
- **Related:** Big unique dungeon
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/exploration-world.md](game/exploration-world.md)
- **Existing mods:** partial

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [RtDOcean (Soloredis)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Soloredis/RtDOcean/) | 2.2.49, 2026-09-24, updated post-1.0 | Sea life, tameable dolphins/orcas/mermaids, sharks, sunken ships with treasure, seaweed, ocean boss Belzor, relics, crops and dishes. No diving needed; no seafloor biome. |
  | [SeaAnimals (Marlthon)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Marlthon/SeaAnimals/) | 0.3.8, 2026-09-11, 1.0 tagged | 12+ marine animals spawning by depth, biome and global key; rideable orca, dolphin, crocodile and shark. |
  | [Bestiary (Radamanto)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Radamanto/Bestiary/) | 1.2.0, date not checked | Creature pack including white sharks and whales. |

- **Inspiration:** Ocean content exists; a real underwater biome does not. The 1.0 AltBiome system (per-sector modifiers that add or block spawns, environments, vegetation and locations, and force environments) could host ocean sub-biomes; height changes are disabled in vanilla. Idea: a deep-ocean seafloor biome with trenches, kelp, ruins, sunken longships, bioluminescence, its own environment and music, and dive-only resources. Depends on diving and Crafting breathing gear. Borrow RtDOcean's POIs and SeaAnimals' depth-based spawning. Heavy art and rendering work.

## Farming

### Harpoon to work on tamed animals

- **Scope:** QoL · **Feasibility:** easy · **Who needs it:** Client · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** [Harpoon Hooks Tames 0.1.0](../src/Farming/Harpoon.HooksTames) (in development)
- **Approach:** A Projectile.IsValidTarget postfix returns true for a tamed target when the owner is the local player and the projectile's status effect resolves to SE_Harpooned. A Character.Damage prefix on the attacker side zeroes damage, push force and stagger for that hit. ApplyDamage then exits early (no damage, no alert), while RPC_Damage still applies the SE on the tame's owner.
- **Hooks:** `Projectile.IsValidTarget`, `Character.Damage`, `SE_Harpooned.SetAttacker`, `SE_Harpooned.UpdateStatusEffect`, `ObjectDB.GetStatusEffect`
- **Risks:** PvP players can already harpoon tames but also damage them; apply the zero-damage rule anyway. Heavy tames drain a lot of stamina (scaled by mass). There is no pull while the tame is on a ship and no upward force. Keep the patch additive to coexist with EpicLoot projectile patches.
- **Related:** Bring tamed / pets on ship
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/farming-cooking.md](game/farming-cooking.md)
- **Existing mods:** full

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [TamedHarpoon (Brainless_Azura)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Brainless_Azura/TamedHarpoon/) | 1.0.0, 2026-09-27, 1.0 tagged, AI-generated | The Abyssal Harpoon hooks tames with no damage or knockback, and the vanilla pull (SE_Harpooned) does the rest. Only the thrower needs it. |
  | [CreatureCarry (Wendigo)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Wendigo/CreatureCarry/) | 1.3.0, 2026-03-21, pre-1.0, AI-generated | Pick up and carry tames, including through portals. Protects tames. |

- **Inspiration:** Covered as of yesterday by a brand-new AI-tagged 1.0.0. Build our own only as part of a Ranching bundle. Possible additions: owner-only or ward-only hooking, a soft 'leash' mode with no line break, and a lead-to-pen command. Vanilla filters tames in the Attack/Projectile hit logic (IsTamed, SharedData.m_tamedOnly).

### Easy plant

- **Scope:** QoL · **Feasibility:** medium · **Who needs it:** Client · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** cancelled
- **Approach:** When a cultivator piece with a Plant component is selected, show an N x M grid of ghosts spaced 2 x m_growRadius and aligned to the rotation. Validate each ghost with the same checks as UpdatePlacementGhost (cultivated, biome, space). On place, call PlacePiece for each valid point and charge resources per plant. Optionally replant on harvest.
- **Hooks:** `Player.UpdatePlacementGhost`, `Player.TryPlacePiece`, `Player.PlacePiece`, `Player.ConsumeResources`, `Player.HaveRequirements`, `Plant.m_growRadius`, `Heightmap.IsCultivated`, `Pickable.Interact`
- **Risks:** Already exists (Advize's PlantEasily), so running both conflicts; prefer compatibility. Ghost count costs performance. The vanilla 1.0 scythe already does mass harvest.
- **Related:** Plant "everything"
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/farming-cooking.md](game/farming-cooking.md)

- **Inspiration:** Don't rebuild. PlantEasily is the reference and is maintained. Just make our plantables ordinary Cultivator pieces with Plant components so they work with it. The only differentiator left is a grid size gated by skill or tool tier.

### Plant "everything"

> cf cultivator

- **Scope:** QoL · **Feasibility:** medium · **Who needs it:** Both · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** cancelled
- **Approach:** Clone a vanilla sapling for each target (RaspberryBush, BlueberryBush, CloudberryBush, Pickable_Mushroom*, Pickable_Thistle, Pickable_Dandelion, tree saplings). Set the target as its only m_grownPrefabs entry, give it a Piece with the seed item as resource and a biome mask, register it in ZNetScene, and add it to the cultivator piece table (or use Jotunn PieceManager).
- **Hooks:** `ZNetScene.Awake`, `ObjectDB.Awake`, `PieceTable.m_pieces (_CultivatorPieceTable)`, `Piece`, `Plant`, `Pickable`
- **Risks:** Already exists (Advize's PlantEverything). Uninstalling leaves ZDOs with unknown prefab hashes. Balance. Interaction with the Ashlands trees idea: its burn and scorch rules touch only the saplings on its target list and the trees they grow, so saplings cloned here are left alone unless they are added to that list.
- **Related:** Easy plant; Ashlands trees
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/farming-cooking.md](game/farming-cooking.md)

- **Inspiration:** Covered. Any new plants we add (Ashlands or Deep North flora, tappable trees) should register as normal Cultivator pieces so PlantEverything and PlantEasily users get them for free.

### Breeding revamp

> (from 50/50 from parent to lowest of parent + chance of proc +1)

- **Scope:** Revamp · **Feasibility:** easy · **Who needs it:** Both · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** [Breeding Star Inheritance 0.1.0](../src/Farming/Breeding.StarInheritance) (in development)
- **Approach:** A MakePregnant postfix finds the nearest ready partner (same prefab or m_seperatePartner) within m_partnerCheckRange and stores its level in a custom ZDO int on the pregnant parent. A Procreate prefix and postfix set and clear a thread-static birth context. Inside that context, Character.SetLevel and ItemDrop.SetQuality prefixes replace the level with max(minOffspring, min(own, partner)) + (Random < chance ? 1 : 0), clamped to a configurable cap (default 3).
- **Hooks:** `Procreation.MakePregnant`, `Procreation.Procreate`, `Character.SetLevel`, `ItemDrop.SetQuality`, `BaseAI.BaseAIInstances`, `Growup.GrowUpdate`
- **Risks:** Pregnancies started before install have no stored partner (fall back to own level). Both parents can become pregnant in vanilla. The SetLevel patch must be strictly scoped to the context, or world spawns are affected. Conflicts with AllTameable-style breeding mods and star-level mods (levels above 3 show no stars in EnemyHud).
- **Related:** Bring tamed / pets on ship; Bring tamed / pets through stone portal
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/farming-cooking.md](game/farming-cooking.md)
- **Existing mods:** partial

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [Procreation Plus (MaxFoxGaming)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/MaxFoxGaming/Procreation_Plus/) | 1.2.2, 2026-09-13, 1.0 tagged | YAML file with a LevelUpChance and MaxStars per prefab (boar 25%, wolf 10%). Adds a chance of +1 on top of the vanilla base level, which is the birthing parent's. |
  | [BreedingUpgrades (Dumba)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Dumba/BreedingUpgrades/) | 1.0.2, 2026-01-11, pre-1.0 | Flat 5% chance of +1 star for live births and eggs. Server-synced config. Source on GitHub (DaiMinhTri/BreedingUpgrades). |
  | [TameCraft (momos3939)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/momos3939/TameCraft/) | 1.0.1, 2026-09-13, 1.0 tagged, AI-generated | Husbandry overhaul: affection, faster breeding, mother and sire tracking. Optional two-parent Mendelian star inheritance with mutation (off by default). |
  | [CreatureGenetics / AllTameable Taming Overhaul (Meldurson)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Meldurson/CreatureGenetics/) | CreatureGenetics 0.0.3, 2026-09-14, 1.0 tagged. AllTameable 1.5.4, 2026-03-14, pre-1.0 | Levels inherited from the parents with a +/-1 mutation. Eggs carry the level. DNA traits. Open source (github.com/meldurson). |

- **Inspiration:** Nobody implements min(parents) + chance of +1. The +1 chance exists on its own, and two-parent inheritance only exists inside big overhauls. Vanilla Procreation.Procreate uses max(m_minOffspringLevel, birthing parent level) and never stores the partner. Plan: when ZDOVars.s_pregnant is set, record the nearest partner's level (within m_partnerCheckRange) in the mother's ZDO. At birth, roll min + chance of +1. Pass the result to eggs through ItemDrop quality (EggGrow reads it). Only the ZDO owner decides. The rule is configurable (min/avg/max/vanilla). The +1 chance could scale with skill or food so the min rule does not stall progress.

### Ashlands trees

> Being able to plant any tree that has no biome restriction in ashlands. If crop is not placed in a shield, burns away and is destroyed. If crop is placed in a shield, then grows following the crop's rules. If tree is fully grown outside of a shield, turns into a scorched tree after a cooldown If tree is fully grown inside of a shield, stays unhurt

- **Scope:** Revamp · **Feasibility:** medium · **Who needs it:** Both · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** idea
- **Approach:** Line 1 (plant trees in the Ashlands): the targets are a config list of sapling prefabs, by default the vanilla tree saplings whose biome list includes Meadows: Beech_Sapling, Birch_Sapling, Oak_Sapling, PineTree_Sapling and FirTree_Sapling (wiki). FirTree_big_Sapling (Deep North) stays out. The list is fixed rather than read from the biome masks, because PlantEverything rewrites them (with it, FirTree_big_Sapling also gets the temperate biomes). Heat-tolerant saplings are never targets. A ZNetScene.Awake postfix that runs after PlantEverything's (Priority.Last) adds AshLands to their Plant.m_biome, and to Piece.m_onlyInBiome when that is set, and undoes it on deactivation. PlantEverything rewrites these fields again on every config change, and only on the prefabs, so a Player.SetupPlacementGhost prefix puts the bit back on the selected prefab before the ghost is made from it (the ghost, the placed instance and CropUtils, which reads the prefab, then all see it), and the Plant.Awake postfix puts it back on target instances as they load. If the saplings have m_vegetationGroundOnly (unverified), placement keeps the vanilla Ashlands ground rule, so nothing can be planted on or next to lava. Line 2 (saplings outside a shield burn): in the sheet, "crop" means the planted tree sapling (decided by the user, 2026-09-30), so a sapling planted outside a shield cannot grow and burns. The Plant.Awake postfix attaches an AshExposure component to every target sapling in the Ashlands without m_tolerateHeat. Placement ghosts have no ZDO and are skipped. The component ticks every 5 s on the ZDO owner, after a 20 s grace from load. Outside every shield (ShieldGenerator.IsInsideShield) it adds the tick to a custom ZDO float; inside a shield it resets the float to 0. Past SaplingBurnDelay (config, about 30 s) it plays a vanilla burn effect (fx_CinderFire_Burn or vfx_Burning) and calls Plant.Destroy. Line 3 (saplings inside a shield): nothing to add. The target sapling follows its vanilla rules (Plant.UpdateHealth), with the Ashlands now in its biome list, and grows as in vanilla. Vegetable crops (carrot, turnip, onion, barley and every other sapling that grows into a Pickable) are out of scope. They are not targets, get no component and keep their vanilla behaviour in the Ashlands (Plant.UpdateHealth): they never burn, and a crop that does not tolerate heat stalls as WrongBiome when its biome list has no Ashlands, or as TooHot outside a shield. Line 4 (grown trees outside a shield scorch): a tree ends up outside a shield when the shield it grew under is removed or breaks, runs out of fuel, or shrinks as its fuel drops (the user's example: a sapling planted in a shield grows into a full tree, then the shield is removed). A Plant.Grow postfix sets a custom ZDO bool on the tree (__result) when the sapling was a target, stood in the Ashlands and had no heat tolerance, so world-generated trees are never touched. The same postfix attaches the component to that tree, because the tree's TreeBase.Awake already ran inside Plant.Grow (during Instantiate), before the flag existed. A TreeBase.Awake postfix attaches it to flagged trees that load later. Past TreeScorchDelay (config, about 10 min of exposure), the owner does four things. It spawns a random configured Scorched Tree prefab (AshlandsTree1/3/4/5/6) through ZNetScene.GetPrefab at the same position and rotation, gives it the tree's scale (ZNetView.SetLocalScale, times a config factor; the scale is saved and sent only if the prefab's ZNetView has m_syncInitialScale, unverified), plays the effect and removes the old tree with ZNetScene.Destroy. Line 5 (grown trees inside a shield): the exposure stays 0, so the tree is never scorched. Vanilla already never spawns cinders inside an active dome and destroys the ones that enter it, so no other protection is needed. An optional TreeBase.RPC_Damage prefix could also ignore CinderFire hits. Exposure counts only time while the object is loaded and lives in the ZDO, so it carries over reloads and owner changes but does not keep adding up while nobody is nearby. Live toggle: undo the prefab edits and destroy the tracked components. To cover objects already loaded when the feature turns on, scan SlowUpdate.GetAllInstaces (saplings) and ZNetScene.m_instances (trees, which are not SlowUpdates). Small extra: a Plant.GetHoverText postfix shows the burn countdown.
- **Hooks:** `ZNetScene.Awake`, `Player.SetupPlacementGhost`, `Plant.Awake`, `Plant.Grow`, `TreeBase.Awake`, `ShieldGenerator.IsInsideShield`, `Heightmap.FindBiome`, `ZNetView.IsOwner`, `Plant.Destroy`, `ZNetScene.GetPrefab`, `ZNetView.SetLocalScale`, `ZNetScene.Destroy`, `Plant.GetHoverText`, `SlowUpdate.GetAllInstaces`, `ZNetScene.m_instances`
- **Risks:** A plant can load before the shield generator that covers it (for example one in another zone), and IsInsideShield reads the animated radius, which is 0 until the generator's first update. Keep the load grace and count exposure only while loaded. The dome shrinks with fuel (10 m almost empty to 30 m full, code defaults), and cinder rain drains fuel: every cinder that enters the dome counts as 10 damage. Trees near the edge are therefore scorched when fuel drops. Warn players through sapling hover text (TreeBase has no hover text). Target saplings already stalled outside a shield when the mod is installed burn about a minute after they load; trees that grew before the install carry no flag and are never scorched. Balance: the vanilla Scorched Tree drops Ashwood and Charcoal Resin (wiki), so this makes Ashwood renewable. A stump (AshlandsTreeStump1-3) or a smaller drop table are alternatives. Scorched Trees are large: tune the scale factor in game. There is no AshlandsTree2 in the manifest, and which AshlandsTree* prefabs are free-standing trees is unverified. PlantEverything rewrites the saplings' m_biome, m_onlyInBiome, m_tolerateHeat and m_destroyIfCantGrow on the prefabs (not on live instances) when it starts and whenever its config changes, hence the ghost-time and Awake re-apply. With its PlantsRequireShielding off, every plant tolerates heat and the burn and scorch rules do nothing. It also patches Plant.Grow (a transpiler), Plant.GetHoverText and TreeBase.Awake; postfixes on the same methods coexist. PlantEasily (reads the ghost's Plant.m_biome) and CropUtils (reads the selected prefab's Plant.m_biome, only when its planting tool is used) refuse spots where a plant cannot grow, so they need the AshLands bit. A player without the mod who owns a planted beech sapling in the Ashlands sees it as wrong biome and never grows it, so the server must refuse players without the mod. Unverified prefab data: the saplings' biome masks, m_onlyInBiome, m_vegetationGroundOnly and m_destroyIfCantGrow, which tree each sapling grows, m_syncInitialScale on the Scorched Tree prefabs, the Scorched Trees' drops and stumps, and how the burn effects look.
- **Related:** Plant "everything"; Cultivator revamp; Sap collector on other trees
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/farming-cooking.md](game/farming-cooking.md)
- **Existing mods:** partial

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [PlantEverything (Advize), partial](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Advize/PlantEverything/) | v1.21.3, updated 2026-09-25, 1.0: yes | Comes close to lines 1 and 3 through config, and to line 2 only as a silent removal. EnforceBiomesVanilla (default on) writes the temperate biomes (fir also Mountain) into the vanilla saplings' Plant.m_biome and Piece.m_onlyInBiome; turned off, every vanilla sapling and crop can grow in any biome, the Ashlands included. PlantsRequireShielding (default on) keeps the vanilla heat rule, so a shield is needed there. Its saplings get m_destroyIfCantGrow unless PlaceAnywhere is on, so an unsheltered sapling is removed without any burn when its grow time ends. It adds a heat-tolerant Ashwood_Sapling (Beech Seeds + Sulfur) that grows AshlandsTree3/4/5/6_big. Nothing scorches grown trees. It rewrites these fields on the prefabs at start and on config changes, and patches Plant.Grow, Plant.GetHoverText and TreeBase.Awake. GPL source on GitHub (AdvizeGH/Advize_ValheimMods). |
  | [Seasons (shudnal), same pattern for cold](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/shudnal/Seasons/) | v1.10.3, updated 2026-09-29, 1.0: yes | Crops and pickables can perish in winter, at once or (a config option) gradually over a set number of seconds. The shield generator works as a greenhouse (against winter only, by default), and fire heat protects plants. Must be installed on the server and every client. Source on GitHub (shudnal/Seasons). |
  | [PlantEasily (Advize), compatibility](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Advize/PlantEasily/) | v2.2.2, updated 2026-09-24, 1.0: yes | Grid planting. Its ghost check reads the ghost's Plant.m_biome and flags TooHot outside a shield in the Ashlands. By default (PreventInvalidPlanting) it refuses spots where the plant cannot grow, so unsheltered Ashlands spots are refused, and trees need the AshLands bit on the ghost. |
  | [CropUtils (NoPetRides), compatibility](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/NoPetRides/CropUtils/) | v2.1.0, updated 2026-09-18, 1.0: yes | Pattern planting. When its planting tool is used, it refuses spots where a crop could never grow, checking the biome plus Ashlands heat and Mountain / Deep North cold like Plant.UpdateHealth (its README, 2.0.1 notes). It reads Plant.m_biome from the selected piece prefab (PlantingUtil.CanGrowAt), so our prefab edit reaches it. Source on GitHub (nopetrides/modding). |
  | [TreesReborn (TastyChickenLegs), related](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/TastyChickenLegs/TreesReborn/) | v1.1.3, updated 2026-09-11, 1.0: yes | Replants a sapling when a stump is destroyed. According to a Nexus snippet, its config maps AshlandsTreeStump to PlantEverything's Ashwood_Sapling. If a chopped Scorched Tree leaves such a stump (unverified), our scorched trees could keep regrowing. |
  | [ServersideQoL PrefabConfigurator (ArgusMagnus), related](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/ArgusMagnus/ServersideQoL_PrefabConfigurator/) | v2.0.14, updated 2026-09-20, 1.0: likely (server-only) | Generic prefab-field editor (plant grow time, space and more). It shows how far pure data tweaks go; it has no heat or scorch logic. |

- **Inspiration:** Partial coverage. With EnforceBiomesVanilla off, PlantEverything already lets every vanilla tree sapling grow in the Ashlands under a shield, but that setting opens every biome at once, crops included. Its defaults remove unsheltered saplings silently once their grow time is over, and it adds a heat-tolerant Ashwood sapling that grows Scorched Trees. No mod burns unsheltered saplings on a timer or turns grown trees into Scorched Trees. Seasons (shudnal) uses the same pattern for winter: plants outside a shield greenhouse perish, at once or gradually, and the mod is required on the server and every client. Ours adds only the Ashlands, only to a fixed list of trees without a special biome, leaves vegetable crops alone, and keeps the shield as the greenhouse. It re-applies its biome bit because PlantEverything rewrites the sapling prefabs, and it skips heat-tolerant saplings. PlantEasily and CropUtils already refuse unsheltered Ashlands spots, which fits the burn rule. Decide whether scorched trees keep the vanilla Ashwood drop, which makes Ashwood renewable.

### Cultivator revamp

> Base cultivator (bronze) works the same as vanilla + replant Dandelion, Rasberry bushes, Blueberry bushes, mushrooms, yellow mushrooms, Thistle need to equip the cultivator and aim at a plant bush/plant with the Replant action, it gives a new item to be planted elsewhere. New cultivator (blackmetal) does the bronze actions + replant Cloudberries, crafted with Bronze cultivator and blackmetal ingot x5 and linnen x10 New cultivator (eitr) does the blackmetal actions + replant Yggdrasil trees, but can only grow near roots and it drains energy to grow , crafted with blackmetal cultivator and refined eitr x15 New cultivator (flametal) does the eitr actions + replant Fiddlehead and Smokepuff (can only grow in Ashlands), crafted with previous cultivator and flametal ingot x5 New cultivator (bloodgold) does the flametal actions + replant lingonberries (can only grow in Deepnorth), crafted with previous cultivator and bloodgold ingot x5

- **Scope:** Revamp · **Feasibility:** medium · **Who needs it:** Both · **Custom assets:** yes · **Status:** [Cultivator Replant 0.1.0](../src/Farming/Cultivator.Replant) (in development)
- **Approach:** Keep one tool and put the four new tiers on top of the vanilla levels. In 1.0 the Cultivator already has quality 1-3 (Bronze and Corewood at the Forge) and the Forge of Potential refines it past 3 with a Wooden Battle Idol (wiki), so the tiers are quality 4-7 (raise m_maxQuality to 7): black metal 4, eitr 5, flametal 6, bloodgold 7. Add the tier materials to the Cultivator recipe with m_amount 0, so they never block recipe discovery or the first craft, and let a Piece.Requirement.GetAmount postfix (on those requirement objects only) return each level's list: BlackMetal 5 + LinenThread 10, Eitr 15, FlametalNew 5, Gold ('Bloodgold') 5, and 0 for the vanilla Bronze and Corewood above level 3. A 0 amount hides the row and is skipped by the checks. Remove the idol (m_upgraderResource) requirement so the Forge of Potential skips the Cultivator and cannot push it into a tier, and record the tier in m_customData in an InventoryGui.DoCrafting postfix (the upgrade creates a new item with empty custom data), so a cultivator refined past 3 before install does not count. The vanilla upgrade unequips and replaces the old cultivator and keeps m_name $item_cultivator, so PlantEasily and PlantEverything still recognise it. Replant action (local player, cultivator equipped): the Use key, read in a Player.UpdatePlacement postfix; it is free because Player.UpdateHover clears m_hovering in place mode. On gamepad, JoyUse in the Alternative layouts; in the Default layout JoyUse toggles the piece menu, so use the Remove button there, caught in a Player.UpdatePlacement prefix that calls ZInput.ResetButtonStatus, so neither vanilla removal nor PlantEverything's removal also fires. Raycast from the camera with m_interactMask (the mask Player.FindHoverObject uses) within m_maxInteractDistance, and look for a Pickable or TreeBase whose prefab name is in the tier table and whose tier is at most the cultivator's. Check Location.IsInsideNoBuildLocation, PrivateArea.CheckAccess, stamina and durability as the vanilla remove path does, claim the ZDO, harvest any fruit with Pickable.Interact, destroy the plant with ZNetScene.Destroy (unless the pick already destroyed it), and give one transplant item (dropped if the inventory is full). A Hud.UpdateCrosshair postfix shows '[E] Replant <plant>' or the tier needed. Each transplant item plants a cloned sapling on _CultivatorPieceTable whose only m_grownPrefabs entry is the vanilla plant, so grown plants are vanilla prefabs; clear Piece.m_cultivatedGroundOnly and Plant.m_needCultivatedGround. Fiddlehead and smoke puff saplings get Plant.m_biome and Piece.m_onlyInBiome = AshLands plus m_tolerateHeat. Lingonberry gets DeepNorth plus m_tolerateCold and Piece.m_allowedInDeepSnow. The Yggdrasil sapling (from YggaShoot_small1 or YggaShoot1-3) grows into YggaShoot1-3. It uses Piece.m_mustConnectTo = YggdrasilRoot with a configurable m_connectRadius, and a Plant.Grow prefix lets a Healthy sapling grow only when ResourceRoot.Drain(cost) on a cached nearby root succeeds (a Plant.GetHoverText postfix explains the wait). A Plant.Grow postfix sets a grown bush to picked, so replanting never creates fruit. Replanted one-shot forage gets a respawn cycle from a Pickable.Awake prefix keyed on a custom ZDO flag; the first grown instance is set up in the Plant.Grow postfix, which must also start UpdateRespawn because its Awake already ran.
- **Hooks:** `Player.UpdatePlacement`, `Player.UpdateHover`, `Player.UpdateBuildGuiInput`, `Player.RemovePiece`, `ZInput.ResetButtonStatus`, `Hud.UpdateCrosshair`, `Pickable.Interact`, `Pickable.SetPicked`, `Pickable.Awake`, `TreeBase`, `Location.IsInsideNoBuildLocation`, `PrivateArea.CheckAccess`, `ZNetView.ClaimOwnership`, `ZNetScene.Destroy`, `Inventory.AddItem`, `ObjectDB.Awake`, `ObjectDB.CopyOtherDB`, `ZNetScene.Awake`, `PieceTable.m_pieces (_CultivatorPieceTable)`, `Recipe.m_resources (Cultivator recipe)`, `Piece.Requirement.GetAmount`, `Player.RequiredCraftingStation`, `InventoryGui.DoCrafting`, `ItemDrop.ItemData.GetTooltip (static overload)`, `Plant.Grow`, `Plant.GetHoverText`, `Piece.m_mustConnectTo`, `ResourceRoot.CanDrain`, `ResourceRoot.Drain`
- **Risks:** The vanilla Cultivator already has three bronze levels and Forge of Potential refinement (wiki; recipe values not checked in game data), so the tier plan changes vanilla: the Cultivator can no longer be refined, a player must reach bronze level 3 before black metal, and quality 7 needs a level-7 station by Recipe.GetRequiredStationLevel unless Player.RequiredCraftingStation is patched per tier. The literal alternative (separate cultivator items, as the sheet words it) needs its own m_name per item, because Inventory.RemoveItem, CountItems and ObjectDB.GetRecipe match by name; a cultivator consumed as an ingredient is not unequipped (Inventory.RemoveItem does not unequip); and PlantEasily's grid and PlantEverything's cultivator removal only work when m_name is $item_cultivator. Quality tiers keep one icon and model; a per-tier icon means patching the one-line ItemDrop.ItemData.GetIcon (inlining risk). Mushrooms, thistle, dandelion, fiddlehead and smoke puff look one-shot in vanilla. PlantEverything writes respawn times into these prefabs but adds m_hideWhenPicked only to Pickable_Stone, which suggests the vanilla forage already hides a child when picked (a picked wild mushroom would then stay as a hidden, picked ZDO instead of being destroyed). Either way a replanted copy gives one harvest without our respawn fix; dump the prefabs to confirm. Transplant items and saplings are unknown prefabs for players without the mod and after an uninstall: the items are dropped from inventories and unfinished saplings stay invisible in the save until the mod is back; grown plants are vanilla prefabs and survive. Wild vegetation is placed once per zone (ZoneSystem.SpawnZone), so uprooting is a permanent move; it needs the ward check. Two players uprooting the same plant in the same instant could both get an item (same race as the hammer). Yggdrasil: Plant.HaveGrowSpace fails on any non-plant collider within m_growRadius and Plant.HaveRoof on anything overhead, so m_connectRadius must be larger than m_growRadius and an overhanging root may block growth. CanDrain is level > cost, so the cost must stay below m_maxLevel (C# default 100). Roots regenerate (C# default 1 per second), so a one-off drain is cheap. CanDrain reads a possibly stale ZDO copy on non-owners. Plant.SUpdate calls Grow on every pass once the grow time is over, so the prefix must be cheap and drain only a Healthy sapling. PlantEverything adds a Piece to the vanilla pickable prefabs and replaces Player.RemovePiece while the cultivator is held: the Remove button deletes any flora it manages, wild bushes and forage included (fruit harvested first, no refund by default). So 'has a Piece' cannot tell wild from planted, the Remove button is taken, and its produce-paid bushes undercut the transplant items. Unverified (prefab): the cultivator table's m_canRemovePieces, the idol requirement on the Cultivator recipe, whether the YggaShoot prefabs carry TreeBase (the wiki calls the Yggdrasil shoot a choppable tree), and which layers the forage colliders use.
- **Related:** Plant "everything"; Easy plant; Ashlands trees; Sap collector on other trees; Deep North revamp; Magic applied to non combat (craft, farming, cooking, sailing, etc,)
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/farming-cooking.md](game/farming-cooking.md)
- **Existing mods:** partial

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [PlantEverything (Advize), partial](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Advize/PlantEverything/) | 1.21.3, 2026-09-25, 1.0 tagged | The cultivator plants every target of this idea (raspberry, blueberry, cloudberry and lingonberry bushes, mushrooms, yellow mushrooms, thistle, dandelion, fiddlehead, smoke puff) directly as pieces, paid with their own produce (default 5 per bush, 5 mushrooms, 5 thistle), with configurable respawn times (240-300 minutes by default). It also adds a 'Ygga Sapling' paid with 1 Sap that grows into YggaShoot1-3. EnforceBiomes is off by default and PlantsRequireShielding is on. It adds a Piece to the vanilla pickable prefabs and takes over Player.RemovePiece while the cultivator is held, so the Remove button already deletes the wild bushes and forage it manages (fruit harvested first; resources only with RecoverResources, off by default). CanRemoveFlora extends removal to decorative flora placed by a player. No tool tiers, no transplant item, no root rule. GPL-3.0 (github.com/AdvizeGH/Advize_ValheimMods), so ideas only. |
  | [RestlessPlant (Restless), partial](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Restless/RestlessPlant/) | 0.2.0, 2026-09-29, 1.0 tagged | Bushes and forage on the cultivator, each costing one of the item it grows (raspberries, blueberries, cloudberries, lingonberries, several mushrooms including smoke puff, thistle, dandelion, fiddlehead), a planting grid, bulk harvest of neighbouring plants and auto-replant of one-shot crops such as carrots. Wild plants stay wild: no transplanting and no tiers. Needs RestlessCore, BepInEx and Jotunn; everyone on a server should run the same minor version. |
  | [Groundwork (sighsorry), related](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/sighsorry/Groundwork/) | 1.1.15, 2026-09-29, 1.0 tagged, AI-generated | YAML-configured pickable planting recipes (cultivation.yml). Its 'Natural Pickable Removal' lets the cultivator permanently remove allowlisted natural pickables (Pickable_Branch, Pickable_Flint, Pickable_Dandelion, Pickable_Mushroom, Pickable_Thistle) with the usual distance, build, ward and ownership checks, but gives nothing back. The closest thing to our uproot action. |
  | [Amazing Nature (Balrond), related](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Balrond/balrond_amazing_nature/) | 1.4.0, 2026-08-21, pre-1.0 | Total overhaul (server and every client, same version) with 33 cultivator plants, new tree saplings and higher-tier tools including BlackMetalCultivator_bal and BlackMetalHoe_bal. The page does not say what the higher-tier cultivator changes. No transplanting. |
  | [ZethProj (Zethin), related](https://www.nexusmods.com/valheim/mods/1242) | 1.7.1, 2023-01-03, pre-1.0 (Nexus; details from search results because the page blocks automated fetches) | Foods plus a Black Metal Cultivator (craftable at the Artisan Table) and a 'Frometal' Cultivator. Nothing in its description suggests new cultivator actions. Its Thunderstore predecessor ZethPack is deprecated (2021). |
  | [PlantEasily (Advize), compatibility](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Advize/PlantEasily/) | 2.2.2, 2026-09-24, 1.0 tagged, client-side | Grid planting only activates when the right-hand item's m_shared.m_name is "$item_cultivator" (its ModUtils.HoldingCultivator); PlantEverything uses the same check for its cultivator removal. Separate tier items with their own names would lose both; tiers stored as quality levels of the vanilla Cultivator keep them. |

- **Inspiration:** Planting wild flora is solved: PlantEverything (and now RestlessPlant) lets the cultivator plant every plant on this list, PlantEverything even a Sap-paid Yggdrasil sapling. But they create new plants from produce, so a wild bush is never worth moving, and nothing is gated by progression. PlantEverything's and Groundwork's cultivators can already delete wild pickables but return no plant. Balrond's and ZethProj's higher-tier cultivators are only better tools. Borrow PlantEverything's per-biome m_tolerateHeat / m_tolerateCold flags and its respawn times for one-shot forage (ideas only, it is GPL), and Groundwork's prefab allowlist with vanilla distance, ward and ownership checks. Ours would differ in four ways. Transplanting moves a finite wild plant, because vegetation never regrows once removed, so scouting the map for good bushes matters. Each biome's flora is gated by the matching cultivator tier. The Yggdrasil sapling ties farming to the Mistlands Ancient Roots (the sap collector's reservoir) instead of a flat cost. Grown plants are vanilla prefabs, so a world survives an uninstall. Keep the tiers on the vanilla Cultivator (quality levels 4-7, above the three bronze levels) so PlantEasily and PlantEverything keep recognising it, and make every transplant sapling an ordinary cultivator piece with a Plant component.

### Hunting

> shows gatherables and animals nearby on the minimap (capped to the discovery radius at 100)

- **Scope:** New · **Feasibility:** easy · **Who needs it:** Client · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** idea
- **Approach:** A client tracker runs every 0.5-1 s. It collects loaded non-player characters and available pickables within min(config radius, m_exploreRadius) and keeps one pooled non-saved PinData per object, updating m_pos. The icon is replaced with the item icon (ItemData.GetIcon) or the creature's trophy icon. Pins are removed when out of range or picked. Optionally gate behind a Hunting SE, trinket or skill.
- **Hooks:** `Minimap.AddPin`, `Minimap.RemovePin`, `Minimap.m_exploreRadius`, `Character.GetAllCharacters`, `Pickable.Awake`, `Pickable.CanBePicked`, `PickableItem`, `CharacterDrop`
- **Risks:** Pin churn and performance in dense areas: pool pins and cap counts. Only loaded objects are visible. Pin-manager mods (Pinnacle, AutoMapPins) may treat these pins oddly: use save:false and a distinct type. Offer a server-synced disable flag for 'cheat' concerns.
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/farming-cooking.md](game/farming-cooking.md)
- **Existing mods:** full

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [OneMapToRuleThemAll (DrummerCraig)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/DrummerCraig/OneMapToRuleThemAll/) | 2.8.1, 2026-09-12, 1.0 tagged, AI-generated | Shared map plus a client radar. Nearby objects and creatures show as temporary pins that fade, and creatures are tracked live. |
  | [Hunting (blacks7ar)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/blacks7ar/Hunting/) | 1.4.5, 2026-09-14, 1.0 tagged | Hunting skill (XP from bow, knife and spear) with a configurable auto-track radius for prey, and yield scaling. The closest to the progression framing. |
  | [CreatureTrackerMinimap (Fusionette)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/ChrisBomarGamingMods/CreatureTrackerMinimap/) | 0.9.10, 2025-09-20, pre-1.0 | All creatures on the minimap using their trophy sprite as the icon, with a drop-icon fallback. Open source. |
  | [UnderTheRadar (Kits_Bitz)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Kits_Bitz/UnderTheRadar/) | 3.1.7, 2025-03-07, deprecated | Former reference: live pins for pickables, ores, critters and locations within a radius. |

- **Inspiration:** The radar/ESP exists. Earned hunter craft does not. Plan: the radius grows with a Hunting skill or a trinket up to Minimap.m_exploreRadius (100). Active only while crouched, or with a horn or tracking potion. Separate toggles for animals and pickables. Fading, non-saved pins (Minimap.AddPin with save=false), trophy icons. Throttle scans to about 1 Hz over Character.GetAllCharacters and a cached list of Pickable objects. Client-only.

### Better fishing

> Current mechanic is: throw bait, wait for the hook to start reeling (< this is ok) then reel in using stamina, if the fish struggles it's using more stamina (< this is bad gameplay) New mechanic proposal for the reeling in:  When the fish is not struggling, stardew valley like minigame (reel in to go up, release to go down). Keeping the fish in the bar reels it in automatically with no stamina cost. Having the fish outside of the bar does not reel in and consumes stamina.  When the fish is struggling, the stardew like mini game goes away and you need to orient your character so that the fishing rod points at the opposite direction of the fish and reel in. This consumes stamina, but reeling in with the wrong orientation consumes A LOT of stamina.  Example: if the fish is struggling away to my right, I need to orient myself so that the rod points left and start reeling in. If the fish changes direction and now is on my left, I need to re orient to point my rod to the right. Once the fish stops struggling, the mini game is back.

- **Scope:** New · **Feasibility:** medium · **Who needs it:** Both · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** [Fishing Fight 0.1.0](../src/Farming/Fishing.Fight) (in development)
- **Approach:** Keep the vanilla cast, bait, bite and hook. Once hooked, a FishingFloat.FixedUpdate prefix runs the whole tick: a calm phase with a Stardew Valley-style catch bar (hold Block to raise the zone; fish in the zone = line in with no UseStamina call, out = vanilla reel cost and no line) and struggle phases owned by the mod (not vanilla Fish.Escape), where Fish.SwimDirection is taken over so the hooked fish runs across the line to one side. The rod direction is the body facing (it follows the camera while blocking): at least 30 degrees off the line on the side away from the run brings line in at half speed, the wrong side costs x4 stamina and holds the line, not reeling lets the fish take line. Success and losses call the vanilla paths (Catch, SetCatch(null), OnHooked(null), destroy). Needed everywhere (gameplay rule): server rules and join check as Swim Dive. Built as Fishing Fight.
- **Hooks:** `FishingFloat.FixedUpdate`, `Fish.CustomFixedUpdate`, `Fish.SwimDirection`, `Fish.RandomizeWaypoint`, `Fish.GetStaminaUse`, `FishingFloat.Catch`, `Hud.Update`, `Skills.SkillType.Fishing`
- **Risks:** Several mods take over the same FixedUpdate (GrindstoneSkills, Hooked, Trolling Fishing, PeasFishing, ChillHook, ChillFishing): stand aside (LocalBlocker) and stay out when an earlier prefix skipped the tick. Stamina mods (EpicLoot, FeastMaster, Reely Good Rod) only stack when the costs come from the float and fish fields inside the tick. Turning the rod away from the fish turns the camera too, so the struggle angle must keep the float in view. Fish prefab values (turn rate, acceleration) are unknown, so the struggle swim is the mod's own.
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/farming-cooking.md](game/farming-cooking.md)
- **Existing mods:** partial

  | [Reely SpecTackleLure (Neobotics)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Neobotics/Reely_SpecTackleLure/) | 1.0.0, 2026-09-11, 1.0 tagged | Gear: craftable and upgradable rods, bait, field skinning, deep-sea danger. No minigame. |
  | [FishTrap (Qmds)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Qmds/FishTrap/) | 1.1.4, 2026-09-27, updated post-1.0 | Passive baited traps in four tiers. |
  | [TheFisher (Marlthon)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Marlthon/TheFisher/) | 0.3.7, 2026-09-12, 1.0 tagged | More than 30 new fish and aquariums (content). |
  | [Hooked (Azumatt)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Azumatt/Hooked/) | 1.1.1, 2026-09-14, deprecated on Thunderstore | A Stardew clone that owns the hooked fight: bar grows with skill, five fish motion patterns, treasure, perfect catch, trophy baits as tackle. Hold Block. Closed source. |
  | [GrindstoneSkills (MilkyTeam)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/MilkyTeam/GrindstoneSkills/) | 0.12.0, 2026-10-01, 1.0 | Skills overhaul with a line-tension fight: reeling a thrashing fish builds tension, the fish tires after a few thrashes, grace at 0 stamina from skill 75. Patches FixedUpdate, TryToHook, RPC_Nibble and Fish.Escape; config switch [50 - Fishing] Fishing Enabled. Server-synced, open source. |
  | [ChillHook (Andejx)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Andejx/ChillHook/) | 1.0.3, 2026-09-30, AI-assisted | A circle minigame: steer a net zone onto the fish with the mouse or left stick (a 2D take on steering against the fish). Client-only, closed source. |
  | [ComfyFishing (Loxley)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Loxley/ComfyFishing/) | 0.1.0, 2026-09-15, needs Jotunn | Auto-reel with no stamina cost and rod tiers; by default only its own better rods get the auto-reel. Open source. |
  | [ChillFishing (Lenson)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Lenson/ChillFishing/) | 1.2.2, 2026-09-30 | Fake nibbles, real bites pull the float under, a tension penalty for holding too long. Closed source. |
  | [Angler's Eye (Jumpingmushroom)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Jumpingmushroom/AnglersEye/) | 0.1.0, 2026-09-29 | A helper HUD: REEL or WAIT while the fish is calm or struggling, a bite cue, a catch forecast, opt-in smart reel. Turns its features off next to fight mods. Open source. |
  | [Trolling Fishing (sighsorry)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/sighsorry/Trolling_Fishing/) | 1.1.3, 2026-09-14 | Several floats per player; patches FixedUpdate, SetCatch, UseStamina and RaiseSkill. |
  | [Reely Good Rod (VentureValheim)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/VentureValheim/Reely_Good_Rod/) | 1.0.0, 2026-09-10 | Halves the fish stamina cost (Fish.GetStaminaUse postfix). |

- **Inspiration:** Several fight replacements now exist: Stardew clones (Hooked, PeasFishing), a tension fight (GrindstoneSkills), a 2D steering circle (ChillHook) and auto-reels (ComfyFishing). None makes the player turn the rod against the fish's run in the world: real side pressure (rod low, toward the side opposite the run) is the Valheim-native part. Combine it with a Stardew bar for the calm phases, as the sheet now asks. Pair with passive traps and gear for progression.

### Bring tamed / pets through stone portal

- **Scope:** New · **Feasibility:** medium · **Who needs it:** Client · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** idea
- **Approach:** When TeleportWorld.Teleport starts a teleport through portal_stone, collect tamed characters within about 10 m that follow the local player, claim their ZDOs and set their ZDO positions to the destination. When Player.UpdateTeleport reports arrival (m_teleporting true to false), find the instances, place them at the player plus an offset snapped to solid height, zero velocity and re-issue follow. Retry briefly if they have not spawned yet.
- **Hooks:** `TeleportWorld.Teleport`, `Player.TeleportTo`, `Player.UpdateTeleport`, `Character.GetAllCharacters`, `MonsterAI.GetFollowTarget`, `ZNetView.ClaimOwnership`, `ZDO.SetPosition`, `ZNetScene.FindInstance`, `ZoneSystem.GetSolidHeight`
- **Risks:** Creatures can fall through terrain that is not loaded yet, so always re-place after arrival. Ownership races if another peer owned the tame. Riders must dismount first. Summons may unsummon by distance. Other teleport-everything mods patch the same method. Offer a synced server toggle.
- **Related:** Bring tamed / pets on ship; Breeding revamp
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/farming-cooking.md](game/farming-cooking.md)
- **Existing mods:** full

  | Mod | Status | Notes |

  | [TeleportEverything (OdinPlus)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/OdinPlus/TeleportEverything/) | 2.9.1, 2026-02-08, pre-1.0 | The original popular mod: allies, ores, carts, enemies. |
  | [ServersideQoL TameAssist (ArgusMagnus)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/ArgusMagnus/ServersideQoL_TameAssist/) | 2.1.0, 2026-09-25, server-only | All tames follow you, even through portals and into dungeons. Vanilla clients OK. |
  | [AdjustablePortals (MidnightMods)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/MidnightMods/AdjustablePortals/) | 0.4.0, 2026-09-12, 1.0 tagged | Tames that follow you travel with you. Portal progression config. |

- **Inspiration:** Fully covered, including server-only. Vanilla has no tame teleport logic (Teleport.Interact, Player.TeleportTo). Build it only if our tame suite needs integration (for example ship passengers plus portals). Respect the vanilla no-ore rule.

### Sap collector on other trees

> Get new cooking / crafting item

- **Scope:** New · **Feasibility:** medium · **Who needs it:** Both · **Custom assets:** yes · **Status:** idea
- **Approach:** Clone piece_sapcollector into a tree tap. Replace the root requirement with an UpdatePlacementGhost rule requiring a TreeBase within 0.3 m. Use a custom TreeTap component copying the s_lastTime/s_product/s_level accumulation and mapping tree prefab to output item (birch syrup, resin, oak tannin, ember sap). Do not attach ResourceRoot to every tree.
- **Hooks:** `ZNetScene.Awake`, `ObjectDB.Awake`, `Piece.m_mustConnectTo`, `Player.UpdatePlacementGhost`, `SapCollector.UpdateTick`, `SapCollector.RPC_Extract`, `ResourceRoot`, `TreeBase`
- **Risks:** New items need icons. Uninstalling leaves unknown prefabs. Chopping the tree breaks the connection. Balance against vanilla Sap. Adding ResourceRoot to all trees would add thousands of timers.
- **Related:** Ashlands trees; Cooking equipment
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/farming-cooking.md](game/farming-cooking.md)
- **Existing mods:** partial

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [ResinCollector (blacks7ar)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/blacks7ar/ResinCollector/) | 1.1.7, 2026-09-15, 1.0 tagged | A build piece that works only on trees (Meadows, Black Forest, Plains) and collects resin. Configurable and server-synced. Proves it is feasible. |
  | [BetterSapCollector (xtavim)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/xtavim/BetterSapCollector/) | 1.1.0, 2026-09-09, 1.0 tagged | Tunes the vanilla sap collector and ancient-root speeds and capacities. Open source. |

- **Inspiration:** The vanilla SapCollector needs a ResourceRoot parent (level and regen in the ZDO), and only Mistlands Ancient Roots have one. Plan: a ResourceRoot-like component or a 'Tap' piece that finds a TreeBase, with a new output per species. Examples: birch syrup (cooking), pine pitch (tar alternative), oak tannin (leather), Yggdrasil or Ashwood reagents (magic). Add a drain cost so it is not free. A cross-category chain: gather -> cook -> craft.

### Bring tamed / pets on ship

- **Scope:** New · **Feasibility:** hard · **Who needs it:** Both · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** idea
- **Approach:** While a tame stands on a moving ship, force a deck state: no wandering, StopMoving, attach active, and a ship-local patrol point. The ship owner claims ownership of onboard tames, so both simulate on one client and use the velocity-correction branch. Enable m_characterParentSync on tameable prefabs so remote clients render them relative to the deck. Optionally add a 'board' command that places a following tame on the deck.
- **Hooks:** `Character.GetStandingOnShip`, `Character.ApplyGroundForce`, `BaseAI.IdleMovement`, `BaseAI.Follow`, `BaseAI.SetPatrolPoint`, `BaseAI.GetPatrolPoint`, `Ship.CustomFixedUpdate`, `Ship.UpdateOwner`, `ZSyncTransform.m_characterParentSync`, `Tameable.RPC_Command`, `ZNetView.ClaimOwnership`
- **Risks:** Ownership churn with ZDOMan.ReleaseNearbyZDOS. Physics instability with heavy creatures pushing ships. Pathfinding on decks. Conflicts with ValheimRAFT-style moving-base mods and AI overhauls.
- **Related:** Harpoon to work on tamed animals; Bring tamed / pets through stone portal
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/farming-cooking.md](game/farming-cooking.md)
- **Existing mods:** partial

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [CreatureCarry (Wendigo)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Wendigo/CreatureCarry/) | 1.3.0, 2026-03-21, pre-1.0, AI-generated | Pick up and carry tames. Workaround. |
  | [Tameable Collector (KG)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/KGvalheim/Tameable_Collector/) | 1.1.0, 2025-10-27, deprecated | Store a tame in an item, keeping saddle, level and HP. The 'Pokeball' workaround. |
  | [ValheimRAFT (zolantris)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/zolantris/ValheimRAFT/) | 5.0.7, 2026-09-25, 1.0 tagged | Buildable vehicles. Pens can be built on deck. Open source. |

- **Inspiration:** No mod makes tames real ship passengers. Plan: a tame inside a ship volume (Character.InNumShipVolumes / GetStandingOnShip) gets 'boarded'. It is parented to the ship, AI locomotion and targeting are paused, it plays a sit animation, and it cannot fall or drown. It disembarks on command or at landfall. Capacity per ship type. The hard part is network ownership: the ship owner vs the creature owner. Prototype early.

## Cooking

### Unlock biome feast in the biome not after

- **Scope:** QoL · **Feasibility:** easy · **Who needs it:** Depends · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** idea
- **Approach:** First dump every Feast* recipe (station, level, ingredients) to see what gates each feast. Then apply a config table feast -> {biome, station level, ingredient overrides} to the Recipe objects in an ObjectDB.Awake postfix. Optionally, an AddKnownBiome postfix marks the mapped feast recipe known on first entry to its biome.
- **Hooks:** `ObjectDB.Awake`, `ObjectDB.CopyOtherDB`, `Recipe.m_minStationLevel`, `Recipe.m_resources`, `Player.AddKnownBiome`, `Player.UpdateKnownRecipesList`, `Player.HaveRequirements`, `Player.AddKnownRecipe`
- **Risks:** Crafting is only checked on the client, so the mod works client-only, but ship a synced config for fairness; mismatched configs only change the recipe lists players see. Load-order fights with other recipe-editing mods (apply late, keyed by prefab name). Known recipes persist after uninstall.
- **Related:** Cooking equipment
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/farming-cooking.md](game/farming-cooking.md)
- **Existing mods:** none

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [TradersExtended (shudnal)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/shudnal/TradersExtended/) | 2.0.4, 2026-09-24, 1.0 tagged | Generic trader-list rewrite with global-key, player-key and discovery requirements. It can approximate the idea by re-keying each spice in config. Open source. |

- **Inspiration:** No dedicated mod. The Bog Witch's spices are gated by Trader.TradeItem.m_requiredGlobalKey (boss killed), filtered in Trader.GetAvailableItems. Feast recipes unlock once every ingredient is a known material (Player.UpdateKnownRecipesList / RequirementMode.IsKnown). Plan: patch GetAvailableItems so the spice for biome X appears once the local player has discovered biome X (Player.IsBiomeKnown(BiomeSector), m_knownBiome). This is per-player, and the spice-to-biome map lives in config. Coexist with TradersExtended.

### Cooking equipment

- **Scope:** New · **Feasibility:** hard · **Who needs it:** Both · **Custom assets:** yes · **Status:** idea
- **Approach:** Phase 1 (medium on its own): new stations cloned from piece_cookingstation or piece_oven with new conversions and items (griddle, drying rack, spit-roast), plus new cauldron StationExtensions. Phase 2: wearable cook's gear whose SE flags are read in OnInteract (bonus yield), UpdateCooking (burn protection, speed; the adder's gear is stored in a slot ZDO key at RPC_AddItem) and DoCrafting (cauldron bonus).
- **Hooks:** `CookingStation.m_conversion`, `CookingStation.UpdateCooking`, `CookingStation.GetDeltaTime`, `CookingStation.OnInteract`, `CookingStation.RPC_AddItem`, `StationExtension`, `CraftingStation.GetLevel`, `InventoryGui.DoCrafting`, `Player.EatFood`, `SE_Stats`
- **Risks:** Needs models, icons and localization. Speed effects must be stored in the ZDO because the station owner may be another player. Many new prefabs make uninstall messy. Content packs may add competing stations.
- **Related:** Unlock biome feast in the biome not after; Sap collector on other trees
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/farming-cooking.md](game/farming-cooking.md)
- **Existing mods:** partial
