- **Inspiration:** Stations and recipes are crowded. Functional gear that changes how you cook is thin. Examples: apron (slower overcook on CookingStation), oven mitts, cleaver (more meat), a spice pouch trinket (the next meal lasts longer). Add vanilla StationExtension add-ons (spice rack, smoking rack) for the cauldron and prep table that unlock recipe tiers. Reuse the vanilla Cooking skill.

## Building

### Torches ON/OFF only, no fuel

- **Scope:** QoL · **Feasibility:** easy · **Who needs it:** Both · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** [Switchable Lights 0.1.0](../src/Building/Lights.Switchable) (in development)
- **Approach:** For whitelisted Fireplace prefabs (torches, sconces, braziers), set m_infiniteFuel=true, m_canTurnOff=true and m_canRefill=false at ZNetScene.Awake. A prefix on Interact allows toggling at 0 fuel, and a postfix on GetHoverText adds on/off text. Toggling uses the vanilla s_state; also top up s_fuel so pieces stay lit for a while after uninstall.
- **Hooks:** `ZNetScene.Awake`, `Fireplace.Interact`, `Fireplace.GetHoverText`, `Fireplace.UpdateState`, `Fireplace.UpdateFireplace`, `Fireplace.SetFuel`
- **Risks:** A vanilla ZDO owner still burns fuel and vanilla viewers see torches go out. Pieces with wet Low/High visuals auto-turn off in rain once m_canTurnOff is true. Overlaps with V+ and other infinite-torch mods. Some 1.0 pieces already use these flags (runtime dump needed).
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/building-crafting.md](game/building-crafting.md)
- **Existing mods:** full

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [ValheimInfiniteFire (MidnightMods)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/MidnightMods/ValheimInfiniteFire/) | 1.4.0, 2026-09-21, 1.0 tagged | Infinite fire per category. Placed fires update live. |
  | [TimedTorchesStayLit (TastyChickenLegs)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/TastyChickenLegs/TimedTorchesStayLit/) | 1.4.0, 2026-09-13, 1.0 tagged | Torches on a schedule. Optionally no fuel. |
  | [ServersideQoL PrefabConfigurator (ArgusMagnus)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/ArgusMagnus/ServersideQoL_PrefabConfigurator/) | 2.0.14, 2026-09-20, server-only | Makes light sources toggleable or gives them infinite fuel, for vanilla and console clients. |
  | [HexInfiniteFuel (Hex_Viking)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Hex_Viking/HexInfiniteFuel/) | 1.0.4, 2026-09-09, 1.0 tagged, AI-generated | Client-side: every Fireplace permanently lit. |

- **Inspiration:** Mostly prefab data. Vanilla Fireplace already has m_infiniteFuel, m_canTurnOff and m_canRefill, plus an on/off state in ZDOVars.s_state that Fireplace.Interact toggles. Our angle is the exact 'light switch' UX: light once to install it, then E toggles with no fuel. Cooking fires and bonfires optionally keep fuel so wood still matters. Bonus: a light group toggled from a switch piece or a ward, which ties into the ward revamp.

### Place sign on chest

- **Scope:** QoL · **Feasibility:** medium · **Who needs it:** Both · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** idea
- **Approach:** A transpiler (or re-validating postfix) in UpdatePlacementGhost skips the m_supports rejection when the ghost has a Sign and the hit piece has a Container. A transpiler in UpdateSupport lets Sign pieces use Container neighbours as support (instead of setting chest m_supports globally, which would allow stacking). Optionally add snappoint children on chest faces. Alternative: a 'chest label' via alt-interact, stored in a custom ZDO string and rendered as a TMP label.
- **Hooks:** `Player.UpdatePlacementGhost`, `WearNTear.UpdateSupport`, `ZNetScene.Awake`, `Sign.SetText`, `Container.Interact`
- **Risks:** A vanilla owner computes zero support and breaks the sign. Transpiler conflicts with build mods (Gizmo, PlanBuild, build cameras). On uninstall, signs on chests collapse. Removing the chest drops the sign.
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/building-crafting.md](game/building-crafting.md)
- **Existing mods:** partial

  | [ServersideQoL ContainerSigns (ArgusMagnus)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/ArgusMagnus/ServersideQoL_ContainerSigns/) | 2.1.0, 2026-09-25, server-only | The server auto-attaches vanilla signs to chests and barrels, optionally with a content list. The signs also configure the other SQoL modules. |
  | [RunicStorage (Chazman)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Chazman/RunicStorage/) | 1.3.12, 2026-09-28, updated post-1.0, AI-generated | Colored labels on chest faces and lids, plus a large storage suite. |
  | [ZenSign (ZenDragon)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/ZenDragon/ZenSign/) | 1.9.1, 2026-09-23, 1.0 tagged | A sign shows an item icon and the total in nearby chests. Tap it to highlight those chests. |

- **Inspiration:** Labeling is covered. The building QoL of placing an ordinary sign flush on a chest or barrel face is not. Plan: a placement tweak that, when the sign ghost's ray hits a Container collider, snaps the sign to that face (normal, center, offset). The sign stays an independent piece, so it is safe for multiplayer and uninstall. Later: Chest Labels-style auto-sized text and ZenSign-style item icons. Test round barrels and all chest sizes.

### Ward revamp

- **Scope:** New · **Feasibility:** hard · **Who needs it:** Both · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** idea
- **Approach:** Extend the ward ZDO with custom keys (tier/radius, protection flags for crops, stations, tames, doors, PvP and raids, member roles) while keeping pu_id/pu_name. Centralize rules in one WardRules.Allowed(action, point, player) called from CheckAccess and from the currently unchecked call sites. Block damage in an owner-side RPC_Damage prefix. Implement the unused Group chest privacy as ward members. Add an alt-interact management UI.
- **Hooks:** `PrivateArea.CheckAccess`, `PrivateArea.HaveLocalAccess`, `PrivateArea.Interact`, `PrivateArea.GetHoverText`, `PrivateArea.RPC_TogglePermitted`, `WearNTear.RPC_Damage`, `Pickable.Interact`, `Switch.Interact`, `Fireplace.Interact`, `Container.CheckAccess`, `ShieldGenerator`
- **Risks:** Conflicts with WardIsLove and V+ ward settings. Enforcement is purely client-side, so require the mod via a version check. CheckAccess is called every frame from hover text, so cache it. Interplay with raid events.
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/building-crafting.md](game/building-crafting.md)
- **Existing mods:** partial

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [ProtectiveWards (shudnal)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/shudnal/ProtectiveWards/) | 2.0.15, 2026-09-21, 1.0 tagged | Per-ward access (password, guild, networks), protected interactions, passive repair, protection for plants, tames and ships, multipliers, offerings. Open source. |

  | [BetterWards (Azumatt)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Azumatt/BetterWards/) | 1.9.5, 2025-03-06, deprecated | WardIsLove's predecessor. |

- **Inspiration:** Existing mods are admin and PvE protection toolkits. A gameplay revamp is open: ward tiers through StationExtension, upkeep in surtling cores or eitr, base auras (processing speed as in ProtectiveWards' multipliers), and ward-scoped automation (light toggling, repair, tame safety). Vanilla PrivateArea has m_radius 10, a permitted list and CheckAccess. The user's intent is unspecified, so clarify first.

### Magic applied to non combat (craft, farming, cooking, sailing, etc,)

- **Scope:** New · **Feasibility:** very-hard · **Who needs it:** Depends · **Custom assets:** yes · **Status:** idea
- **Approach:** A shared 'blessing' framework: staffs, meads or runes grant SEs that either modify skills (craft bonus/speed, harvest radius, fishing; no patch needed) or set custom StatusAttribute bits synced via s_seAttrib. Owners of plants, stations and ships poll nearby players for those bits (cached) and apply multipliers. Station-bound runes write ZDO flags on nearby stations.
- **Hooks:** `SE_Stats`, `SEMan.ModifySkillLevel`, `SEMan.HaveStatusAttribute`, `StatusEffect.m_attributes`, `Plant.GetGrowTime`, `CookingStation.UpdateCooking`, `Smelter.UpdateSmelter`, `Ship.GetSailForce`, `InventoryGui.DoCrafting`, `EffectArea`
- **Risks:** Effects on world objects must run on the object's owner, so everyone needs the mod. Cost of range scans (cache them). Bit collisions with other mods. Balance. Scope: build it as a library reused by several category mods.
- **Related:** Enchanting; Gemstone revamp
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/building-crafting.md](game/building-crafting.md)
- **Existing mods:** partial

Casts (terrain), augments, and engravings on standing stones: weather canopy, calm waters, dry land, repair buildings and ships, extinguish. |
  | [MagicRevamp (blacks7ar)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/blacks7ar/MagicRevamp/) | 1.5.1, 2026-09-20, 1.0 tagged | 96 spells, staves, wands, a spellbook. Mostly combat. |
  | [MagicPlugin (blacks7ar)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/blacks7ar/MagicPlugin/) | 2.2.2, 2026-09-24, 1.0 tagged | Combat magic weapons and gear. |

- **Inspiration:** Rune Magic proves the appetite but uses a parallel economy. Plan: utility spells powered by vanilla eitr and the Blood/Elemental magic skills (or a new Seidr skill). Candidates: Growth (Plant speed-up), Kindle (faster smelting), Preserve (food buffs), Tailwind, Soothe (taming), Mend (area repair). Add persistent engravings or totems fed by eitr or sap. Build it as a shared magic core for the Farming, Cooking and Building mods.

## Crafting

### One click repair all

- **Scope:** QoL · **Feasibility:** trivial · **Who needs it:** Client · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** [One Click Repair All 0.1.0](../src/Crafting/Repair.OneClickAll) (in development)
- **Approach:** A postfix on OnRepairPressed loops the vanilla RepairOneItem while HaveRepairableItems is true (safety cap) and shows one summary message. Optional hotkey and optional 'any station in range' extension of CanRepair.
- **Hooks:** `InventoryGui.OnRepairPressed`, `InventoryGui.RepairOneItem`, `InventoryGui.HaveRepairableItems`, `InventoryGui.CanRepair`, `InventoryGui.Update`
- **Risks:** Minimal. Must coexist with repair-cost mods; calling the vanilla method in a loop keeps their patches active. Suppress the per-item message spam. It still repairs only items valid for the current station.
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/building-crafting.md](game/building-crafting.md)
- **Existing mods:** full

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [SmartRepair (LJIndustries / 3WiseStooges)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/LJIndustries/SmartRepair/) | 1.0.1, 2026-09-16, 1.0 tagged | Repairs everything when a station opens. Later stations repair earlier gear (RequireStationLevel toggle). Keeps skill gain. Open source. |
  | [RhythmicRepairs (hoskope)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/hoskope/RhythmicRepairs/) | 1.0.0, 2026-06-15, pre-1.0 | One click repairs everything, paced one item at a time to keep the vanilla sound and effects rhythm. Open source. |
  | [AzuWorkbench Inventory Repair (Azumatt)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Azumatt/AzuWorkbench_Inventory_Repair/) | 1.0.2, 2024-11-05, pre-1.0 (ServerSync break risk) | Auto-repair on interact. |
  | [RepairAll (LoadedGun)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/LoadedGun/RepairAll/) | 1.1.1, 2023-03-01, pre-1.0 | The original 'press once' mod. |

- **Inspiration:** Trivial: vanilla InventoryGui.OnRepairPressed -> RepairOneItem repairs one worn item per click, filtered by CanRepair and CraftingStation.CheckUsable. Loop it. Borrow RhythmicRepairs' paced effects, show one summary message, keep old behavior on Shift+click, keep skill gain. Leave station-tier crossover (SmartRepair) off by default. Only worth it inside a Crafting QoL bundle.

### Shift + E to feed 5 items to furnaces and kilns

- **Scope:** QoL · **Feasibility:** easy · **Who needs it:** Client · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** [Batch Station Feeding 0.1.0](../src/Crafting/Stations.BatchFeed) (in development)
- **Approach:** A prefix on Switch.Interact detects alt (AltPlace = Shift) on a Smelter's add-ore or add-fuel switch. Compute n = min(5, m_maxOre - GetQueueSize(), inventory count of the FindCookableItem type) (fuel: m_maxFuel - ceil(GetFuel())), then n times remove 1 item and InvokeRPC RPC_AddOre / RPC_AddFuel. Show one summary message and add a hover hint.
- **Hooks:** `Switch.Interact`, `Smelter.OnAddOre`, `Smelter.OnAddFuel`, `Smelter.OnHoverAddOre`, `Smelter.OnHoverAddFuel`, `CookingStation.OnAddFuelSwitch`
- **Risks:** Non-owners read a stale queue/fuel count and the owner handlers don't cap, so simultaneous feeding can overfill (a vanilla race too); track recently sent counts. Low conflict with auto-fuel and V+ since limits are read live.
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/building-crafting.md](game/building-crafting.md)
- **Existing mods:** full

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [BulkSmelt (ChillCodeClub)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/ChillCodeClub/BulkSmelt/) | 3.0.4, 2026-09-12, updated post-1.0, AI-generated | Shift+E fills to capacity: kiln, smelter, blast furnace, windmill, spinning wheel. |
  | [ComfyAddAllFuel (ComfyMods)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/ComfyMods/ComfyAddAllFuel/) | 1.10.0, 2024-11-12, pre-1.0 | Shift adds a stack or fills to the limit. One unified interaction patch covers smelters, kilns, fireplaces, cooking stations and shield generators. Clean open source. |
  | [AutomaticFuel (TastyChickenLegs)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/TastyChickenLegs/AutomaticFuel/) | 1.5.2, 2026-09-27, 1.0 tagged | Stations pull fuel and ore from nearby containers automatically. |
  | [Add All Fuel And Ore (rin_jugatla)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/rin_jugatla/Add_All_Fuel_And_Ore_For_Smelter_Charcoalkiln_etc/) | 1.6.2, 2021-03-07, pre-1.0 | The original. Can pull from containers. |

- **Inspiration:** The difference is a deliberate fixed batch (N=5, configurable) rather than 'fill all', which keeps the pacing. Vanilla Smelter.OnAddOre / OnAddFuel add one item and send one RPC. Call that path N times, clamped by capacity and inventory, so it works on vanilla servers. Cover smelter, kiln, blast furnace, eitr refinery, windmill, spinning wheel and shield generator. Show feedback like '+5 (12/20)'.

### Forge of potential revamp

> Only consumes the idol on failure, does not destroy the item. Need higher tier idol when high level enough. Might require skill level to be able to craft, Upgrade idols with trophies to inrease odds (low trophy brings it up to 50%, elite to 70, zone boss 95)

- **Scope:** QoL · **Feasibility:** medium · **Who needs it:** Client · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** [Forge Idol Upgrades 0.1.0](../src/Crafting/Forge.IdolUpgrades) (in development)
- **Approach:** A prefix on InventoryGui.DoCrafting takes over the Forge branch (station.m_upgrader). It rolls our own chance for the idol actually spent: a configurable base for a plain idol, or 50%, 70% or 95% for an idol infused with a common, elite or zone-boss trophy (the trophy sets the chance, it does not add to it). On success it raises m_quality in place, which keeps the crafter, world level and m_customData. Durability must be set on purpose: a higher quality has a higher max durability, and vanilla re-creates the item at full durability. Keep vanilla's unequip so hand, back and shoulder visuals refresh. On failure it removes only that idol (Inventory.RemoveItem by name and quality) and leaves the item untouched. The infusion is stored as the idol's own m_quality (1 plain, 2 common, 3 elite, 4 boss). This vanilla field is saved everywhere, and Inventory.FindFreeStackItem already stacks only equal qualities. Raising the idols' SharedData.m_maxQuality to 4 in ObjectDB.Awake/CopyOtherDB also keeps levels apart on drag and drop (ItemData.IsSameType, InventoryGrid.DropItem) and shows the level on the slot. Infusing happens in the Upgrade tab of a normal station: one recipe per idol prefab with m_noCraftOnlyUpgrade and m_requireOnlyOneIngredient, whose trophy requirements are banded per step by a Piece.Requirement.GetAmount postfix. Our own DoCrafting branch runs these recipes, because the vanilla upgrade path removes the whole idol stack and gives back one item. Trophy classes are built at ZNetScene.Awake: boss = trophies in the CharacterDrop of prefabs with Character.m_boss; elite = a config list; common = every other trophy. The Deep North boss (Kall Fimbulbringer) has no trophy, so the Bloodgold boss step needs a configured item such as Sacrificial Blood (FrozenKingDrop). A biome-tier gate stops a cheap trophy from lifting a high-tier idol. Keep the higher-tier idol bands at high levels and the Crafting skill gate in UpdateRecipe and DoCrafting.
- **Hooks:** `InventoryGui.DoCrafting`, `InventoryGui.OnCraftPressed`, `InventoryGui.UpdateRecipe`, `InventoryGui.UpdateRecipeList`, `Piece.Requirement.GetAmount`, `Player.HaveRequirementItems`, `Player.GetFirstRequiredItem`, `ObjectDB.Awake`, `ObjectDB.CopyOtherDB`, `ZNetScene.Awake`, `Character.IsBoss`, `ItemDrop.ItemData.GetTooltip`
- **Risks:** The sheet's ladder only makes sense if a plain idol rolls below 50%, which is lower than vanilla's 65%. That is fair only because a failure no longer costs the item, so make it configurable and flag it. What a vanilla failure does is disputed (the wiki and Forge No Destroy: the item is always destroyed; OdinBet and Forge of Certainty: usually a level is lost), so dump the idol prefabs. Without a biome gate, a Boar trophy or a re-summoned Eikthyr would lift a Bloodgold idol. 'Elite' has no vanilla flag, so it needs a config list. The vanilla only-one-ingredient helpers mishandle a 0-amount requirement: Player.HaveRequirementItems returns true, and Player.GetFirstRequiredItem returns a null item that Recipe.GetAmount dereferences in both OnCraftPressed and DoCrafting. Recipe.GetRequiredStationLevel (max(1, m_minStationLevel) + q - 1) needs a level-4 station for the boss step. Raising the idols' m_maxQuality also puts other mods' idol recipes (ReforgedPotential, OdinBet, JGFoP, DowngradeableIdols) in their station's Upgrade tab, where the vanilla upgrade path eats the whole stack. Where idols keep m_maxQuality 1 (vanilla players, or after an uninstall), the vanilla Forge counts only quality-1 idols, so infused idols are refused until they are dragged onto a plain stack, and plain idols dragged onto an infused stack become infused. ReforgedPotential reports greyed-out icons with dynamic requirements. Many Forge mods patch DoCrafting or the idol data (EpicLoot, ReforgedPotential, OdinBet, JGFoP, Wire's, the IL patch in ForgeOfPotentialSafe, Potential Forge NoFail, Potential Draught, Forge No Destroy, G3A3), so keep the prefix narrow and declare them incompatible. Our framework has no config sync yet. Fix the vanilla $msg_upgrader_failed key.
- **Related:** Gemstone revamp; Enchanting; One trinket per trophy; Trophy on mobs without trophy; Boss summon revamp
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/building-crafting.md](game/building-crafting.md)
- **Existing mods:** partial

Break Chance decides whether a failed item breaks or loses one level. Craftable idols with configurable recipes (Artisan table by default), configurable idol cost scaling and upgrade duration. Boss progression, and an idol progression that changes the required idol at level thresholds (our 'higher tier idol' part). No trophy odds and no skill gate. Known bug: dynamic requirements grey out icons in the upgrade list. EpicLoot compatible. |
  | [OdinBet ForgeOfPotential (Igao)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Igao/OdinBet_ForgeOfPotential/) | v1.4.2, updated 2026-09-28, BepInEx only, requires Valheim 1.0 (1.0: yes) | Gambling framing, 100% success by default. A failure can lose levels, destroy the item with a partial refund, or reset it to level 1. With LevelsLostOnFailure = 0 the item keeps its level and only the ingredients are spent, which covers our 'item survives' part as a setting. All 16 idols are craftable, each tier with its biome boss trophy; the Bloodgold tier uses Sacrificial Blood (FrozenKingDrop, from Kall). Trophies make idols but do not change the odds. Idols are craftable at the standard Forge with one boss trophy per tier (Sacrificial Blood for Bloodgold). Server-synced; install on the server and every client. |

Failure chance drops from 80% to 30%, idol cost drops by up to 25%, and the penalty goes from destroy to losing two levels, then one (the JSON config can set None). Failed attempts still use the idols. The host and every player need it. Closest to 'trophies raise the odds', but per station, not per idol. |

The numbers are fixed. It swaps the idol chance only around the crafting call. It also caps normal Forge refinement at level 25 by default (server setting). Required on the server and every client. |
  | [Forge No Destroy (Nexus 3781, whitebarbie)](https://www.nexusmods.com/valheim/mods/3781) | v1.0.1, updated 2026-09-16, Valheim 1.0 (1.0: yes) | Keeps vanilla odds, but a failure drops one level instead of destroying the item: it sets the idols' m_breakChance negative. Its page says vanilla failures destroy the item. Install on host and clients. Alternative to the same author's Potential Forge NoFail. |
  | [Potential Forge NoFail (Nexus 3751, whitebarbie)](https://www.nexusmods.com/valheim/mods/3751) | v1, updated 2026-09-14, Valheim 1.0 (1.0: yes) | Every refinement succeeds; idols are still consumed. Harmony only; its page says it conflicts with mods that touch InventoryGui.DoCrafting. Install on host and clients. |
  | [Wire's Forge of Potential Rework (Wire)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Wire/WiresForgeOfPotentialRework/) | v1.0.0, updated 2026-09-28, also on Nexus (4079, by Kr0sshair) (1.0: yes) | 90% then 95% success, falling 5 points per level to a 65% floor. A failure loses no level up to +5 (35% of the attempted materials come back), one level at +6 and +7, and is split 50/50 between a one-level loss and a break from +8. A 20% jackpot chain grants up to +5. The idol is always consumed. Shows live odds in the Forge UI. |
  | [Forge of Potential Safe (OutsiderTeam)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/OutsiderTeam/ForgeOfPotentialSafe/) | v1.0.3, updated 2026-09-13, tested on 1.0.12 (1.0: yes) | 100% success and 0% break through an IL patch on InventoryGui.DoCrafting; SuccessChance and BreakChance are configurable. Odin's Blessing (valheim_gigachads, 0.0.1, 2026-09-13, https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/valheim_gigachads/OdinsBlessingForgeOfPotential/) gives 100% success with no options. |
  | [Forge of Certainty (Nexus 3860, Rrostayk)](https://www.nexusmods.com/valheim/mods/3860) | v1, updated 2026-09-19, client-side (1.0: yes) | 100% success by default, with configurable SuccessChance and BreakChance; the idol and materials are still consumed. It gives vanilla odds as 65/25/10 (success, level lost, destroyed), which matches the code defaults but contradicts the wiki's 65% success / 35% destroyed. Verify with a data dump. |
  | [DowngradeableIdols (valheim_gigachads)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/valheim_gigachads/DowngradeableIdols/) | v1.0.0, updated 2026-09-28, no Jotunn (1.0: yes) | Workbench recipes turn one idol into one idol of the tier below, for both families. It shows that idol recipes are a plain ObjectDB job. Source: github.com/lemigu/downgradeable_idols. |
  | [G3A3 (G3A1)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/G3A1/G3A3/) | v1.9.73, updated 2026-09-28, Jotunn 2.30.2 (1.0: yes) | All-in-one pack. Its ForgeOfPotential section changes the upgrade odds, and its difficulty sections (BossIdols, Idols) hand out Forge of Potential idols as a reward. Server-synced; the server and every player need the same version. One more mod that touches the Forge odds. |

- **Inspiration:** Vanilla already spends only the idol at the Forge: Player.ConsumeResources skips non-upgrader requirements there. What a failure really costs is the item, but sources disagree on how. The wiki and Forge No Destroy say every vanilla 1.0 failure destroys it (65% success, 35% destroyed with 35% of the materials back, which needs idol prefabs with m_breakChance of at least 0.35). OdinBet's readme says a normal failure loses a level, and Forge of Certainty quotes 65/25/10, which is what the code defaults give. A data dump settles it. Most mods remove the gamble (ForgeOfPotentialSafe, Odin's Blessing, Potential Forge NoFail, Forge of Certainty, and ReforgedPotential and OdinBet at their 100% defaults) or soften it (Forge No Destroy downgrades instead of destroying; Wire's loses no level up to +5; OdinBet with LevelsLostOnFailure 0 and Forge of Progression with the None penalty keep the item's level, which already covers our first part as a setting). Odds boosters exist, but not per idol: Forge of Progression spends trophies to upgrade the station, and Potential Draught is a timed potion. OdinBet and JGFoP use boss trophies (and Sacrificial Blood for Bloodgold) only as idol ingredients, and nobody infuses idols with trophies. Ours: a failure loses only the idol, a plain idol has a low base chance, and trophies set 50/70/95%. The infusion is stored as idol quality, so there are no new prefabs and the mod stays client-only; after an uninstall, infused idols must be merged onto a plain stack before the vanilla Forge accepts them. Trophies are biome-gated, higher-tier idols are required at high levels, the Crafting skill gate is optional and the chance is shown on the button. Borrow Wire's live odds display, ReforgedPotential's idol-threshold progression and Forge of Progression's biome-tier trophy costs.

### Gemstone revamp

> Find a crafting use for them because right now we have too much gold

- **Scope:** New · **Feasibility:** easy · **Who needs it:** Depends · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** idea

Lower m_value (client-side trader) to curb coin inflation. Keep all numbers in a server-synced config.
- **Hooks:** `ObjectDB.Awake`, `ObjectDB.CopyOtherDB`, `ItemDrop.ItemData.SharedData.m_value`, `StoreGui.SellItem`, `Incinerator.m_conversions`, `InventoryGui.DoCrafting`, `Recipe.m_requireOnlyOneIngredient`
- **Risks:** Economy balance. Recipe-editor mods may override. New gem items or Obliterator conversions require everyone to have the mod (owner-side). Shares the DoCrafting patch with idea 1.

### Barber shop to customize armor

> (shield pattern, armor color glow, etc)

- **Scope:** New · **Feasibility:** medium · **Who needs it:** Depends · **Custom assets:** yes · **Status:** idea
- **Approach:** Phase 1 (client-only): a tailor UI re-selects m_variant on owned shields and capes for a fee; vanilla already syncs left and shoulder variants. Phase 2 (everyone): store tint/glow in m_customData, mirror the equipped values to custom ZDO keys, and apply MaterialPropertyBlock colors on chest, legs and helmet renderers for all clients.
- **Hooks:** `InventoryGui.UpdateRecipe`, `InventoryGui.OnShowVariantSelection`, `VariantDialog.Setup`, `InventoryGui.OnVariantSelected`, `Humanoid.SetupVisEquipment`, `ItemStyle.Setup`, `VisEquipment.UpdateEquipmentVisuals`, `VisEquipment.SetChestEquipped`, `VisEquipment.SetLegEquipped`, `VisEquipment.SetHelmetEquipped`, `Barber`, `PlayerCustomizaton`
- **Risks:** Chest, legs and helmet have no vanilla variant sync. Shader compatibility (_Style is an atlas index) and clashes with MaterialMan highlight overrides. Armor visuals are invisible to vanilla players. Conflicts with armor model replacers. New patterns need textures.

  | [Armoire (Advize)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Advize/Armoire/) | 1.2.2, 2026-09-17, 1.0 tagged | Buildable wardrobe that unlocks appearances and saves 3 outfits. |
  | [Change Style Of Existing Shield (Goldenrevolver)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Goldenrevolver/Change_Style_Of_Existing_Shield/) | 1.1.0, 2023-10-23, pre-1.0 | Use a shield or linen cape on a workbench to change its style variant. Open source. |
  | [balrond BannerColorizer (Balrond)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Balrond/balrond_BannerColorizer/) | 1.0.8, 2026-09-11, 1.0 tagged | Dye station and dyes for build pieces. |

- **Inspiration:** Transmog is covered. Dye and tint channels, emissive glow color, and post-craft shield patterns on 1.0 are missing. Vanilla already has a Barber piece and PlayerCustomizaton.ShowBarberGui (hair and beard). Extend it into a Tailor/Armorer with tabs for tint (dyes), glow (costs eitr or gems) and pattern (swap ItemData.m_variant). Store choices in m_customData and apply them in VisEquipment through MaterialPropertyBlocks so every player sees them. Apply our tint after Transmog's mesh swap.

### Player trophy

- **Scope:** New · **Feasibility:** medium · **Who needs it:** Both · **Custom assets:** yes · **Status:** idea
- **Approach:** A postfix on Player.OnDeath (owner) checks for a PvP kill (m_lastHit.m_hitType == PlayerHit and the attacker is a Player) and spawns a Trophy_Player ItemDrop with m_customData {victim, id, killer, day}. Name and tooltip are patched to show the victim. The data persists in chests and item stands.
- **Hooks:** `Player.OnDeath`, `HitData.GetAttacker`, `ObjectDB.Awake`, `ZNetScene.Awake`, `ItemDrop.ItemData.GetTooltip`, `ItemDrop.GetHoverText`, `ItemStand`, `InventoryGui.UpdateTrophyList`
- **Risks:** PvP farming/griefing (add cooldowns). The attacker must be loaded for GetAttacker. Only works when the victim has the mod. Needs a model and icon.
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/building-crafting.md](game/building-crafting.md)
- **Existing mods:** partial

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [Player Heads (KG)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/KGvalheim/Player_Heads/) | 1.0.0, 2023-10-10, deprecated | A player head drops on PvP death. The only prior art. |
  | [UsefulTrophies (probablykory), related](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/probablykory/UsefulTrophies/) | not checked | Consume trophies for skill XP or boss powers. |

- **Inspiration:** Nothing current. Plan: a player trophy from a PvP kill, or an optional memento placed in the tombstone. Store the name and look (hair and beard, colors) in ItemData.m_customData, and render the real face on ItemStand from VisEquipment data. Uses: a memorial trinket, a trophy wall, bounties.

### Smelt gold

> We have too much gold, Smelt it in bars and create things with it (weapons, armors, tools, probably on the fancy side not necessarily strong, or with an interesting set bonus)

- **Scope:** New · **Feasibility:** medium · **Who needs it:** Both · **Custom assets:** yes · **Status:** idea
- **Approach:** Treat Coins as the ore. Craft a Coin Sack (default 50 Coins) at the workbench and give it a max stack of 1, so InventoryGui.DoCrafting hands out no bonus sacks. Smelt the sack in the vanilla Smelter: append a Smelter.ItemConversion (Coin Sack to Gold Bar) to the smelter prefab's m_conversion at ZNetScene.Awake, so fuel, timing and Batch Station Feeding work unchanged. Register a Gold Bar and a small Gilded line in ObjectDB.Awake and ObjectDB.CopyOtherDB: crown, chest, legs, one weapon, a pickaxe, a trinket, and a stack or throne decor piece with comfort in the Hammer's PieceTable. Clone each one from a vanilla prefab, tint it gold, and give it a prefixed name, because vanilla 1.0 game data already uses 'Gold' for Bloodgold. Stats sit around the Bronze/Iron tier. The reason to wear the set is its bonus: a data-only SE_Stats set through m_setName, m_setSize and m_setStatusEffect. The trinket and the weapon can count toward the set (Humanoid.GetSetCount counts every slot), but the armour pieces must carry the effect, because the trinket never grants it. Optional coded perks could include better sell prices through StoreGui.SellItem (client-side). Keep m_value at 0 so nothing gold sells back to a trader, and make each upgrade level cost more bars, so the sink grows with the player's hoard. Register the prefabs once at load and keep them registered even while the feature is off. All numbers go in a server-synced config. The code is small and mostly data. Feasibility is medium rather than easy because this is the first MC mod that registers new prefabs, and every item needs a gold look and an icon.
- **Hooks:** `ObjectDB.Awake`, `ObjectDB.CopyOtherDB`, `ZNetScene.Awake`, `PieceTable.m_pieces`, `Smelter.m_conversion`, `Smelter.OnAddOre`, `Smelter.RPC_AddOre`, `InventoryGui.DoCrafting`, `Player.UpdateKnownRecipesList`, `Inventory.GetValuableItems`, `StoreGui.GetSellableItem`, `StoreGui.SellItem`, `Humanoid.UpdateEquipmentStatusEffects`, `Humanoid.HaveSetEffect`, `Humanoid.GetSetCount`, `VisEquipment.SetChestEquipped`, `ItemDrop.ItemData.GetTooltip`
- **Risks:** Every peer needs the mod, including the dedicated server. A peer without it loses gold items: Inventory.AddItem skips unknown prefabs when an inventory loads, and the server destroys world ZDOs it cannot instantiate (ZNetScene.CreateObjectsSorted). It also sees gilded armour on other players as missing (VisEquipment.SetChestEquipped logs 'Missing chest item'). As a smelter owner, it silently drops a Coin Sack that a modded client has already removed from its inventory, because Smelter.RPC_AddOre re-checks IsItemAllowed against the owner's own list. A sack that is already queued when a vanilla peer takes ownership is consumed with no output. The MC framework's Both handshake only checks the server, so a vanilla client can still join a modded server; blocking or warning those players is extra work (Jotunn's version check does it). Prefab registration must not follow the framework's live toggle: a character that loads while the prefabs are missing loses its gold items at the next save. Economy loops can duplicate coins: any m_value above 0, a melt-back recipe, or a stackable crafted intermediate (through the craft bonus). Watch for name clashes with vanilla Gold/GoldOre, Digitalroot's GoldIngot/CoinPile and Balrond's GoldBar_bal, and with display names such as vanilla's Coin Pile piece. Uninstalling the mod deletes the items. This is the first MC mod that registers prefabs, so it needs a shared helper or Jotunn. Art and balance will take most of the time.
- **Related:** Gemstone revamp; Better shops; Barber shop to customize armor; One trinket per trophy; Forge of potential revamp
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/building-crafting.md](game/building-crafting.md)
- **Existing mods:** partial

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [Digitalroots GoldBars (Digitalroot)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Digitalroot/Digitalroots_GoldBars/) | 1.2.34, 2026-09-10, 1.0 tagged | The closest match. 200 Coins craft a Coin Pile, and a Coin Pile crafts back into 200 Coins (no station for either). The Smelter turns a Coin Pile into a Gold Ingot (GoldIngot). A level-2 forge turns an ingot plus 1 Copper back into 100 Coins. Hammer pieces: Coin Pile (200 Coins, 1 comfort) and Gold Stack (48 ingots, 2 comfort), with the comfort configurable. No weapons, armour or tools. Uses Jotunn. AGPL-3.0 source on GitHub. |
  | [Ravenwood Currency (JamesJonesTV)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/JamesJonesTV/Ravenwood_Currency/) | 1.0.2, 2026-09-20, 1.0 tagged | Six metal coins minted from bars at a Currency Station, used in Ravenwood mod recipes. Its 'Vanilla Coin Exchange' is an explicit coin sink: vanilla coins buy its coins at 2x their value (5x for Copper and Mithril). Uses Jotunn. |
  | [RepairRequiresCoins (cjayride)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/cjayride/RepairRequiresCoins/) | 1.2.10, 2026-09-26, 1.0 tagged, tagged AI Generated (the author says AI only checked the 1.0 port) | A fork of aedenthorn's RepairRequiresMats. Repairs cost Coins, priced from the item's recipe materials. A recurring coin sink with no new items. |
  | [OttoPay + OttoAura (potto007)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/potto007/OttoPay/) | 1.6.4, 2026-09-26, updated post-1.0 (README: built against 1.0.16) | A coin bank balance kept by the traders. The companion OttoAura (1.4.4, 2026-09-25) spends the balance on services: gear repairs inside a ward (1 coin per item per tick by default; the ward's healing is free), moving placed pieces (5 coins per move), and a fee for selling valuables through the bank. Coins buy services, not things. |
  | [Weather Altar (EnyMan)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/EnyMan/Weather_Altar/) | 1.0.3, 2026-09-14, 1.0 tagged, AI-generated | Pay 10 Coins and 10 HP to force a weather for 10 minutes. Needs the server and every client. Uses Jotunn. Another service-style coin sink. |
  | [VanillaNPC (fedorovdgap)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/fedorovdgap/VanillaNPC/) | 1.1.0, 2026-09-16, updated post-1.0 (README: requires Valheim 1.0) | NPCs paid in coins: a fisherman who charges a small fee per fish, guards at 300 coins per banner, and a tavern selling meads for 30 to 200 coins. Everyone needs it, including the dedicated server. Uses Jotunn. Another service-style coin sink. |
  | [Terraheim (DasSauerkraut)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/DasSauerkraut/Terraheim/) | 2.3.9, 2023-09-12, deprecated | Armour overhaul with a bonus for each set. Its Hyran (Nomad) bow set is money-themed: hits sometimes drop coins, damage grows with the coins carried, and each attack spends a coin. A good model for a coin-themed set bonus. |
  | [balrond amazing nature (Balrond)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Balrond/balrond_amazing_nature/) | 1.4.0, 2026-08-21, pre-1.0 | Large overhaul where Gold is a mined progression material (gold_Rock_bal deposits, GoldOre_bal, GoldBar_bal, GoldScrap_bal, goldbar_stack_bal). It also lists a Peningar_bal item with its own treasure_pile_peningar_bal and treasure_stack_peningar_bal pieces; the README gives names only. Its prefab names must not clash with ours. |
  | [Glitnir Blood & Gold Weapon (GlitnirBr)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/GlitnirBr/Glitnir_Blood_Gold_Weapon/) | 2.2.3, 2026-04-28, deprecated | Weapons made for the Glitnir server: a gold-themed staff, sword, bow, shield and arrows. They are not made from coins. |

- **Inspiration:** Digitalroot's GoldBars proves the loop works (coins, then Coin Pile, then Smelter, then Gold Ingot), but it stops at decoration: the Coin Pile crafts back into its 200 Coins, an ingot melts back into 100 Coins at the forge, and nothing uses the bars for gear. The coin-sink mods spend coins on services or on another currency rather than on things to own: RepairRequiresCoins and OttoAura (repairs, moving pieces), Weather Altar, VanillaNPC (fisherman, guards, tavern) and Ravenwood's coin exchange. Terraheim's Hyran set shows how fun a coin-themed set bonus can be. Ours would keep the vanilla Smelter step so it feels like Valheim, with a Coin Sack as the 'ore'. It would add a small hand-made Gilded line (armour, one weapon, a tool, a trinket, decor) whose value is looks plus a set bonus, not raw stats. To stay a real sink, bars and gear cannot be sold (m_value 0), the crafted intermediate gets no craft bonus, and there is no melt-back by default. Every prefab name gets a prefix (vanilla 1.0 already uses Gold for Bloodgold). Optionally, Digitalroot's GoldIngot could be accepted when that mod is installed.

### Trophy on mobs without trophy

- **Scope:** New · **Feasibility:** medium · **Who needs it:** Both · **Custom assets:** yes · **Status:** idea
- **Approach:** At ZNetScene.Awake, scan prefabs with a CharacterDrop that has no ItemType.Trophy drop, and append a Drop for a new trophy (cloned trophy prefab with a swapped mesh/icon, free m_trophyPos, <name>_lore localization, and an 'attach' child for item stands). Use a configurable chance and exclusions (summons, tames).
- **Hooks:** `ZNetScene.Awake`, `CharacterDrop.GenerateDropList`, `CharacterDrop.Start`, `ObjectDB.Awake`, `InventoryGui.UpdateTrophyList`, `ItemStand.GetAttachPrefab`
- **Risks:** Drop-table mods (Drop That, CLLC, EpicLoot) may overwrite m_drops, so ordering matters. Pseudo-random smoothing for chances <= 0.3. Trophy panel overlap. Needs art per creature.
- **Related:** One trinket per trophy
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/building-crafting.md](game/building-crafting.md)
- **Existing mods:** none

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [Drop That (ASharpPen)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/ASharpPen/Drop_That/) | 3.1.6, 2026-09-23, 1.0 tagged | Loot-table configurator. Can add existing items as drops, not new trophies. We must coexist because it rewrites CharacterDrop. |
  | [CreatureLevelAndLootControl (Smoothbrain)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Smoothbrain/CreatureLevelAndLootControl/) | 4.6.4, 2025-05-26, pre-1.0 | Level and loot control. No new trophy items. |
  | [TrophyBuffs (blacks7ar)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/blacks7ar/TrophyBuffs/) | 1.2.9, 2025-09-12, pre-1.0 | Craftable decorative trophies, not creature drops. |

- **Inspiration:** Clear gap. The wiki lists creatures with no trophy: Greyling, Gull, Crow, Root, Fish, Leviathan, Oozer, Bat, Chicken/Hen, Seeker Brood, Ash Crow, Charred Twitcher, Skugg. Plan: clone a trophy prefab with a head mesh taken from the creature's renderer and a runtime icon. Inject a low-rate CharacterDrop entry. Register it as a trophy so Player.AddTrophy and the trophy wall work. Give each one a use (trinkets, item stands). Fish (items) and Leviathan (a location) need special handling.

### One trinket per trophy

- **Scope:** New · **Feasibility:** hard · **Who needs it:** Both · **Custom assets:** yes · **Status:** idea
- **Approach:** A data table maps each trophy to {station, mats, m_maxAdrenaline, equip SE, full-adrenaline SE}. Clone a vanilla trinket prefab, set SharedData, and generate icons by compositing the trophy icon. Register items, recipes and SEs (Jotunn). The vanilla adrenaline pop applies m_fullAdrenalineSE with no patch. Implement effects with a few parameterized SE_Stats subclasses.
- **Hooks:** `ObjectDB.Awake`, `ObjectDB.CopyOtherDB`, `ZNetScene.Awake`, `Player.AddAdrenaline`, `Humanoid.UpdateEquipmentStatusEffects`, `ItemDrop.ItemData.GetTooltip`
- **Risks:** About 70 items to balance and create icons for. Removing the mod deletes the items. SE name-hash collisions. Overlap with trophy-buff mods. Relative balance against vanilla trinkets.
- **Related:** Trophy on mobs without trophy; Gemstone revamp
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/building-crafting.md](game/building-crafting.md)
- **Existing mods:** partial

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [ClassTrinkets (JamesJonesTV)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/JamesJonesTV/ClassTrinkets/) | 1.0.2, 2026-02-12, pre-1.0 | 40 trinkets (8 classes x 5 ranks) with static stats. Rank 1 needs a class trophy. |
  | [Glorious Trinkets (Catharis)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Catharis/Glorious_Trinkets/) | 2.0.0, 2026-05-15, deprecated | New trinkets, capes and utility items. |
  | [BetterTrinkets (Schwifty)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Schwifty/BetterTrinkets/) | 1.0.0, 2026-02-28, pre-1.0 | Makes vanilla trinkets passive, and a full adrenaline bar doubles them. A rebalance, not new trinkets. |
  | [TrophyBuffs (blacks7ar)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/blacks7ar/TrophyBuffs/) | 1.2.9, 2025-09-12, pre-1.0 | 72 craftable glowing trophies that can be eaten for buffs. |

- **Inspiration:** Gap: map each vanilla trophy to a themed trinket that uses the 1.0 adrenaline system (ItemType.Trinket = 24, SharedData.m_maxAdrenaline, m_fullAdrenalineSE). Data-driven table of trophy -> trinket (tier metal, status effect). Clone a vanilla trinket prefab, render the trophy mesh as a pendant, generate the icon at runtime. The recipe unlocks when the trophy is known. Balance against vanilla trinkets. Feeds from the new-trophies idea and the player trophy.

### Enchanting

- **Scope:** New · **Feasibility:** very-hard · **Who needs it:** Both · **Custom assets:** yes · **Status:** idea
- **Approach:** Store versioned affixes in m_customData under a namespaced key. Enchant at a new station consuming gems, essences and eitr. Apply stats via cached postfixes on the ItemData getters and SEs via UpdateEquipmentStatusEffects. Carry customData across upgrades and refinement in DoCrafting. Keep affixes data-driven.
- **Hooks:** `ItemDrop.ItemData.m_customData`, `ItemDrop.ItemData.GetTooltip`, `ItemDrop.ItemData.GetDamage`, `ItemDrop.ItemData.GetArmor`, `ItemDrop.ItemData.GetBaseBlockPower`, `ItemDrop.ItemData.GetMaxDurability`, `Humanoid.UpdateEquipmentStatusEffects`, `Character.Damage`, `InventoryGui.DoCrafting`
- **Risks:** Direct overlap with EpicLoot (tooltips, customData, DoCrafting); detect it and disable or bridge. Hot-path performance. Vanilla upgrades wipe customData. Inventories are client-authoritative, so cheating can't be prevented.
- **Related:** Gemstone revamp; Forge of potential revamp; Magic applied to non combat (craft, farming, cooking, sailing, etc,)
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/building-crafting.md](game/building-crafting.md)
- **Existing mods:** full

  | Mod | Status | Notes |

  | [EpicLoot (RandyKnapp / OrianaVenture)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/RandyKnapp/EpicLoot/) | 0.14.13, 2026-09-24, 1.0 tagged | Flagship: rarity loot, enchanting, augmenting, sets, and an Adventure mode (a coin sink). Data-driven JSON. Open source. Incompatible with Jewelcrafting. |
  | [Valheim Enchantment System (KG)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/KGvalheim/Valheim_Enchantment_System/) | 1.7.4, 2025-03-08, deprecated | MMO-style +N scrolls with failure chances. |
  | [Jewelcrafting (Smoothbrain)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Smoothbrain/Jewelcrafting/) | 2.0.1, 2026-05-06, deprecated | Sockets and tiered gems with break chances. Open source. |

- **Inspiration:** Saturated and EpicLoot-dominated. Ours should be small and hand-authored, grounded in 1.0 systems: infuse through Forge of Potential upgrades or gems, and tie into adrenaline trinkets and the Ashlands gemstone variants. Store data in ItemDrop.ItemData.m_customData (vanilla, saved and networked). Decide early whether we stay EpicLoot-compatible.

## UX

### Loot filter

- **Scope:** QoL · **Feasibility:** easy · **Who needs it:** Client · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** [Loot Pickup Filter 0.1.0](../src/UX/AutoPickup.Filter) (in development)
- **Approach:** A small transpiler on Player.AutoPickup replaces the m_autoPickup field check with LootFilter.Allow(drop), which returns drop.m_autoPickup && !blocked(prefabName/itemType). Items are toggled with Alt+right-click in the inventory and marked with a tinted overlay. The list is stored per character in Player.m_customData['vm.lootfilter'] with a BepInEx default list. An optional three-state V toggle cycles Off / All / Filtered.
- **Hooks:** `Player.AutoPickup (transpile ldfld ItemDrop::m_autoPickup)`, `ItemDrop.m_autoPickup`, `ItemDrop.m_itemData.m_dropPrefab`, `InventoryGui.OnRightClickItem`, `InventoryGrid.UpdateGui`, `InventoryElement.m_noteleport (overlay template)`, `Player.m_customData`, `Humanoid.Pickup (left untouched for manual pickup)`
- **Risks:** Transpiler collisions with other AutoPickup patchers (pickup-range and pickup-selector mods, ValheimPlus remnants). Never overwrite m_autoPickup, because it also encodes 'dropped by me'. Token name collisions between modded items, so key on the prefab name. No save or network risk.
- **Related:** Sort bags; Search chest
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/ux.md](game/ux.md)
- **Existing mods:** full (sheet link: https://www.nexusmods.com/valheim/mods/116)

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [Loki's Autoloot Trash Filtering (LCDR, Nexus 116)](https://www.nexusmods.com/valheim/mods/116) | Thunderstore reupload v1.0.1, 2022-12-18 (1.0: unknown) | Block all, block trash list, or allow all; trash list in config. |
  | [AutoPickupIgnorer (PipMods)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/PipMods/AutoPickupIgnorer/) | v1.1.0, updated 2026-09-09 (1.0: likely) | Uncomment item names in the config; a hotkey cycles 3 modes. Open source. |
  | [LootFilter (wonkotron)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/wonkotron/LootFilter/) | v1.0.2, 2026-02-17 (1.0: unknown) | Comma-separated prefab blacklist through Configuration Manager. Open source. |
  | [InventoryActions (sighsorry)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/sighsorry/InventoryActions/) | v1.1.8, 2026-09-26 (1.0: likely) | Per-item auto-pickup control from an inventory hover button, controller support, plus sort and favourites. |

- **Inspiration:** Most mods use text lists of prefab names, which is poor UX. The best idea found is the deprecated RagnarsRokare AutoPickupSelector (clickable per-item toggles in the Trophies tab). Vanilla: Player.AutoPickup (overlap sphere, m_autoPickupRange, global toggle). Ours: modifier+click in the inventory to mark 'never pick up', category rules, and one shared item-marking model with sort, trash and favourites.

### Search crafting station

- **Scope:** QoL · **Feasibility:** easy · **Who needs it:** Client · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** [Crafting Search and Sort 0.1.0](../src/UX/Crafting.SearchSort) (in development)
- **Approach:** Add a search field above m_recipeListRoot. MVP: write the terms into Player.s_FilterCraft and call UpdateCraftingPanel(false); Clear() it when the field is empty, because a [''] filter hides everything. Better: a GetAvailableRecipes postfix with AND matching, ingredient names and a 'craftable only' toggle, active only during our refresh. Add a sort dropdown bound to the vanilla 'sortcraft' key and clear the filter on Hide.
- **Hooks:** `Player.s_FilterCraft (vanilla static filter)`, `Player.GetAvailableRecipes (postfix)`, `InventoryGui.UpdateCraftingPanel (private, refresh)`, `InventoryGui.UpdateRecipeList (private)`, `InventoryGui.SortMethod / 'sortcraft' unique key`, `InventoryGui.Awake/Hide`, `Chat.HasFocus`, `Recipe.m_resources`
- **Risks:** s_FilterCraft is static and shared with the console, so a stale filter survives. GetAvailableRecipes is also called by other mods (recipe browsers, EpicLoot UIs), so the filter must be scoped with a flag. Crafting-panel reskins and craft-from-containers mods may relayout the panel.
- **Related:** Search chest
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/ux.md](game/ux.md)
- **Existing mods:** full

  | Mod | Status | Notes |

  | [CraftSearch (XineladaLendaria)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/XineladaLendaria/CraftSearch/) | v2.0.10, 2026-09-27 (1.0: yes) | Recipe search at all stations, category filters, @mod search, quantity crafting. |
  | [AAA Crafting (Azumatt)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Azumatt/AAA_Crafting/) | v2.1.8, updated 2026-09-14, but flagged deprecated | Amount field, search, '!' prefix to search by ingredient, recipe tracking, controller virtual keyboard. |
  | [CraftingFilter (cjayride fork)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/cjayride/CraftingFilter/) | v1.2.1, 2026-09-09, 'Updated for Valheim 1.0' (1.0: yes) | Category filter menu on the crafting tab. Fork of aedenthorn's mod. |
  | [Sorted Menus (Goldenrevolver)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Goldenrevolver/Sorted_Menus_Cooking_Crafting_and_Skills_Menu/) | v1.3.4, 2024-10-04 (1.0: unknown) | Sorts the crafting, cooking and skills lists. Open source. |

- **Inspiration:** Vanilla 1.0 gave the build menu a search field (BuildUi.UpdateSearch) but not the InventoryGui crafting list, so a clone of the native build search is the most natural option. Borrow ingredient search ('!'), a 'craftable now' toggle and category chips. Share one search component across crafting, containers and build.

### Sort bags

- **Scope:** QoL · **Feasibility:** easy · **Who needs it:** Client · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** cancelled
- **Approach:** 'Bags' is defined as the player inventory plus carts and ship storage, since 1.0 has no bag items. Player sort touches only rows 1..H-1 and optionally keeps equipped items fixed, filling materials bottom-up like vanilla TopFirst. It reuses the shared merge and sort routine from Sort chest, with a button and a hotkey gated on InventoryGui.IsVisible.
- **Hooks:** `InventoryGui.Awake (button on m_player)`, `Player.GetInventory`, `Inventory.GetHeight (4..9 via Player.SetInventorySize / 'invrows')`, `Inventory.GetHotbar`, `Player.IsItemEquiped`, `Inventory.Changed`, `Vagon.m_container`, `Inventory.GetAllItemsInGridOrder (radial order)`
- **Risks:** The gamepad radial lists items in grid order, so sorting reshuffles it. Mods that park equipment in extra inventory rows need those rows excluded (rows beyond 'invrows'). Same custom-data merge caveat as Sort chest.
- **Related:** Sort chest; Loot filter
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/ux.md](game/ux.md)

  | [Stackmaster (JStack424)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/JStack424/Stackmaster/) | v1.2.0, 2026-09-24 (1.0: likely) | 'Backpack' means the player inventory: auto-sort on open, fixed hotbar, protected restock targets. Open source. |
  | [InventoryActions (sighsorry)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/sighsorry/InventoryActions/) | v1.1.8, 2026-09-26 (1.0: likely) | Sorts inventories and containers, favourites, controller support. |
  | [AdventureBackpacks (Vapok)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Vapok/AdventureBackpacks/) | v2.2.1, 2026-09-28 (1.0: likely) | Main backpack mod. No sort documented; a compatibility target if 'bags' means backpacks. |

- **Inspiration:** Open question for the user: does 'bags' mean the player inventory (fully covered) or backpack items (gap: sorting inside AdventureBackpacks and similar mods, which needs soft integration)? Otherwise this is the same module as Sort chest applied to the player grid, with the hotbar locked and favourites respected.

### Sort chest

- **Scope:** QoL · **Feasibility:** easy · **Who needs it:** Client · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** [Sort Chest 0.1.0](../src/UX/Container.Sort) (in development)
- **Approach:** A Sort button is cloned from Stack All. On click, only when the local client is owner and no drag is active, merge partial stacks of IsSameType items with identical m_customData (OR the m_cheated flags). Order by type group, localized name, quality and stack, reassign m_gridPos row-major, and call Inventory.Changed once so Container.Save writes ZDO s_items. Auto-sort on close and locked slots are optional.
- **Hooks:** `InventoryGui.Awake (clone m_stackAllButton)`, `InventoryGui.m_currentContainer`, `Container.IsOwner`, `Inventory.GetAllItems`, `Inventory.Changed (private, reverse patch)`, `InventoryGui.SetupDragItem`, `InventoryGui.CloseContainer (auto-sort on close)`, `Container.OnContainerChanged/Save`
- **Risks:** IsSameType ignores m_customData (EpicLoot and similar mods), so the merge must compare custom data explicitly. Do not rebuild with AddItem, which works per unit and fires Changed per call. UI reskins and other sort mods (Quick Stack-Store-Sort-Trash, AzuAutoStore) occupy the same button area.
- **Related:** Sort bags; Search chest
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/ux.md](game/ux.md)
- **Existing mods:** full

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [Quick Stack Store Sort Trash Restock (Goldenrevolver)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Goldenrevolver/Quick_Stack_Store_Sort_Trash_Restock/) | v1.4.15, 2026-09-12, rebuilt for 1.0 (1.0: yes) | Sort by category, name, weight or value; favourites; quick stack, store, trash, restock; multiplayer-safe. About 940K downloads. MIT source. |
  | [QuickStackPlus (Goneryx)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Goneryx/QuickStackPlus/) | v1.2.1, 2026-09-24 (1.0: yes) | Per-container Smart Storage filters stored on the container; sorting only on demand; blacklist and junk. |
  | [RunicStorage (Chazman)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Chazman/RunicStorage/) | v1.3.12, 2026-09-28 (1.0: likely) | Remembers chest contents, routing rules, labels, previews, search and sort. |
  | [HexQuickStackStorage (Hex_Viking)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Hex_Viking/HexQuickStackStorage/) | v1.4.3, 2026-09-26 (1.0: yes) | Sort button on the container and an S button for the inventory, plus quick stack and delete. |

- **Inspiration:** Saturated. Vanilla InventoryGui only offers Take All and Stack All. Only worth building as part of a unified storage-UX module (sort, search, filter and loot rules on one category and favourites model). Borrow QSSSTR's favouriting and multiplayer safety, respect ZDO ownership, and avoid auto-sorting on open.

### notifications stay longer and show multiple

- **Scope:** QoL · **Feasibility:** medium · **Who needs it:** Client · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** idea
- **Approach:** Replace vanilla's single top-left line (one message per second, 4 s fade, merges only consecutive duplicates) with a pooled stack of N rows cloned from m_messageText and m_messageIcon, each with a configurable lifetime. Rows with the same key aggregate as xN and refresh their timer. Center messages get a small queue with a minimum display time instead of being overwritten. Unlock toasts get more slots and a slower animator. Pickups can optionally be logged into the 50-entry history.
- **Hooks:** `MessageHud.ShowMessage (prefix reroute TopLeft/Center)`, `MessageHud.UpdateMessage (keep for cross-fade buffer)`, `MessageHud.Awake (m_maxUnlockMessages)`, `MessageHud.UpdateUnlockMsg (Animator.speed on new toasts)`, `MessageHud.HideAll`, `MessageHud.m_messageText / m_messageIcon (clone templates)`, `Character.ShowPickupMessage`, `Player.Message`, `Hud.IsUserHidden`
- **Risks:** Other mods that prefix ShowMessage or read MessageHud internals. Skipping UpdateMessage entirely would break the center fade, so only reroute inside ShowMessage. More unlock slots at 110 px spacing can run off-screen at high UI scale. The feed must honour user-hidden HUD and showDespiteHiddenHUD.
- **Related:** Ping system revamp
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/ux.md](game/ux.md)
- **Existing mods:** partial

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [NotificationsOverhaul (blacks7ar)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/blacks7ar/NotificationsOverhaul/) | v1.0.3, 2026-02-25 (1.0: unknown) | Configurable duration, max visible, position, font and colour; pickup-only or global. About 12K downloads. |
  | [BetterPickupNotifications (Pfhoenix)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Pfhoenix/BetterPickupNotifications/) | v1.3.3, 2025-11-15 (1.0: unknown) | Reworked pickup message handling. |
  | [StackingNotifications (paddywan)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/paddywan/StackingNotifications/) | v1.0.3, 2021-09 (dead) | Timed stacked notification layer. Open source. |
  | [NotificationTweaks (digitiliad)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/digitiliad/NotificationTweaks/) | v0.3.2, 2021-04, deprecated (dead) | Count shown at once, fade, merging of similar messages. |

- **Inspiration:** Vanilla MessageHud shows top-left messages one at a time from a queue on a short timer, merging identical consecutive messages (MessageHud.UpdateMessage). Unlock messages have their own queue, and there is a 50-entry m_messageLog. Ours: a stacked N-line feed, per-item merged counts ('Wood x37'), longer display for important messages than for pickups, and an optional history panel from the existing log. Small and low-risk, and nothing maintained is confirmed on 1.0.

### Search chest

- **Scope:** QoL · **Feasibility:** medium · **Who needs it:** Client · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** idea
- **Approach:** Clone the 1.0 build-menu search field into the container and player panel headers and normalize the term like BuildUi.UpdateSearch. An InventoryGrid.UpdateGui postfix greys out non-matching slots each frame; nothing is written, so there is no network impact. It matches cached localized name, token, prefab and type, and supports t:/@ prefixes. v2 scans cached nearby Container instances (read-only, respecting ward and privacy) and highlights chests that contain matches.
- **Hooks:** `InventoryGui.Awake`, `BuildUi.m_searchField (private, clone template)`, `InventoryGrid.UpdateGui (postfix dim via InventoryElement.m_icon)`, `InventoryGrid.m_elements (private)`, `InventoryGui.Show/Hide/CloseContainer`, `Chat.HasFocus (postfix input block)`, `Localization.Localize`, `Container.CheckForChanges (read-only nearby search)`, `WearNTear.Highlight`, `PrivateArea.CheckAccess`
- **Risks:** Typed keys leak into game hotkeys: Tab, E and Esc close the inventory, and console binds fire. The Chat.HasFocus postfix plus a guard on binds fixes this; gamepad needs testing. Per-frame cost requires a cached localized-name dictionary. Other mods reuse the same template or relayout the panel. Nearby search must not leak warded or private chest contents.
- **Related:** Search crafting station; Sort chest
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/ux.md](game/ux.md)
- **Existing mods:** full

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [SonicChestFilters (SonicDM)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/SonicDM/SonicChestFilters/) | v1.0.1, 2026-09-11 (1.0: yes) | Jötunn text box on the open chest that hides non-matching slots; a locate command makes nearby chests glow. Tagged AI Generated. Open source. |
  | [TidyChests (Muindor)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Muindor/TidyChests/) | v1.3.1, 2026-09-24 (1.0: likely) | Ctrl+O searches everything in nearby chests; a hotkey finds where an item is stored. Open source. |
  | [RunicStorage (Chazman)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Chazman/RunicStorage/) | v1.3.12, 2026-09-28 (1.0: likely) | Remembers contents so it can search unloaded chests; highlights matches. |
  | [CraftSearch (XineladaLendaria)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/XineladaLendaria/CraftSearch/) | v2.0.10, 2026-09-27 (1.0: yes) | Search in the open chest moves matches to the front of the grid, plus recipe search. |

- **Inspiration:** The user assumed nothing existed, but a wave of mods appeared after 1.0. Two features: filter the open grid (dim or highlight rather than hide, to keep spatial memory) and find across nearby containers (only loaded zones have live inventories, so searching further needs remembered contents). Vanilla 1.0 now has a native search field in the build menu (BuildUi GuiInputField, BuildUi.UpdateSearch with localized terms). Copy that look and controller behaviour so ours feels built-in.

### Ping system revamp

- **Scope:** QoL · **Feasibility:** hard · **Who needs it:** Depends · **Custom assets:** no · **Status:** idea
- **Approach:** Add an in-world ping key that raycasts from GameCamera (about 250 m) and classifies the hit: enemy, loot, resource, structure or 'go here'; double-tap marks danger. It sends a rate-limited routed RPC 'VM_Ping' (ZPackage with type, true position, optional ZDOID and label) to everyone, plus the vanilla Chat.SendPing for unmodded peers. Modded receivers suppress the duplicate vanilla ping in an AddInworldText prefix. Receivers render pooled world markers cloned from Chat.m_worldTextBase with ObjectDB or pin icons and distance, an edge arrow, typed minimap pins via AddPin(save:false), several pings per player and a configurable TTL. Gamepad users get a radial ping wheel.
- **Hooks:** `Chat.SendPing`, `Chat.AddInworldText (private)`, `Chat.UpdateWorldTexts (private)`, `Chat.m_worldTextTTL / m_worldTextBase`, `Minimap.UpdatePingPins (private)`, `Minimap.AddPin / RemovePin`, `Minimap.OnMapMiddleClick / OnMapDblClick`, `ZRoutedRpc.Register / InvokeRoutedRPC(ZRoutedRpc.Everybody)`, `Player.FindHoverObject (reference raycast)`, `Hoverable.GetHoverName`, `Hud.m_radialMenu (IRadialConfig ping wheel)`, `Game.m_noMap / Minimap.m_nomapPingDistance`, `RelationsManager.CheckPermissionAsync`
- **Risks:** Spam and griefing, so rate-limit both sending and receiving. A custom RPC bypasses platform block-list checks unless RelationsManager is reused. Vanilla hides pings beyond 50 m in no-map worlds, so the mod should respect that for fairness. Key conflicts (middle mouse is secondary attack and remove) and a lack of free gamepad buttons. World-space UI cost, so pool the markers. Conflicts with other waypoint or ping mods patching the same Chat or Minimap methods. Only clients with the mod see rich pings; the server needs nothing.
- **Related:** notifications stay longer and show multiple; Cartography table revamp
- **Game systems:** [docs/game/ux.md](game/ux.md)
- **Existing mods:** partial

  | Mod | Status | Notes |
  |---|---|---|
  | [QuickPing, TeamVibes revival (Nexus 2033)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/TeamVibes/QuickPing/) | v1.0.5, 2026-09-27 (1.0: yes); original Atopy version deprecated 2024 | Ping what you look at (name in the world and on the minimap), auto-pin resources and locations, pin editor, tracer beam. |
  | [PingTweaks (Riintouge)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Riintouge/PingTweaks/) | v1.0.7, updated 2026-09-11, tagged Deep North (1.0: yes) | Private pings by default, persistent pings, cancel your own ping, distance and elevation, duration and colour, pin others' pings. LGPL source. |
  | [ImRightHere (Vidguy)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/Vidguy/ImRightHere/) | v1.0.2, 2026-03-17 (1.0: unknown) | Ping your own position with a hotkey; live distance; pin others' pings. |
  | [Hotkey To Ping (shineblind)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/shineblind/Hotkey_To_Ping/) | date not checked | Aim and press T to send a vanilla ping with a point emote. |

- **Inspiration:** Vanilla pings only come from the map (Minimap calls Chat.SendPing, sent through the ChatMessage routed RPC as Talker.Type.Ping and drawn by Minimap.UpdatePingPins). Keep the vanilla RPC so players without the mod still see a basic ping. Borrow QuickPing's look-at ping and PingTweaks' distance, cancel and private options. New: typed contextual pings (enemy, loot, go here, danger) or a small wheel, a shared in-world marker with distance, and a 'target' link for our summons.

## Infrastructure proposed by the research

Shared building blocks the research agents recommended while assessing the ideas.

### [Infra] VMods.Core content registration helpers (prefab clone + register)

- **Feasibility:** medium · **Who needs it:** Both
- **Approach:** A shared library API (RegisterItem/Piece/Recipe/StatusEffect/Prefab) that clones vanilla prefabs under an inactive DontDestroyOnLoad root and validates names (no spaces or parentheses, unique hash). It de-duplicates SharedData on clones and injects content idempotently into ZNetScene (Awake prefix) and both ObjectDBs (Awake/CopyOtherDB postfix + UpdateRegisters). This is essentially a minimal in-house Jotunn; alternatively adopt Jotunn itself.
- **Risks:** Duplicate names throw inside vanilla Awake and can break world load. Items unregistered at Player.Load are permanently deleted. Prefab edits persist across sessions (idempotency). The SharedData sharing semantics need a runtime check.

### [Infra] Mod version handshake (mod parity check)

- **Feasibility:** easy · **Who needs it:** Both
- **Approach:** Register a peer RPC in an OnNewConnection postfix. The client sends {guid, version, required} for every VMods plugin. The server validates in an RPC_PeerInfo prefix and rejects with Error(3) plus a readable reason sent first via RemotePrint or a custom RPC. The client mirrors the check against the server's list. Per-mod policy: required, optional, or client-only.
- **Risks:** Must not block vanilla-compatible client-only mods. Message ordering relies on the reliable channel. Crossplay console players are excluded from required-mod servers.

### [Infra] Server-authoritative config sync

- **Feasibility:** medium · **Who needs it:** Both
- **Approach:** Mark config entries as server-synced. On peer ready, the server pushes a compressed ZPackage of values; on change, it re-broadcasts. Clients override their local values while connected and restore them on disconnect. Could wrap the ServerSync library instead of writing our own.
- **Risks:** Payload size on PlayFab relay. Admin-only edits need a socket-identity check (routed-RPC sender is spoofable). Values must be applied before owners use them.

### [Infra] Localization loader

- **Feasibility:** easy · **Who needs it:** Client
- **Approach:** Each mod ships translations/<Language>.json (or yaml) embedded. The core adds words in a SetupLanguage postfix with English fallback, and also immediately if the Localization instance already exists. It enforces the $vm_<mod>_ token prefix.
- **Risks:** The LRU cache keeps [token] placeholders if a token is used before registration. Tokens must be sent over the network instead of localized text.

### [Infra] Console command framework + live tuning knobs

- **Feasibility:** easy · **Who needs it:** Depends
- **Approach:** Register a 'vm <mod> <sub>' command tree in an InitTerminal postfix, with isCheat:false for diagnostics (status, dump ZDO keys, list registered content) and remoteCommand for admin server actions. A dev-build helper reads Terminal.TryTest* for live tuning.
- **Risks:** isCheat:true permanently flags characters as cheated. Cheat commands only run on the host or server.

### [Infra] Input helper with vanilla-consistent gating

- **Feasibility:** easy · **Who needs it:** Client
- **Approach:** VmInput.CanTakeInput() mirrors Player.TakeInput's UI checks. KeyboardShortcut config entries are evaluated via ZInput.GetKey/GetKeyDown, with optional gamepad chords via ZInput Joy* buttons.
- **Risks:** Real rebindable actions in the settings UI would need reflection into ZInput.m_buttons and are deferred to UX.

### [Infra] Network/persistence helpers (keys, owner RPC, session hashes)

- **Feasibility:** easy · **Who needs it:** Depends
- **Approach:** A typed key registry (static readonly int hashes with a vm.<mod>. prefix), per-session re-registration of session-only keys (server included), a RequestOwner/Apply-on-owner RPC pattern helper modeled on Container, and typed m_customData accessors (bool, int, float, compact json) with namespaced keys.
- **Risks:** Writing ZDOs as non-owner gets overwritten. m_customData is lost when stackable items merge. Keep payloads small (whole-ZDO resend).

### [Infra] Custom SoftRef asset injection (locations / dungeon rooms)

- **Feasibility:** hard · **Who needs it:** Both
- **Approach:** Generate an AssetID for mod prefabs and register an AssetLoader entry in AssetBundleLoader.Instance via reflection (Jotunn-style), then add ZoneLocation entries through a LocationList instance or a SetupLocations postfix. Only needed when Exploration features add locations or rooms.
- **Risks:** Relies on internal loader structures that can change per patch. Location placement is generated server-side in ghost zones, so the server must have the mod. World-gen changes are permanent in saves.

### [Infra] Runtime self-tests & topology test scripts

- **Feasibility:** medium · **Who needs it:** Depends
- **Approach:** A 'vm selftest' command asserts engine assumptions after spawn: SharedData reference sharing, registration round-trip, customData survival through drop/pickup/container, session hashes present. Scripts launch the local dedicated server (-nographics -batchmode) and two clients for single-player, host, and dedicated test matrices.
- **Risks:** The local dedicated server is 1.0.15 vs client 1.0.16 (both network 40). Keep them in sync for release validation.

### [Infra] Game.isModded policy

- **Feasibility:** trivial · **Who needs it:** Client
- **Approach:** The core plugin sets Game.isModded = true in Awake, as IronGate requests, and documents the consequence in user-facing READMEs.
- **Risks:** It disables achievements and achievement stats for users. This is a product decision the user must confirm.

| Mod | Why |
|---|---|
| [PlantEasily / PlantEverything / Armoire (Advize)](https://github.com/AdvizeGH/Advize_ValheimMods) | Mature, polished, 1.0-tagged within weeks. Placement-ghost grids, validity highlighting, gamepad, localization. GPL, so study only. |
| [ServersideQoL suite (ArgusMagnus)](https://github.com/ArgusMagnus/ValheimServersideQoL) | Server-only QoL that works with vanilla and console clients, done through ZDO manipulation. Its modules cover container signs, toggleable or infinite fires, tames through portals and auto-feeding. The reference for what is possible server-side in the crossplay 1.0 world. Very active. |
| [Jewelcrafting + blaxxun libraries (ItemManager/PieceManager/ServerSync)](https://github.com/blaxxun-boop/Jewelcrafting) | Socket and gem design, and the most widely embedded helper libraries. Also a cautionary tale: embedded old ServerSync broke on 1.0 (ZRoutedRpc.Everybody became a const) and the mod ended up deprecated. Compile against current game assemblies and do not vendor stale binaries. |
| [Smoothbrain / blaxxun-boop mods (PassivePowers, SmartSkills)](https://github.com/blaxxun-boop/PassivePowers) | Small, focused, server-syncable mods from the author of the widely used ServerSync and manager helper libraries. A model for the 'one feature per mod, config synced' style. |
| [ComfyMods (BruceOfTheBow)](https://github.com/BruceOfTheBow/BruceComfyMods) | Small, well-structured single-feature mods (ComfyAddAllFuel with one unified interaction patch, ColorfulLights, Pinnacle). A model for our QoL mod granularity. |
| [Quick Stack Store Sort Trash Restock (Goldenrevolver)](https://github.com/Goldenrevolver/QuickStackStore) | MIT, rebuilt for 1.0 on 2026-09-12, about 940K downloads. Mature reference for inventory and container manipulation, favourites and multiplayer-safe container access. |
| [Goldenrevolver mini-mods / QuickStackStore](https://github.com/Goldenrevolver/ValheimMiniMods) | Tiny, idiomatic Harmony patches, such as restyling an existing shield by using it on a station. Good minimal-surface examples. |
| [BetterDiving (humansandbag / MainStreetGaming)](https://github.com/humansandbag/Valheim-Better-Diving-Mod) | Apache-2.0 diving and oxygen implementation, 1.0 tagged. Reference for the swim-dive and underwater biome ideas. |
| [valheim-dps (JereKuusela)](https://github.com/JereKuusela/valheim-dps) | Unlicense, updated for 1.0 (2026-09-10). Dummy spawning plus DPS and stamina meters: a practical tool for balancing our combat revamps. |
| [Expand World Data (JereKuusela)](https://github.com/JereKuusela/valheim-expand_world_data) | YAML-driven world generation: biomes, locations, dungeon generators and rooms, and (since 1.73) events. Updated for 1.0. The reference for the underwater biome, Deep North dormancy and custom dungeons. Jere's Dungeon Splitter and Upgrade World are also worth reading. |
| [Cheb's Necromancy (jpw1991)](https://github.com/jpw1991/chebs-necromancy) | Unlicense, pushed 2026-09-28. Best open-source example of minion AI, command keys, worker behaviours and custom pieces with Jötunn. Relevant to summons and rituals. |
| [AllTameable / CreatureGenetics (Meldurson)](https://github.com/meldurson/AllTameable) | Breeding internals: level inheritance through ZDO data, egg-level passthrough, mutations, DNA traits. Directly relevant to the breeding revamp and the tame suite. |
| [MidnightMods (StarLevelSystem, ImpactfulSkills, ValheimFortress)](https://github.com/MidnightsFX/Valheim_Star_Levels_Expanded) | Creature-modifier system with a public API (mob variants, boss trials), level-gated skill unlocks, and arena wave challenges with rewards. All maintained for 1.0. |
| [Valheim Community Patch (MidnightsFX)](https://github.com/MidnightsFX/Valheim-Community-Patch) | Written for 1.0 and Unity 6 (pushed 2026-09-27). Shows how the 1.0 internals (AI, ZDO) are patched today. GPL-3.0: study only, do not copy code. |
| [Better Cartography Table (nbusseneau)](https://github.com/nbusseneau/BetterCartographyTable) | MIT, clean and well documented. Per-pin private/public/guild sharing stored on the table, mirrored into vanilla shared pins so unmodded clients still work. Not updated for 1.0 yet, so the cartography niche is open. |
| [EpicLoot (OrianaVenture fork)](https://github.com/OrianaVenture/Randy_Vapok_ValheimMods) | The largest open-source item-effect and enchanting system. Data-driven JSON, custom item data, crafting-panel UI injection, multiplayer sync. |
| [Almanac (RustyMods)](https://github.com/RustyMods/Almanac) | Large YAML-driven framework for achievements, bounties, quests, NPC dialogue, index UI and metrics; 354 dependent mods. Shows what players expect from Compendium and quest features, and the feature-bloat trap. |
| [ProtectiveWards / TradersExtended (shudnal)](https://github.com/shudnal/ProtectiveWards) | Clean, maintained, 1.0-tagged mods with server-synced JSON/YAML data lists and in-game editors. Patterns for wards, trader gating (feast unlock) and data-driven config. |
| [shudnal suite (HipLantern, ExtraSlots, TradersExtended, Seasons, LongshipUpgrades)](https://github.com/shudnal/Seasons) | Prolific and updated for 1.0 within days, all open source. Patterns for custom equipment slots, environment/weather control, trader UI and ConditionalConfigSync server sync. |
| [PingTweaks / Valheim-Plugins (Terrenteller)](https://github.com/Terrenteller/Valheim-Plugins) | LGPL-3.0. Minimal, clean patches of the vanilla ping pipeline (Chat and Minimap), maintained across every update up to Deep North. |
| [Jötunn (Valheim-Modding)](https://github.com/Valheim-Modding/Jotunn) | MIT library, v2.30.2 released 2026-09-21 for 1.0. The standard toolkit for items, pieces, GUI and input, used by several mods in this survey. Read it even if we decide not to depend on it. |
| [ASharpPen (Spawn That, Custom Raids, Drop That)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/ASharpPen/Spawn_That/) | Robust config-driven spawners and raids with global-key conditions, updated for 1.0. The foundation for Deep North dormant/active phases and regional mob variants. |
| [Goo's Combat Overhaul (gnls)](https://thunderstore.io/c/valheim/p/gnls/GoosCombatOverhaul/) | Closed source, but the most complete combat design reference for 1.0.16: per-weapon YAML profiles, hyperarmor, running and jump attacks, tower shields. Also the main mod our combat revamps must coexist with or detect. |
